Their historical pending/not-qualified labels are not the current caller or E5 status; the current REPORT.md and pinned status registry record the completed caller, scoped controls and independent E5 consumption. No historical receipt or theorem meaning is rewritten.

The concrete RF/TF finding is **CLOSED_WITH_NORMAL_ENTRY_BOUND** under separately versioned E5. All qualified scalar successors preserve RF=0 and TF=0; the RF-clearing relation is identical on this domain. Both OpenSSL public interfaces and the actual composed caller were freshly requalified through their unchanged posts. **RF=1/debug/resume entries and fault-priority correspondence are EXCLUDED_SCOPE.** E5 establishes this specific model correspondence; unqualified full-processor correspondence is NOT_ESTABLISHED. The physical interpretation also requires the explicitly enumerated legal state, disabled debug facilities and absent delivered events; these are architectural assumptions, not hidden theorem premises. ## Provider discovery

In the separately isolated discovery process, the default and base provider dispatch callbacks mapped to the frozen libcrypto.so.3; the legacy dispatch callback mapped to the frozen legacy.so module. This inspects the selected build rather than assuming all operations belong to one artifact. These are runtime binding observations, not provider correctness/selection proofs or dependencies of the scoped memory routines. Evidence: [results/providers/inventory.json](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/results/providers/inventory.json) and [results/providers/provider-dispatch-bindings.tsv](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/results/providers/provider-dispatch-bindings.tsv).

## Current literal formal interface and semantics

The following are literal frozen sources for the checked E2 package. The public entry is the unchanged production symbol at load bias 0x10000000 + ELF VA 0x241840 = 0x10241840. Inputs remain universal over all admissible uint64 lengths, contents, alignments and overlaps; there is no nonzero length cap or buffer mutual-separation requirement. The incoming slot/code separation, DF clear, AC clear, canonical/nonwrapping full-width spans, alignment and permission assumptions are explicit. n=0 makes the input spans vacuous. The return target is canonical and distinct from entry, but need not be outside other routine instructions or executable: scope ends immediately after actual RET.


Literal source: [development/approved-plan/contract/crypto-pre-v2.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/contract/crypto-pre-v2.ml)

```ocaml
(* BP-20260930-E2 candidate: explicit scalar-state entry domain.
   A definition is not a theorem or an assumption of target behavior.
   The missing architectural ABI fields require a qualified extension.
   Human review remains pending. No old E1 result is relabeled E2. *)

let bp_e2_crypto_pre = new_definition
 `bp_e2_crypto_pre pc stackpointer ra p q n m rp xp ordinary (s:x86state) <=>
    n < 2 EXP 64 /\ pc+95 <= 2 EXP 64 /\
    bytes_loaded s (word pc) crypto_memcmp_mc /\
    read RIP s = word pc /\ read RSP s = stackpointer /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) s = ra /\
    C_ARGUMENTS [p;q;word n] s /\ read memory s = m /\
    val(stackpointer:int64) MOD 16 = 8 /\ val stackpointer+8 < 2 EXP 64 /\
    assurance_canonical48 (val(ra:int64)) /\ ~(ra = word pc) /\
    read DF s = F /\ ~bit 18 (read rflags s) /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. xp a /\ ordinary a) pc 95 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) (val stackpointer) 8 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) (val(p:int64)) n /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) (val(q:int64)) n /\
    (!a. ~(assurance_region (word pc) 95 a /\
           assurance_region stackpointer 8 a))`;;

let bp_e2_crypto_allowed_read = new_definition
 `bp_e2_crypto_allowed_read rp ordinary p q n stackpointer a <=>
    rp a /\ ordinary a /\
    (assurance_region p n a \/ assurance_region q n a \/
     assurance_region stackpointer 8 a)`;;

let bp_e2_crypto_allowed_write = new_definition
 `bp_e2_crypto_allowed_write (a:num) <=> F`;;

let bp_e2_crypto_allowed_fetch = new_definition
 `bp_e2_crypto_allowed_fetch xp ordinary pc a <=>
    xp a /\ ordinary a /\ assurance_region (word pc) 95 a`;;

(* No code/data parse is postulated here. Independent final-ELF extraction,
   PT_LOAD/address/relocation correspondence and a declared loaded image bind
   crypto_memcmp_mc and pc to the final library in the acceptance driver. *)

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/assurance/e2-wrappers/full-domain-definitions.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/assurance/e2-wrappers/full-domain-definitions.ml)

```ocaml
(* All lengths, including empty and production sixteen-byte fast path. *)
let bp_assurance_call_steps = new_definition
 `bp_assurance_call_steps n = if n=0 then 6 else if n=16 then 15 else 7*n+10`;;
let bp_assurance_call_post = new_definition
 `bp_assurance_call_post pc stackpointer ra p q n e m s (s':x86state) <=>
    read RIP s' = ra /\ val(read RSP s') = val(stackpointer:int64)+8 /\
    read RAX s' = (if (!i. i<n ==> m(word_add p (word i)) =
                                  m(word_add q (word i)))
                  then word 0 else word 1) /\
    read memory s' = m /\ bytes_loaded s' (word pc) crypto_memcmp_mc /\
    read events s' = APPEND (crypto_trace_call pc p q n stackpointer ra) e /\
    (MAYCHANGE [RIP;RSP;RAX;RDI;RSI;RDX;R10;R11] ,,
       MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,, MAYCHANGE [events]) s s'`;;

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/interface/crypto/definitions-v1.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/interface/crypto/definitions-v1.ml)

```ocaml
(* E2 fixed post-initialization public-entry interface, definition V1.
   This narrows loading/environment state, never the universal input domain.
   file_bytes is the entire pinned production ELF's acquired byte map;
   its acquisition interpretation is an explicit trusted boundary. The
   actual public symbol is load_bias+0x241840 = 0x10241840. *)
let bp_e2_crypto_public_pre = new_definition
 `bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n
                         m rp xp ordinary (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) <=>
    fb_file_code file_bytes /\ fb_full_image file_bytes initial_rw padding m /\
    bp_e2_crypto_pre 0x10241840 stackpointer ra p q n m rp xp ordinary s /\
    (!addr. lw_image_domain addr
       ==> rp addr /\ ordinary addr /\ (xp addr <=> lw_execute addr)) /\
    assurance_canonical48 (val(read ABI_FS_BASE a))`;;
let bp_e2_crypto_public_post = new_definition
 `bp_e2_crypto_public_post file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n
                          e m s a (t:x86state#abi_omitted_state) <=>
    bp_assurance_call_post 0x10241840 stackpointer ra p q n e m s (FST t) /\
    fb_full_image file_bytes initial_rw padding (read memory (FST t)) /\
    SND t = a /\ abi_required_controls_preserved a (SND t)`;;

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/interface/crypto/statements-v1.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/interface/crypto/statements-v1.ml)

```ocaml
(* The pre/post definitions are frozen independently of the proof recipe.
   All allowed lengths, contents, alignments and overlaps remain quantified.
   Policy includes progress, all original successors, every full-width data
   access and fetch, and the actual final RET transition. *)
let bp_e2_crypto_public_policy_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp xp ordinary s a.
    bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n
      m rp xp ordinary (s,a)
    ==> assurance_checked_run (bp_assurance_call_steps n)
      (bp_assurance_call_post 0x10241840 stackpointer ra p q n
         (read events s) m s)
      (assurance_zero_scalar_step
        (bp_e2_crypto_allowed_read rp ordinary p q n stackpointer)
        bp_e2_crypto_allowed_write
        (bp_e2_crypto_allowed_fetch xp ordinary 0x10241840)) s`;;
let bp_e2_crypto_public_interface_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp xp ordinary s a.
    bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n
      m rp xp ordinary (s,a)
    ==> eventually_n abi_scalar_extended_step (bp_assurance_call_steps n)
      (bp_e2_crypto_public_post file_bytes initial_rw padding
        stackpointer ra p q n (read events s) m s a) (s,a)`;;
(* This implication connects every concrete family witness to exactly the
   public pre used by both checked interface results, not a weaker proxy. *)
let bp_e2_crypto_public_witness_bridge_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding image p q n different.
    fb_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding image p q n different
    ==> bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding
      (word 0x300008) (word 0x400000) p q n
      (lw_memory image p q n different) (fb_read p q n) lw_execute
      (fb_ordinary p q n) (fb_machine image p q n different)`;;

```


The policy recursively requires progress and checks every original successor at each step; endpoint function, frame and event properties are synchronized at the same exact count without a determinism assumption. It forbids every store and restricts each full-width load to the logical input spans or incoming return slot. Fetch coverage checks every decoded instruction byte against the declared immutable 95-byte code region and executable ordinary-memory permissions. The observer projects to the original transition and backlifts all original successors. It records actual pre-RIP, decoded opcode/length and chronological events, rather than inferring them from an endpoint list.


Literal source: [development/approved-plan/assurance/path.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/assurance/path.ml)

```ocaml
(* Exact finite runs over every original successor. Policy is an obligation on
   each successor, never a guard that changes the original x86 relation. *)
let assurance_checked_run = new_recursive_definition num_RECURSION
 `(assurance_checked_run 0 (P:x86state->bool)
      (C:num->x86state->x86state->bool) s <=> P s) /\
  (assurance_checked_run (SUC n) P C s <=>
     (?s'. x86 s s') /\
     !s'. x86 s s' ==> C (SUC n) s s' /\
                        assurance_checked_run n P C s')`;;

let ASSURANCE_RUN_PROJECT = prove
 (`!n P C s. assurance_checked_run n P C s
    ==> eventually_n x86 n P s`,
  INDUCT_TAC THENL
   [REWRITE_TAC[assurance_checked_run; EVENTUALLY_N_TRIVIAL];
    REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN REWRITE_TAC[assurance_checked_run] THEN
    REWRITE_TAC[ARITH_RULE `SUC n = 1+n`; EVENTUALLY_N_STEP] THEN
    STRIP_TAC THEN CONJ_TAC THENL
     [ASM_MESON_TAC[];
      X_GEN_TAC `next:x86state` THEN DISCH_TAC THEN
      FIRST_X_ASSUM MATCH_MP_TAC THEN ASM_MESON_TAC[]]]);;

let ASSURANCE_RUN_EVENTUALLY = prove
 (`!n P C s. assurance_checked_run n P C s ==> eventually x86 P s`,
  MESON_TAC[ASSURANCE_RUN_PROJECT; EVENTUALLY_N_EVENTUALLY]);;

let ASSURANCE_RUN_ALL_SUCCESSORS = prove
 (`!n P C s. assurance_checked_run (SUC n) P C s
    ==> (?s'. x86 s s') /\
        !s'. x86 s s' ==> C (SUC n) s s' /\
                           assurance_checked_run n P C s'`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_checked_run]);;

let assurance_step_policy = new_definition
 `assurance_step_policy rp wp xp (s:x86state) (s':x86state) <=>
   ?len inst d.
     x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) /\
     assurance_span_ok xp (val(read RIP s)) len /\
     read events s' = APPEND d (read events s) /\
     ALL (assurance_event_policy rp wp) d`;;

let assurance_zero_step = new_definition
 `assurance_zero_step rp wp xp remaining s s' <=>
   assurance_step_policy rp wp xp s s' /\
   (remaining = 1 ==> assurance_actual_ret 2 s s')`;;

let ASSURANCE_DECODED_OBSERVATION_ALL_SUCCESSORS = prove
 (`!len inst s s'.
    x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) /\ x86 s s'
    ==> assurance_observed_step s
          (assurance_observation s len inst s') s'`,
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN REWRITE_TAC[x86] THEN
  DISCH_THEN(CONJUNCTS_THEN2 ASSUME_TAC
    (X_CHOOSE_THEN `n:num`
      (X_CHOOSE_THEN `decoded:instruction` STRIP_ASSUME_TAC))) THEN
  SUBGOAL_THEN `(n:num) = (len:num) /\ (decoded:instruction) = (inst:instruction)`
    (CONJUNCTS_THEN SUBST_ALL_TAC) THENL
   [MP_TAC(ISPECL [`s:x86state`; `read RIP s`;
      `(len:num,inst:instruction)`; `(n:num,decoded:instruction)`]
      x86_decode_unique) THEN ASM_REWRITE_TAC[PAIR_EQ] THEN MESON_TAC[];
    REWRITE_TAC[assurance_observed_step] THEN
    MAP_EVERY EXISTS_TAC [`len:num`; `inst:instruction`] THEN
    ASM_REWRITE_TAC[]]);;

let ASSURANCE_ALL_REVERSE = prove
 (`!P (l:A list). ALL P (REVERSE l) <=> ALL P l`,
  GEN_TAC THEN LIST_INDUCT_TAC THEN
  ASM_REWRITE_TAC[REVERSE; ALL_APPEND; ALL] THEN MESON_TAC[]);;

let ASSURANCE_STEP_POLICY_OBSERVED = prove
 (`!rp wp xp s s'.
    x86 s s' /\ assurance_step_policy rp wp xp s s'
    ==> ?obs. assurance_observed_step s obs s' /\
              assurance_fetch_policy xp obs /\
              ALL (assurance_event_policy rp wp) (SND(SND(SND obs))) /\
              read events s' = APPEND (REVERSE(SND(SND(SND obs))))
                                      (read events s)`,
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN REWRITE_TAC[assurance_step_policy] THEN
  DISCH_THEN(CONJUNCTS_THEN2 ASSUME_TAC
    (X_CHOOSE_THEN `len:num` (X_CHOOSE_THEN `inst:instruction`
      (X_CHOOSE_THEN `d:uarch_event list` STRIP_ASSUME_TAC)))) THEN
  EXISTS_TAC `assurance_observation s len inst s'` THEN
  CONJ_TAC THENL
   [MATCH_MP_TAC ASSURANCE_DECODED_OBSERVATION_ALL_SUCCESSORS THEN
    ASM_REWRITE_TAC[];
    FIRST_ASSUM(ASSUME_TAC o MATCH_MP ASSURANCE_DELTA_EXACT) THEN
    ASM_REWRITE_TAC[assurance_observation; assurance_fetch_policy;
      FST; SND; ASSURANCE_ALL_REVERSE; REVERSE_REVERSE]]);;

let ASSURANCE_CODE_OFFSET_VALUE = prove
 (`!pc offset. pc+95 <= 2 EXP 64 /\ offset < 95
    ==> val(word(pc+offset):int64) = pc+offset`,
  REWRITE_TAC[VAL_WORD; DIMINDEX_64] THEN REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
  MATCH_MP_TAC MOD_LT THEN ASM_ARITH_TAC);;

let ASSURANCE_CODE_BASE_VALUE = prove
 (`!pc. pc+95 <= 2 EXP 64 ==> val(word pc:int64) = pc`,
  REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
  MP_TAC(SPECL [`pc:num`; `0`] ASSURANCE_CODE_OFFSET_VALUE) THEN
  ASM_REWRITE_TAC[ADD_CLAUSES; ARITH_RULE `0 < 95`]);;

let ASSURANCE_RUN_STEP = prove
 (`!n P C s. assurance_checked_run (1+n) P C s <=>
     (?s'. x86 s s') /\
     !s'. x86 s s' ==> C (1+n) s s' /\
                        assurance_checked_run n P C s'`,
  REWRITE_TAC[ARITH_RULE `1+n = SUC n`; assurance_checked_run]);;

(* The stepping tactic reuses the pinned decoder/executor conversion and state
   update machinery. It adds a proved obligation on each original successor.
   No original successor is discarded to make the obligation true. *)
let ASSURANCE_RET_FROM_DECODE_AND_STEP = prove
 (`!len s s'. x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,RET) /\ x86 s s'
    ==> assurance_actual_ret len s s'`,
  MESON_TAC[ASSURANCE_DECODED_RET_ALL_SUCCESSORS]);;

let ASSURANCE_ZERO_LOCAL_POLICY_TAC decode_ths offset original_step update =
  let dt = Option.get decode_ths.(offset) in
  let _,conseq = dest_imp(snd(strip_forall(concl dt))) in
  let len,inst = dest_pair(rand conseq) in
  let d = if offset = 14 then
      `[EventJump(word(pc+16),word(pc+93))]`
    else if offset = 93 then
      `[EventJump(word(pc+95),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer:int64,8)]`
    else `[]:uarch_event list` in
  GEN_REWRITE_TAC I [assurance_zero_step] THEN CONJ_TAC THENL
   [REWRITE_TAC[assurance_step_policy] THEN
    MAP_EVERY EXISTS_TAC [len; inst; d] THEN
    REPEAT CONJ_TAC THENL
     [ASM_REWRITE_TAC[] THEN MATCH_MP_TAC(REWRITE_RULE[ADD_CLAUSES] dt) THEN ASM_REWRITE_TAC[];
      ASM_REWRITE_TAC[] THEN
      ASM_SIMP_TAC[ASSURANCE_CODE_OFFSET_VALUE; ASSURANCE_CODE_BASE_VALUE;
        ARITH_RULE(mk_binary "<" (mk_numeral(num offset),`95`))] THEN
      MP_TAC(ISPECL [`xp:num->bool`; `pc:num`; `95`;
                     mk_numeral(num offset); len] ASSURANCE_SPAN_SUBRANGE) THEN
      ANTS_TAC THENL
       [ASM_REWRITE_TAC[] THEN ARITH_TAC;
        REWRITE_TAC[ADD_CLAUSES]];
      SUBST1_TAC(SYM update) THEN
      ASM_REWRITE_TAC[APPEND] THEN
      CONV_TAC(TOP_DEPTH_CONV COMPONENT_READ_OVER_WRITE_CONV) THEN
      ASM_REWRITE_TAC[APPEND];
      REWRITE_TAC[ALL; assurance_event_policy] THEN ASM_REWRITE_TAC[]];
    (if offset <> 93 then
       CONV_TAC NUM_REDUCE_CONV THEN REWRITE_TAC[]
     else REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
    MATCH_MP_TAC ASSURANCE_RET_FROM_DECODE_AND_STEP THEN
    CONJ_TAC THENL
     [ASM_REWRITE_TAC[] THEN
      MATCH_MP_TAC(Option.get decode_ths.(93)) THEN ASM_REWRITE_TAC[];
      ACCEPT_TAC original_step])];;

let ASSURANCE_ZERO_BASIC_STEP_TAC offset decode_ths sname
      (store_inst_term_to:term ref option) (asl,w) =
  let _,args = strip_comb w in
  let stepn = dest_numeral(hd args) in
  let count = stepn -/ num 1 in
  let n_th = GSYM(NUM_ADD_CONV(mk_binary "+" (`1:num`,mk_numeral count))) in
  let sv = last args and sv' = mk_var(sname,`:x86state`) in
  let eth = X86_CONV decode_ths (map snd asl)
      (mk_comb(mk_comb(`x86`,sv),sv')) in
  (GEN_REWRITE_TAC
     (RATOR_CONV o RATOR_CONV o RATOR_CONV o RAND_CONV) [n_th] THEN
   GEN_REWRITE_TAC I [ASSURANCE_RUN_STEP] THEN CONJ_TAC THENL
    [GEN_REWRITE_TAC BINDER_CONV [eth] THEN
     CONV_TAC EXISTS_NONTRIVIAL_CONV;
     X_GEN_TAC sv' THEN DISCH_THEN(fun original_step ->
       MP_TAC(EQ_MP eth original_step) THEN
       REPEAT X86_UNDEFINED_CHOOSE_TAC THEN
       DISCH_THEN(fun update -> CONJ_TAC THENL
        [ASSURANCE_ZERO_LOCAL_POLICY_TAC decode_ths offset original_step update;
         MP_TAC update]))]) (asl,w);;

let ASSURANCE_ZERO_STEP_TAC offset (length_th,decode_ths) sname =
  ASSURANCE_ZERO_BASIC_STEP_TAC offset decode_ths sname None THEN
  NONSELFMODIFYING_STATE_UPDATE_TAC
    (MATCH_MP bytes_loaded_update length_th) THEN
  ASSUMPTION_STATE_UPDATE_TAC THEN
  DISCH_THEN(fun update ->
    let reads = STATE_UPDATE_NEW_RULE update in
    if reads = [] then ALL_TAC else
    MP_TAC(end_itlist CONJ reads) THEN
    ASSEMBLER_SIMPLIFY_TAC THEN STRIP_TAC);;

let ASSURANCE_CRYPTO_ZERO_COMPLETE_POLICY = prove
 (`!pc stackpointer ra p q xp rp e s.
    pc+95 <= 2 EXP 64 /\ assurance_span_ok xp pc 95 /\
    assurance_span_ok
      (\a. rp a /\ assurance_region stackpointer 8 a)
      (val(stackpointer:int64)) 8 /\
    bytes_loaded s (word pc) crypto_memcmp_mc /\
    read RIP s = word pc /\ read RSP s = stackpointer /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) s = ra /\
    C_ARGUMENTS [p;q;word 0] s /\ read events s = e
    ==> assurance_checked_run 6
      (\s'. read RIP s' = ra /\
            read RSP s' = word_add stackpointer (word 8) /\
            read RAX s' = word 0 /\
            bytes_loaded s' (word pc) crypto_memcmp_mc /\
            read events s' = APPEND
              [EventJump(word(pc+95),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
               EventJump(word(pc+16),word(pc+93))] e)
      (assurance_zero_step
        (\a. rp a /\ assurance_region stackpointer 8 a) (\a. F) xp) s`,
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN
  REWRITE_TAC[C_ARGUMENTS; fst ASSURANCE_CRYPTO_EXEC] THEN STRIP_TAC THEN
  MAP_EVERY (fun (offset,sname) ->
    ASSURANCE_ZERO_STEP_TAC offset ASSURANCE_CRYPTO_EXEC sname)
    [0,"z1";4,"z2";7,"z3";10,"z4";14,"z5";93,"z6"] THEN
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_checked_run] THEN ASM_REWRITE_TAC[APPEND]);;
```

Literal source: [development/approved-plan/assurance/policy.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/assurance/policy.ml)

```ocaml
(* Mathematical access policy on observations of the original relation.
   Permissions remain explicit environment predicates. No new memory
   interpreter and no predicate filters the x86 successor relation. *)

let assurance_canonical48 = new_definition
 `assurance_canonical48 a <=>
    a < 2 EXP 64 /\ (a < 2 EXP 47 \/ 2 EXP 64 - 2 EXP 47 <= a)`;;

let assurance_region = new_definition
 `assurance_region (p:int64) n a <=> val p <= a /\ a < val p+n`;;

let assurance_span_ok = new_definition
 `assurance_span_ok perm a width <=>
    a+width <= 2 EXP 64 /\
    !j. j < width ==> assurance_canonical48 (a+j) /\ perm(a+j)`;;

let assurance_event_policy = new_definition
 `assurance_event_policy rp wp e <=>
    match e with
    | EventLoad(adr,width) -> assurance_span_ok rp (val adr) width
    | EventStore(adr,width) -> assurance_span_ok wp (val adr) width
    | _ -> T`;;

let assurance_fetch_policy = new_definition
 `assurance_fetch_policy xp
      (obs:int64#num#instruction#uarch_event list) <=>
    assurance_span_ok xp (val(FST obs)) (FST(SND obs))`;;

let ASSURANCE_SPAN_SUBRANGE = prove
 (`!perm a width offset count.
    assurance_span_ok perm a width /\ offset+count <= width
    ==> assurance_span_ok perm (a+offset) count`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_span_ok] THEN
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN STRIP_TAC THEN CONJ_TAC THENL
   [ASM_ARITH_TAC;
    X_GEN_TAC `j:num` THEN DISCH_TAC THEN
    FIRST_X_ASSUM(MP_TAC o SPEC `offset+j:num`) THEN
    ASM_REWRITE_TAC[ADD_ASSOC] THEN ANTS_TAC THENL
     [ASM_ARITH_TAC; MESON_TAC[]]]);;

let ASSURANCE_ZERO_STACK_EVENT_POLICY = prove
 (`!rp wp ip ra sp.
    assurance_span_ok rp (val(sp:int64)) 8
    ==> ALL (assurance_event_policy rp wp)
        [EventJump(ip,ra);EventLoad(sp,8)]`,
  REWRITE_TAC[ALL; assurance_event_policy] THEN MESON_TAC[]);;

let ASSURANCE_RET_EVENT_POLICY = prove
 (`!rp wp len s s'.
    assurance_actual_ret len s s' /\
    assurance_span_ok rp (val(read RSP s)) 8
    ==> ALL (assurance_event_policy rp wp)
              (assurance_delta s s')`,
  REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
  FIRST_ASSUM(MP_TAC o MATCH_MP ASSURANCE_ACTUAL_RET_EFFECTS) THEN
  STRIP_TAC THEN
  FIRST_X_ASSUM(ASSUME_TAC o MATCH_MP ASSURANCE_DELTA_EXACT) THEN
  ASM_REWRITE_TAC[ALL; assurance_event_policy]);;

let ASSURANCE_STACK_ADVANCE_NONWRAP = prove
 (`!stackpointer:int64. val stackpointer + 8 < 2 EXP 64
    ==> val(word_add stackpointer (word 8)) = val stackpointer + 8`,
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN DISCH_TAC THEN
  REWRITE_TAC[VAL_WORD_ADD; VAL_WORD; DIMINDEX_64] THEN
  CONV_TAC NUM_REDUCE_CONV THEN
  MATCH_MP_TAC MOD_LT THEN
  RULE_ASSUM_TAC(CONV_RULE NUM_REDUCE_CONV) THEN ASM_REWRITE_TAC[]);;

let ASSURANCE_FETCH_INTERVAL_FROM_REGION = prove
 (`!xp codebase codesize ip len inst es.
    assurance_span_ok xp (val(codebase:int64)) codesize /\
    val codebase <= val(ip:int64) /\
    val ip+len <= val codebase+codesize
    ==> assurance_fetch_policy xp (ip,len,inst,es)`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_fetch_policy; FST; SND] THEN
  REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
  MP_TAC(ISPECL [`xp:num->bool`; `val(codebase:int64)`; `codesize:num`;
    `val(ip:int64)-val(codebase:int64)`; `len:num`]
    ASSURANCE_SPAN_SUBRANGE) THEN
  ANTS_TAC THENL [ASM_REWRITE_TAC[] THEN ASM_ARITH_TAC; ALL_TAC] THEN
  SUBGOAL_THEN
    `val(codebase:int64)+(val(ip:int64)-val(codebase:int64)) = val ip`
    SUBST1_TAC THENL [ASM_ARITH_TAC; MESON_TAC[]]);;

let ASSURANCE_SPAN_SELF_REGION = prove
 (`!rp p n. assurance_span_ok rp (val(p:int64)) n
    ==> assurance_span_ok
          (\a. rp a /\ assurance_region p n a) (val p) n`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_span_ok; assurance_region] THEN
  REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
  ASM_MESON_TAC[ARITH_RULE
   `j < n ==> p <= p+j /\ p+j < p+n`]);;

let ASSURANCE_SPAN_MONO = prove
 (`!rp rp' a width.
    assurance_span_ok rp a width /\ (!x. rp x ==> rp' x)
    ==> assurance_span_ok rp' a width`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_span_ok] THEN MESON_TAC[]);;

let ASSURANCE_SPAN_WORD_OFFSET_8 = prove
 (`!rp p. assurance_span_ok rp (val(p:int64)) 16
    ==> assurance_span_ok rp (val(word_add p (word 8))) 8`,
  REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
  SUBGOAL_THEN `val(p:int64)+8 < 2 EXP 64` ASSUME_TAC THENL
   [FIRST_X_ASSUM(MP_TAC o REWRITE_RULE[assurance_span_ok]) THEN
    ARITH_TAC; ALL_TAC] THEN
  ASM_SIMP_TAC[ASSURANCE_STACK_ADVANCE_NONWRAP] THEN
  MATCH_MP_TAC ASSURANCE_SPAN_SUBRANGE THEN
  EXISTS_TAC `16` THEN ASM_REWRITE_TAC[] THEN ARITH_TAC);;

let ASSURANCE_SPAN_PREFIX = prove
 (`!rp a width count.
    assurance_span_ok rp a width /\ count <= width
    ==> assurance_span_ok rp a count`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_span_ok] THEN
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN STRIP_TAC THEN CONJ_TAC THENL
   [ASM_ARITH_TAC;
    X_GEN_TAC `j:num` THEN DISCH_TAC THEN
    ASM_MESON_TAC[LTE_TRANS]]);;

let ASSURANCE_CRYPTO_16_DATA_POLICY = prove
 (`!rp wp p q stackpointer ip ra.
    assurance_span_ok rp (val(p:int64)) 16 /\
    assurance_span_ok rp (val(q:int64)) 16 /\
    assurance_span_ok rp (val(stackpointer:int64)) 8
    ==> ALL (assurance_event_policy
          (\a. rp a /\ (assurance_region p 16 a \/
                        assurance_region q 16 a \/
                        assurance_region stackpointer 8 a)) wp)
        [EventJump(ip,ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
         EventLoad(word_add q (word 8),8);EventLoad(q,8);
         EventLoad(word_add p (word 8),8);EventLoad(p,8)]`,
  REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
  SUBGOAL_THEN
   `assurance_span_ok
      (\a. rp a /\ (assurance_region p 16 a \/
                    assurance_region q 16 a \/
                    assurance_region stackpointer 8 a)) (val(p:int64)) 16 /\
    assurance_span_ok
      (\a. rp a /\ (assurance_region p 16 a \/
                    assurance_region q 16 a \/
                    assurance_region stackpointer 8 a)) (val(q:int64)) 16 /\
    assurance_span_ok
      (\a. rp a /\ (assurance_region p 16 a \/
                    assurance_region q 16 a \/
                    assurance_region stackpointer 8 a)) (val(stackpointer:int64)) 8`
   STRIP_ASSUME_TAC THENL
    [REPEAT CONJ_TAC THEN MATCH_MP_TAC ASSURANCE_SPAN_MONO THENL
      [EXISTS_TAC `\a. rp a /\ assurance_region (p:int64) 16 a`;
       EXISTS_TAC `\a. rp a /\ assurance_region (q:int64) 16 a`;
       EXISTS_TAC `\a. rp a /\ assurance_region (stackpointer:int64) 8 a`] THEN
     ASM_SIMP_TAC[ASSURANCE_SPAN_SELF_REGION] THEN MESON_TAC[];
     REWRITE_TAC[ALL; assurance_event_policy] THEN
     ASM_SIMP_TAC[ASSURANCE_SPAN_WORD_OFFSET_8] THEN
     REPEAT CONJ_TAC THEN
     MATCH_MP_TAC ASSURANCE_SPAN_PREFIX THEN
     EXISTS_TAC `16` THEN ASM_REWRITE_TAC[] THEN ARITH_TAC]);;
```

Literal source: [development/approved-plan/assurance/correspondence.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/assurance/correspondence.ml)

```ocaml
(* Conservative observed-transition bridge. Development evidence only.
   The relation does not filter any successor of the pinned x86 relation.
   Event deltas are meaningful only after the separate append-prefix property
   has been established; exception semantics can overwrite the events field. *)

let assurance_delta = new_definition
 `assurance_delta (s:x86state) (s':x86state) =
    @d:uarch_event list. read events s' = APPEND d (read events s)`;;

let assurance_observation = new_definition
 `assurance_observation (s:x86state) (len:num) (inst:instruction) (s':x86state) =
    (read RIP s,len,inst,REVERSE(assurance_delta s s'))`;;

let assurance_observed_step = new_definition
 `assurance_observed_step (s:x86state)
      (obs:int64#num#instruction#uarch_event list) (s':x86state) <=>
    ?len inst.
      x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) /\
      (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,,
       x86_execute inst) s s' /\
      obs = assurance_observation s len inst s'`;;

let ASSURANCE_OBSERVED_PROJECTION = prove
 (`!s obs s'. assurance_observed_step s obs s' ==> x86 s s'`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_observed_step; x86] THEN MESON_TAC[]);;

let ASSURANCE_OBSERVED_BACKLIFT_ALL = prove
 (`!s s'. x86 s s' ==> ?obs. assurance_observed_step s obs s'`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_observed_step; x86] THEN MESON_TAC[]);;

let ASSURANCE_OBSERVED_EQUIVALENCE = prove
 (`!s s'. (?obs. assurance_observed_step s obs s') <=> x86 s s'`,
  MESON_TAC[ASSURANCE_OBSERVED_PROJECTION;
            ASSURANCE_OBSERVED_BACKLIFT_ALL]);;

let ASSURANCE_OBSERVED_PROGRESS = prove
 (`!s. (?s'. x86 s s') <=> ?obs s'. assurance_observed_step s obs s'`,
  MESON_TAC[ASSURANCE_OBSERVED_EQUIVALENCE]);;

let ASSURANCE_OBSERVED_FETCH_CORRESPONDENCE = prove
 (`!s ip len inst es s'.
    assurance_observed_step s (ip,len,inst,es) s'
    ==> ip = read RIP s /\
        x86_decode s ip (len,inst) /\
        (?l. bytes_loaded s ip l /\ LENGTH l = len /\
             decode l = SOME(inst,[]))`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_observed_step; assurance_observation;
              PAIR_EQ; x86_decode] THEN MESON_TAC[]);;

let ASSURANCE_DELTA_EXACT = prove
 (`!s s' d. read events s' = APPEND d (read events s)
             ==> assurance_delta s s' = d`,
  REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN REWRITE_TAC[assurance_delta] THEN
  MATCH_MP_TAC SELECT_UNIQUE THEN GEN_TAC THEN
  ASM_REWRITE_TAC[APPEND_RCANCEL] THEN MESON_TAC[]);;

let ASSURANCE_OBSERVATION_EVENT_ORDER = prove
 (`!s s' len inst d.
    read events s' = APPEND d (read events s)
    ==> assurance_observation s len inst s' =
        (read RIP s,len,inst,REVERSE d)`,
  SIMP_TAC[assurance_observation; ASSURANCE_DELTA_EXACT]);;

let ASSURANCE_DELTA_CONCATENATION = prove
 (`!s0 s1 s2 d1 d2.
    read events s1 = APPEND d1 (read events s0) /\
    read events s2 = APPEND d2 (read events s1)
    ==> assurance_delta s0 s2 = APPEND d2 d1 /\
        REVERSE(assurance_delta s0 s2) =
          APPEND (REVERSE d1) (REVERSE d2)`,
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN STRIP_TAC THEN
  SUBGOAL_THEN `assurance_delta s0 s2 = APPEND d2 d1` ASSUME_TAC THENL
   [MATCH_MP_TAC ASSURANCE_DELTA_EXACT THEN
    ASM_REWRITE_TAC[APPEND_ASSOC];
    ASM_REWRITE_TAC[REVERSE_APPEND]]);;

(* Same universal-successor eventuality, without throwing away deadlocks or
   nondeterministic choices. This is a relation equality, not a new prover. *)
let ASSURANCE_EVENTUALLY_CORRESPONDENCE = prove
 (`!P s. eventually (\a b. ?obs. assurance_observed_step a obs b) P s
           <=> eventually x86 P s`,
  REWRITE_TAC[ASSURANCE_OBSERVED_EQUIVALENCE; ETA_AX]);;

let assurance_actual_ret = new_definition
 `assurance_actual_ret len (s:x86state) (s':x86state) <=>
    x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,RET) /\
    (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,, x86_RET) s s'`;;

let ASSURANCE_EXECUTE_RET = prove
 (`x86_execute RET = x86_RET`,
  REWRITE_TAC[FUN_EQ_THM; x86_execute]);;

let ASSURANCE_ACTUAL_RET_PROJECTION = prove
 (`!len s s'. assurance_actual_ret len s s' ==> x86 s s'`,
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN REWRITE_TAC[assurance_actual_ret; x86] THEN
  STRIP_TAC THEN EXISTS_TAC `len:num` THEN EXISTS_TAC `RET` THEN
  ASM_REWRITE_TAC[ASSURANCE_EXECUTE_RET]);;

let ASSURANCE_ACTUAL_RET_EFFECTS = prove
 (`!len s s'. assurance_actual_ret len s s'
    ==> read RSP s' = word_add (read RSP s) (word 8) /\
        read RIP s' = read (memory :> bytes64 (read RSP s)) s /\
        read events s' = APPEND
          [EventJump(word_add (read RIP s) (word len),
                     read (memory :> bytes64 (read RSP s)) s);
           EventLoad(read RSP s,8)] (read events s)`,
  REWRITE_TAC[assurance_actual_ret; SEQ; x86_RET;
              LET_DEF; LET_END_DEF; assign] THEN
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN STRIP_TAC THEN
  FIRST_X_ASSUM(SUBST1_TAC o SYM) THEN
  REWRITE_TAC[APPEND] THEN
  CONV_TAC(DEPTH_CONV COMPONENT_READ_OVER_WRITE_CONV) THEN
  REWRITE_TAC[]);;

let ASSURANCE_ACTUAL_RET_OBSERVATION = prove
 (`!len s s'. assurance_actual_ret len s s'
    ==> assurance_observation s len RET s' =
        (read RIP s,len,RET,
         [EventLoad(read RSP s,8);
          EventJump(word_add (read RIP s) (word len),
                    read (memory :> bytes64 (read RSP s)) s)])`,
  REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
  FIRST_ASSUM(MP_TAC o MATCH_MP ASSURANCE_ACTUAL_RET_EFFECTS) THEN
  STRIP_TAC THEN
  FIRST_X_ASSUM(ASSUME_TAC o MATCH_MP ASSURANCE_DELTA_EXACT) THEN
  ASM_REWRITE_TAC[assurance_observation; REVERSE; APPEND]);;

let ASSURANCE_DECODED_RET_ALL_SUCCESSORS = prove
 (`!len s. x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,RET)
    ==> !s'. x86 s s' ==> assurance_actual_ret len s s'`,
  REPEAT GEN_TAC THEN DISCH_TAC THEN GEN_TAC THEN
  REWRITE_TAC[x86] THEN
  DISCH_THEN(X_CHOOSE_THEN `n:num`
    (X_CHOOSE_THEN `inst:instruction` STRIP_ASSUME_TAC)) THEN
  SUBGOAL_THEN `(n:num) = (len:num) /\ inst = RET`
    (CONJUNCTS_THEN SUBST_ALL_TAC) THENL
   [MP_TAC(ISPECL [`s:x86state`; `read RIP s`; `(len:num,RET)`;
                   `(n:num,inst:instruction)`] x86_decode_unique) THEN
    ASM_REWRITE_TAC[PAIR_EQ] THEN MESON_TAC[];
    RULE_ASSUM_TAC(REWRITE_RULE[ASSURANCE_EXECUTE_RET]) THEN
    ASM_REWRITE_TAC[assurance_actual_ret]]);;

let ASSURANCE_DECODED_RET_PROGRESS = prove
 (`!len s. x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,RET)
    ==> ?s'. assurance_actual_ret len s s'`,
  REPEAT STRIP_TAC THEN
  ASM_REWRITE_TAC[assurance_actual_ret; SEQ; x86_RET;
                  LET_DEF; LET_END_DEF; assign] THEN
  MESON_TAC[]);;

```


The separately versioned fourteen-field product semantics adds actual architectural-state fields omitted by the original model. Generic qualified scalar instruction effects preserve them; unqualified instructions leave them unconstrained. Physical fidelity of that effect clause remains an explicit ISA assumption justified by frozen primary architectural documents. The theorem establishes reached-site qualification and preservation in this declared semantics; a projection proof or inert ghost record alone would not establish physical ABI preservation. Original modeled frames independently preserve callee-saved integer, SIMD and mask fields.


Literal source: [development/approved-plan/abi-qualification/state-v4.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/abi-qualification/state-v4.ml)

```ocaml
(* SCALAR_ABI_EXTENSION_V4. Omitted fields; SIMD/mask/rflags remain in x86state. *)
let abi_omitted_state_INDUCT,abi_omitted_state_RECURSION,
    abi_omitted_state_COMPONENTS = define_auto_record_type
 "abi_omitted_state =
   { ABI_X87_CW : (16)word;
     ABI_X87_SW : (16)word;
     ABI_X87_TAG : (16)word;
     ABI_X87_FOP : (11)word;
     ABI_X87_IP : (64)word;
     ABI_X87_CS : (16)word;
     ABI_X87_DP : (64)word;
     ABI_X87_DS : (16)word;
     ABI_X87_REGS : (3)word -> (80)word;
     ABI_MXCSR : (32)word;
     ABI_FS_SELECTOR : (16)word;
     ABI_FS_BASE : (64)word;
     ABI_GS_SELECTOR : (16)word;
     ABI_GS_BASE : (64)word
   }";;

(* ABI-required x87/MXCSR controls plus FS thread-pointer state.
   MXCSR status bits 0..5 are caller-saved.
   DAZ bit 6 is included when supported; reserved values are not made into
   physically realizable states by this mathematical definition. *)
let abi_required_controls_preserved = new_definition
 `abi_required_controls_preserved (a:abi_omitted_state) a' <=>
    read ABI_X87_CW a' = read ABI_X87_CW a /\
    word_and (read ABI_MXCSR a') (word 0xffc0) =
    word_and (read ABI_MXCSR a) (word 0xffc0) /\
    read ABI_FS_SELECTOR a' = read ABI_FS_SELECTOR a /\
    read ABI_FS_BASE a' = read ABI_FS_BASE a`;;
```

Literal source: [development/approved-plan/abi-qualification/extension-v2.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/abi-qualification/extension-v2.ml)

```ocaml
(* SCALAR_ABI_EXTENSION_V2. Generic documented scalar instruction effects,
   independent of target identity, pc, memory contents or library bytes.
   Load state-v2.ml first. No unsupported instruction is assigned a no-op.
   Original decode/execute always supplies every modeled-state successor. *)

let abi_integer_memory_width = new_definition
 `abi_integer_memory_width w <=>
    w = Byte \/ w = Word \/ w = Doubleword \/ w = Quadword`;;

let abi_integer_writable = new_definition
 `abi_integer_writable op <=>
    (?r. op = Register r) \/
    (?w addr. op = Memop w addr /\ abi_integer_memory_width w)`;;

let abi_integer_immediate = new_definition
 `abi_integer_immediate op <=>
    (?v. op = Imm8 v) \/ (?v. op = Imm16 v) \/
    (?v. op = Imm32 v) \/ (?v. op = Imm64 v)`;;

let abi_integer_operand = new_definition
 `abi_integer_operand op <=>
    abi_integer_writable op \/ abi_integer_immediate op`;;

let abi_cmov_operand = new_definition
 `abi_cmov_operand op <=>
    (?r size. op = Register(Gpr r size) /\
              (size = Full_64 \/ size = Lower_32 \/ size = Lower_16)) \/
    (?w addr. op = Memop w addr /\
              (w = Word \/ w = Doubleword \/ w = Quadword))`;;

let abi_scalar_instruction = new_definition
 `abi_scalar_instruction inst <=>
    inst = ENDBR64 \/ inst = RET \/
    (?dst src. inst = MOV dst src /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\ abi_integer_operand src) \/
    (?dst src. inst = XOR dst src /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\ abi_integer_operand src) \/
    (?dst src. inst = OR dst src /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\ abi_integer_operand src) \/
    (?dst src. inst = CMP dst src /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\ abi_integer_operand src) \/
    (?dst. inst = DEC dst /\ abi_integer_writable dst) \/
    (?dst. inst = NEG dst /\ abi_integer_writable dst) \/
    (?r addr. inst = LEA (Register r) addr) \/
    (?cond r size src. inst = CMOV cond (Register(Gpr r size)) src /\
       (size = Full_64 \/ size = Lower_32 \/ size = Lower_16) /\ abi_cmov_operand src) \/
    (?cond disp. inst = JUMP cond (Imm8 disp)) \/
    (?cond disp. inst = JUMP cond (Imm32 disp)) \/
    (?dst count. inst = SHR dst count /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\
       ((?v. count = Imm8 v) \/ count = Register(Gpr (word 1) Lower_8)))`;;

(* The semantic clause for documented classes is equality of every omitted
   field. The original scalar transition is retained exactly. For all other
   decoded instructions, omitted-state successors are unconstrained. Formal
   projection/backlift proves relation conservativity, not physical fidelity. *)
let abi_scalar_extended_step = new_definition
 `abi_scalar_extended_step (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) (s',a') <=>
    ?len inst.
      x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) /\
      (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,, x86_execute inst) s s' /\
      (abi_scalar_instruction inst ==> a' = a)`;;

let abi_all_decodes_scalar = new_definition
 `abi_all_decodes_scalar (s:x86state) <=>
    !len inst. x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst)
               ==> abi_scalar_instruction inst`;;

```


The O2 result quantifies arbitrary actual first-RET execution pairs, including every original nondeterministic successor. Neither trace length, the comparison result nor the first mismatch is a premise. Nonempty trace shape excludes earlier RET and zero-step completion. Initial writable-image data and page padding may differ; the returned equality and loaded secret values are excluded from observations. Terminating execution existence is also checked. This is architectural instruction/control/data trace noninterference; elapsed time, speculation and microarchitectural behavior are outside the theorem.


Literal source: [development/approved-plan/crypto-loop/trace/noninterference/model.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/crypto-loop/trace/noninterference/model.ml)

```ocaml
(* Whole finite architectural observations over the ORIGINAL x86 relation.
   Each constructor demands progress and the same exact observation for EVERY
   original successor; it never guards or filters an execution to fit a trace. *)
let crypto_observed_run = new_recursive_definition list_RECURSION
 `(crypto_observed_run [] (P:x86state->bool) s <=> P s) /\
  (crypto_observed_run (CONS (obs:int64#num#instruction#uarch_event list) rest) P s <=>
     (?s'. x86 s s') /\
     !s'. x86 s s' ==> assurance_observed_step s obs s' /\
                         crypto_observed_run rest P s')`;;
(* A possibly nondeterministic actual observed path. This is used by the
   relational consequence to quantify all choices, not to pick a nice pair. *)
let crypto_observed_execution = new_recursive_definition list_RECURSION
 `(crypto_observed_execution [] (s:x86state) t <=> t=s) /\
  (crypto_observed_execution (CONS (obs:int64#num#instruction#uarch_event list) rest) s t <=>
     ?next. assurance_observed_step s obs next /\
            crypto_observed_execution rest next t)`;;

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/crypto-loop/trace/noninterference/first-return/model.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/crypto-loop/trace/noninterference/first-return/model.ml)

```ocaml
(* Actual observed execution ending at its FIRST decoded RET. No step count,
   comparison-result, memory equality or convenient successor choice is assumed. *)
let crypto_observation_is_ret = new_definition
 `crypto_observation_is_ret (obs:int64#num#instruction#uarch_event list) <=>
    FST(SND(SND obs)) = RET`;;
let crypto_first_ret_execution = new_definition
 `crypto_first_ret_execution trace (s:x86state) t <=>
    ~(trace=[]) /\ crypto_observed_execution trace s t /\
    crypto_observation_is_ret (LAST trace) /\
    ALL (\obs. ~crypto_observation_is_ret obs) (BUTLAST trace)`;;
(* Recursive characterization used only to prove the explicit relation above. *)
let crypto_ret_shape = new_recursive_definition list_RECURSION
 `(crypto_ret_shape [] <=> F) /\
  (crypto_ret_shape (CONS (obs:int64#num#instruction#uarch_event list) rest) <=>
     if crypto_observation_is_ret obs then rest=[] else crypto_ret_shape rest)`;;

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/crypto-loop/trace/noninterference/schema.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/crypto-loop/trace/noninterference/schema.ml)

```ocaml
(* Public fixed-code schema, chronological order. Decode entries are literal
   instruction terms from the production instruction sites; later theorems
   must establish each against the actual loaded decoder. No loaded value,
   result bit, or accumulator is present in an observation. *)
let crypto_public_decode = new_definition
 `crypto_public_decode off =
    if off=0 then (4,ENDBR64)
    else if off=4 then (3,XOR (% rax) (% rax))
    else if off=7 then (3,XOR (% r10) (% r10))
    else if off=10 then (4,CMP (% rdx) (Imm8 (word 0)))
    else if off=14 then (2,JUMP Condition_Z (Imm8 (word 77)))
    else if off=16 then (4,CMP (% rdx) (Imm8 (word 16)))
    else if off=20 then (2,JUMP Condition_NZ (Imm8 (word 42)))
    else if off=22 then (3,MOV (% r10) (Memop Quadword (%% (rdi,0))))
    else if off=25 then (4,MOV (% r11) (Memop Quadword (%% (rdi,8))))
    else if off=29 then (7,MOV (% rdx) (Imm32 (word 1)))
    else if off=36 then (3,XOR (% r10) (Memop Quadword (%% (rsi,0))))
    else if off=39 then (4,XOR (% r11) (Memop Quadword (%% (rsi,8))))
    else if off=43 then (3,OR (% r10) (% r11))
    else if off=46 then (4,CMOV Condition_NZ (% rax) (% rdx))
    else if off=50 then (2,RET)
    else if off=64 then (3,MOV (% r10b) (Memop Byte (%% (rdi,0))))
    else if off=67 then (4,LEA (% rdi) (%% (rdi,1)))
    else if off=71 then (3,XOR (% r10b) (Memop Byte (%% (rsi,0))))
    else if off=74 then (4,LEA (% rsi) (%% (rsi,1)))
    else if off=78 then (3,OR (% al) (% r10b))
    else if off=81 then (3,DEC (% rdx))
    else if off=84 then (2,JUMP Condition_NZ (Imm8 (word 234)))
    else if off=86 then (3,NEG (% rax))
    else if off=89 then (4,SHR (% rax) (Imm8 (word 63)))
    else if off=93 then (2,RET)
    else (0,RET)`;;
let crypto_public_observation = new_definition
 `crypto_public_observation pc off (es:uarch_event list) =
    (word(pc+off):int64,FST(crypto_public_decode off),SND(crypto_public_decode off),es)`;;
let crypto_public_entry5 = new_definition
 `crypto_public_entry5 pc n =
   [crypto_public_observation pc 0 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 4 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 7 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 10 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 14
      [EventJump(word(pc+16),if n=0 then word(pc+93) else word(pc+16))]]`;;
let crypto_public_entry7 = new_definition
 `crypto_public_entry7 pc n = APPEND (crypto_public_entry5 pc n)
   [crypto_public_observation pc 16 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 20
      [EventJump(word(pc+22),if n=16 then word(pc+22) else word(pc+64))]]`;;
let crypto_public_body7 = new_definition
 `crypto_public_body7 pc (p:int64) q count =
   [crypto_public_observation pc 64 [EventLoad(p,1)];
    crypto_public_observation pc 67 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 71 [EventLoad(q,1)];
    crypto_public_observation pc 74 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 78 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 81 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 84
      [EventJump(word(pc+86),if count=1 then word(pc+86) else word(pc+64))]]`;;
let crypto_public_exit = new_definition
 `crypto_public_exit pc (stackpointer:int64) returnaddress =
   [crypto_public_observation pc 86 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 89 [];
    crypto_public_observation pc 93
      [EventLoad(stackpointer,8);EventJump(word(pc+95),returnaddress)]]`;;
let crypto_public_loop = new_recursive_definition num_RECURSION
 `(crypto_public_loop pc (p:int64) q 0 stackpointer returnaddress =
     crypto_public_exit pc stackpointer returnaddress) /\
  (crypto_public_loop pc p q (SUC n) stackpointer returnaddress =
     APPEND (crypto_public_body7 pc p q (SUC n))
       (crypto_public_loop pc (word_add p (word 1)) (word_add q (word 1)) n
         stackpointer returnaddress))`;;
let crypto_public_call = new_definition
 `crypto_public_call pc (p:int64) q n stackpointer returnaddress =
   if n=0 then APPEND (crypto_public_entry5 pc 0)
    [crypto_public_observation pc 93
      [EventLoad(stackpointer,8);EventJump(word(pc+95),returnaddress)]]
   else if n=16 then APPEND (crypto_public_entry7 pc 16)
    [crypto_public_observation pc 22 [EventLoad(p,8)];
     crypto_public_observation pc 25 [EventLoad(word_add p (word 8),8)];
     crypto_public_observation pc 29 [];
     crypto_public_observation pc 36 [EventLoad(q,8)];
     crypto_public_observation pc 39 [EventLoad(word_add q (word 8),8)];
     crypto_public_observation pc 43 [];
     crypto_public_observation pc 46 [];
     crypto_public_observation pc 50
      [EventLoad(stackpointer,8);EventJump(word(pc+52),returnaddress)]]
   else APPEND (crypto_public_entry7 pc n)
     (crypto_public_loop pc p q n stackpointer returnaddress)`;;

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/interface/crypto/trace-statement-v1.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/interface/crypto/trace-statement-v1.ml)

```ocaml
(* Additive fixed-image O2 interface target, frozen before its recipe.
   Neither trace length nor returned equality nor mismatch position is public.
   Memory contents may vary, including initial writable-library data/padding.
   Both original first-RET executions and all nondeterministic choices count. *)
let bp_e2_crypto_public_trace_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw1 padding1 initial_rw2 padding2 stackpointer ra p q n
       m1 m2 rp xp ordinary s1 a1 s2 a2 trace1 trace2 t1 t2.
    bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw1 padding1 stackpointer ra
      p q n m1 rp xp ordinary (s1,a1) /\
    bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw2 padding2 stackpointer ra
      p q n m2 rp xp ordinary (s2,a2) /\
    crypto_first_ret_execution trace1 s1 t1 /\
    crypto_first_ret_execution trace2 s2 t2
    ==> trace1=crypto_public_call 0x10241840 p q n stackpointer ra /\
        trace2=crypto_public_call 0x10241840 p q n stackpointer ra /\
        trace1=trace2 /\
        read RIP t1=ra /\ read RSP t1=word_add stackpointer (word 8) /\
        read RIP t2=ra /\ read RSP t2=word_add stackpointer (word 8)`;;

```


The full-image witness definition binds every mapped PT_LOAD page to the same public Pre. Concrete witnesses include empty, equal, unequal, 16-byte, bytes 128/255, overlap and ordinary page-boundary cases with a complete fourteen-field initial ABI record and ordinary canonical TLS. Separate canonical-edge examples witness the mathematical domain, not Linux user mapping feasibility. The first three immutable segments follow raw-file bytes; the complete originally writable post-initialization segment, including RELRO/BSS, and remaining page padding are explicit stable parameters. Image acquisition, loading and actual page enforcement remain environmental assumptions.


Literal source: [development/approved-plan/loaded-witness/full-binding/definitions-v2.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/loaded-witness/full-binding/definitions-v2.ml)

```ocaml
(* BP-E2 full-image nonvacuity binding V2. Definitions only; freeze before
   proof search. file_bytes is the entire acquired final ELF byte map, whose
   interpretation is checked by an explicitly trusted acquisition boundary.
   The first three originally nonwritable PT_LOAD intervals are immutable.
   The fourth PT_LOAD's entire memory interval is public post-init state,
   INCLUDING its subsequently RELRO pages and initialized BSS. Page padding
   is explicit stable public background. No target behavior is assumed. *)

let fb_immutable = new_definition
 `fb_immutable a <=>
    (0x10000000 <= a /\ a < 0x100c4bd0) \/
    (0x100c5000 <= a /\ a < 0x103434c9) \/
    (0x10344000 <= a /\ a < 0x10421a40)`;;

let fb_initialized_rw = new_definition
 `fb_initialized_rw a <=> 0x10422e50 <= a /\ a < 0x104895b0`;;

let fb_reference_image = new_definition
 `fb_reference_image (file_bytes:num->byte) (initial_rw:int64->byte)
                     (padding:int64->byte) (a:int64) =
    if fb_immutable (val a) then file_bytes (val a-0x10000000)
    else if fb_initialized_rw (val a) then initial_rw a
    else padding a`;;

(* One coherent reference image fixes EVERY byte of EVERY mapped PT_LOAD
   page, not just code or logical segment extents. The conditional clauses
   above expose the precise exception to raw-file agreement. *)
let fb_full_image = new_definition
 `fb_full_image file_bytes initial_rw padding (image:int64->byte) <=>
    !a. lw_image_domain (val(a:int64))
        ==> image a = fb_reference_image file_bytes initial_rw padding a`;;

let fb_file_code = new_definition
 `fb_file_code (file_bytes:num->byte) <=>
    !i. i<95 ==> file_bytes(0x241840+i) = EL i crypto_memcmp_mc`;;

let fb_tls_page = new_definition
 `fb_tls_page a <=> 0x500000 <= a /\ a < 0x501000`;;

let fb_read = new_definition
 `fb_read p q n a <=> lw_read p q n a \/ fb_tls_page a`;;

let fb_ordinary = new_definition
 `fb_ordinary p q n a <=> lw_ordinary p q n a \/ fb_tls_page a`;;

(* This literal record has empty x87 tags, a reset control word, zero x87
   status/opcode/pointers/data, reset MXCSR, zero FS selector with canonical
   FS base at a declared ordinary mapped TLS page, and zero GS state.
   Fourteen field order is exactly state-v4.ml. *)
let fb_initial_abi = new_definition
 `fb_initial_abi = abi_omitted_state_RECORD
    (word 0x37f) (word 0) (word 0xffff) (word 0)
    (word 0) (word 0) (word 0) (word 0)
    (\r:(3)word. word 0) (word 0x1f80)
    (word 0) (word 0x500000) (word 0) (word 0)`;;

(* Expanded initial-state clauses are frozen and audited independently of
   the constructor. These are initial data, never a preservation premise. *)
let fb_abi_initial_fields = new_definition
 `fb_abi_initial_fields (a:abi_omitted_state) <=>
    read ABI_X87_CW a = word 0x37f /\
    read ABI_X87_SW a = word 0 /\
    read ABI_X87_TAG a = word 0xffff /\
    read ABI_X87_FOP a = word 0 /\
    read ABI_X87_IP a = word 0 /\
    read ABI_X87_CS a = word 0 /\
    read ABI_X87_DP a = word 0 /\
    read ABI_X87_DS a = word 0 /\
    (!r:(3)word. read ABI_X87_REGS a r = word 0) /\
    read ABI_MXCSR a = word 0x1f80 /\
    read ABI_FS_SELECTOR a = word 0 /\
    read ABI_FS_BASE a = word 0x500000 /\
    read ABI_GS_SELECTOR a = word 0 /\
    read ABI_GS_BASE a = word 0`;;

let fb_machine = new_definition
 `fb_machine image p q n different =
    (lw_state image p q n different,fb_initial_abi)`;;

(* The same scalar Pre used by all four all-length endpoints is instantiated
   with coherent page permissions extended by TLS. Its complete pair is
   supplied, not an assumed valid_state or preservation fact. *)
let fb_pre = new_definition
 `fb_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding image p q n different <=>
    fb_full_image file_bytes initial_rw padding
      (read memory (FST(fb_machine image p q n different))) /\
    bp_e2_crypto_pre 0x10241840 (word 0x300008) (word 0x400000)
      p q n (lw_memory image p q n different) (fb_read p q n) lw_execute
      (fb_ordinary p q n) (FST(fb_machine image p q n different)) /\
    fb_abi_initial_fields (SND(fb_machine image p q n different)) /\
    (!a. fb_tls_page a ==> fb_read p q n a /\ fb_ordinary p q n a) /\
    assurance_canonical48 (val(read ABI_FS_BASE
      (SND(fb_machine image p q n different))))`;;

```


OPENSSL_cleanse has the same universal length/address domain with a writable destination and no destination-readability premise. Its strict policy permits destination stores only; the only data reads are the incoming return slot. The fixed initial loaded image is bound by the public Pre. Legal clearing may change writable library pages, so no fixed final initialized-RW image is promised. Actual return and preservation outside the designated destination are proved. It does not establish physical erasure, register clearing or removal of other copies.


Literal source: [development/approved-plan/cleanse/spec-v2.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/cleanse/spec-v2.ml)

```ocaml
(* BP-20260930-E2 candidate unchanged OPENSSL_cleanse whole-routine contract.
   Human specification review PENDING. Main functional proof not constructed.
   Readability is required only for the actual incoming return slot. *)
let bp_e2_cleanse_pre = new_definition
 `bp_e2_cleanse_pre pc stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary (s:x86state) <=>
    n<2 EXP 64 /\ pc+97<=2 EXP 64 /\
    bytes_loaded s (word pc) openssl_cleanse_mc /\
    read RIP s=word pc /\ read RSP s=stackpointer /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) s=ra /\
    C_ARGUMENTS [p;word n] s /\ read memory s=m /\
    val(stackpointer:int64) MOD 16=8 /\ val stackpointer+8<2 EXP 64 /\
    assurance_canonical48(val(ra:int64)) /\ ~(ra=word pc) /\
    read DF s=F /\ ~bit 18(read rflags s) /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. xp a /\ ordinary a) pc 97 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) (val stackpointer) 8 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. wp a /\ ordinary a) (val(p:int64)) n /\
    (!a. ~(assurance_region p n a /\ assurance_region (word pc) 97 a)) /\
    (!a. ~(assurance_region p n a /\ assurance_region stackpointer 8 a))`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_read = new_definition
 `bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_read rp ordinary stackpointer a <=>
    rp a /\ ordinary a /\ assurance_region stackpointer 8 a`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_write = new_definition
 `bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_write wp ordinary p n a <=>
    wp a /\ ordinary a /\ assurance_region p n a`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_fetch = new_definition
 `bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_fetch xp ordinary pc a <=>
    xp a /\ ordinary a /\ assurance_region (word pc) 97 a`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_functional_statement =
 `!pc stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary.
    ensures x86
     (bp_e2_cleanse_pre pc stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary)
     (\s. read RIP s=ra /\ val(read RSP s)=val(stackpointer:int64)+8 /\
          (!i. i<n ==> read memory s (word_add p (word i))=word 0) /\
          (!a. ~(assurance_region p n (val a)) ==> read memory s a=m a))
     (MAYCHANGE [RIP;RSP;RAX;RDI;RSI] ,,
      MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,, MAYCHANGE [memory :> bytes(p,n)] ,, MAYCHANGE [events])`;;

(* All-count genuine internal byte-loop component, not full O1. No small cap. *)
let cleanse_byte_loop_statement =
 `!pc p n.
    0<n /\ n<2 EXP 64 /\ nonoverlapping (word pc,97) (p,n) ==>
    ensures x86
     (\s. bytes_loaded s (word pc) openssl_cleanse_mc /\
          read RIP s=word(pc+19) /\ read RDI s=p /\
          read RSI s=word n /\ read RAX s=word 0)
     (\s. read RIP s=word(pc+31) /\ read RDI s=word_add p (word n) /\
          read RSI s=word 0 /\ read RAX s=word 0 /\
          read (memory :> bytes(p,n)) s=0)
     (MAYCHANGE [RIP;RDI;RSI] ,, MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,,
      MAYCHANGE [memory :> bytes(p,n)] ,, MAYCHANGE [events])`;;

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/cleanse/interface/definitions-v1.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/cleanse/interface/definitions-v1.ml)

```ocaml
(* Independently frozen callable interface. The initialized image is bound
   only at entry. A legal destination may overlap writable library pages.
   file_bytes denotes the acquired production ELF through an explicit trusted
   acquisition boundary. The public symbol is load_bias+0x2417d0. *)
let bp_e2_cleanse_call_post = new_definition
 `bp_e2_cleanse_call_post pc stackpointer ra p n m (s:x86state) (t:x86state) <=>
    read RIP t=ra /\ val(read RSP t)=val(stackpointer:int64)+8 /\
    (!i. i<n ==> read memory t (word_add p (word i))=word 0) /\
    (!a. ~assurance_region p n (val a) ==> read memory t a=m a) /\
    bytes_loaded t (word pc) openssl_cleanse_mc /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) t=ra /\
    (MAYCHANGE [RIP;RSP;RAX;RDI;RSI] ,, MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,,
     MAYCHANGE [memory :> bytes(p,n)] ,, MAYCHANGE [events]) s t`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_public_pre = new_definition
 `bp_e2_cleanse_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p n
                          m rp wp xp ordinary (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) <=>
    clw_file_code file_bytes /\ fb_full_image file_bytes initial_rw padding m /\
    bp_e2_cleanse_pre 0x102417d0 stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary s /\
    (!addr. lw_image_domain addr ==> rp addr /\ ordinary addr /\
       (xp addr <=> lw_execute addr) /\ (wp addr <=> lw_image_write addr)) /\
    assurance_canonical48 (val(read ABI_FS_BASE a))`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_public_post = new_definition
 `bp_e2_cleanse_public_post stackpointer ra p n m s a
                           (t:x86state#abi_omitted_state) <=>
    bp_e2_cleanse_call_post 0x102417d0 stackpointer ra p n m s (FST t) /\
    SND t=a /\ abi_required_controls_preserved a (SND t)`;;

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/cleanse/interface/statements-v1.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/cleanse/interface/statements-v1.ml)

```ocaml
(* No callee correctness, permission invariant or preserved-state fact is an
   entry assumption. Each policy step quantifies over every original successor,
   with progress, full data/fetch footprint and the final actual RET. *)
let bp_e2_cleanse_policy_frame_statement =
 `!pc stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary (s:x86state).
    bp_e2_cleanse_pre pc stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary s
    ==> assurance_checked_run (cleanse_functional_steps p n)
      (bp_e2_cleanse_call_post pc stackpointer ra p n m s)
      (\k source target.
        cleanse_access_covered_step pc
          (bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_read rp ordinary stackpointer)
          (bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_write wp ordinary p n)
          (bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_fetch xp ordinary pc) k source target /\
        abi_all_decodes_cleanse_scalar source) s`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_extended_statement =
 `!pc stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary (s:x86state) a.
    bp_e2_cleanse_pre pc stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary s
    ==> eventually_n abi_cleanse_scalar_extended_step (cleanse_functional_steps p n)
      (\t. bp_e2_cleanse_call_post pc stackpointer ra p n m s (FST t) /\
           SND t=a /\ abi_required_controls_preserved a (SND t)) (s,a)`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_public_policy_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
    bp_e2_cleanse_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p n
      m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a)
    ==> assurance_checked_run (cleanse_functional_steps p n)
      (bp_e2_cleanse_call_post 0x102417d0 stackpointer ra p n m s)
      (\k source target.
        cleanse_access_covered_step 0x102417d0
          (bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_read rp ordinary stackpointer)
          (bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_write wp ordinary p n)
          (bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_fetch xp ordinary 0x102417d0) k source target /\
        abi_all_decodes_cleanse_scalar source) s`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_public_interface_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
    bp_e2_cleanse_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p n
      m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a)
    ==> eventually_n abi_cleanse_scalar_extended_step (cleanse_functional_steps p n)
      (bp_e2_cleanse_public_post stackpointer ra p n m s a) (s,a)`;;
let bp_e2_cleanse_public_witness_bridge_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding image p n high_first.
    clw_file_code file_bytes /\
    clw_full_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding image p n high_first
    ==> bp_e2_cleanse_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding
      (word 0x300008) (word 0x400000) p n (clw_memory image p n high_first)
      (clw_read p n) (clw_write p n) lw_execute (clw_ordinary p n)
      (clw_machine image p n high_first)`;;

```


## Coverage

Declared target-region bytes: **1328**. Completed full-domain four-property routine instruction bytes: **165**. CRYPTO binds95declared bytes, reaches83instruction bytes and preserves12padding bytes; cleanse binds97,reaches82,preserves15padding bytes. ## Remaining blockers, failures and trust boundary

No production vulnerability is established. The checked finite-prefix STRICT_BUFFER_ACCESS counterexample remains scoped separately from mapped-page safety. Baseline glibc bytes 0f38f007 at 0x152653 decode to NONE: MOVBE is a model qualification gap. Remaining actual AVX2/BZHI forms need exact-encoding qualification, any justified extension and G1 proof. The frozen selector remains actual entry 0x152320; an easier variant cannot replace it as baseline proof.

The original backend misdecodes actual FF25 PLT targets as32bits, addsRIP and omits the8byte GOT load. The checked blocker is preserved in [results/development/r1-plt-qualification-blocker-v1.json](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/results/development/r1-plt-qualification-blocker-v1.json). A separately versioned E3 generic repair has fresh ISA qualification53objects/7definitions and fresh execution/V6 qualification53objects/41definitions. [results/development/near-indirect-qualification-closed-20260930T175408Z-c5daabb4/receipt.json](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/results/development/near-indirect-qualification-closed-20260930T175408Z-c5daabb4/receipt.json), [results/development/near-indirect-execution-closed-20260930T193434Z-8cf9a0a4/receipt.json](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/results/development/near-indirect-execution-closed-20260930T193434Z-8cf9a0a4/receipt.json) and [development/approved-plan/call-binding/near-indirect-v1/TRUST-E3-v1.md](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/call-binding/near-indirect-v1/TRUST-E3-v1.md) pin scope and architecture justification. Three actual PLT transfers now have functionality/frame, exact one-step termination and universal fetch/GOT-read policy proofs. CALL event order LOAD,JUMP,STORE is the inherited model convention, not physical chronology. Both callee chains and public V6 interfaces have completed fresh E3 requalification; the secret caller has now completed fresh binding/composition, access and ABI theorem-package acceptance through these interfaces. Remaining work includes caller controls3/4, portable caller replay and the glibc/public caller chain. The earlier unnormalized execution-rule tactic failure is retained; a checked normalization adapter resolved it. E2 routine/trace acceptance remains unchanged.

Literal source: [development/approved-plan/glibc-qualification-v2/prefix-definitions-v1.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/glibc-qualification-v2/prefix-definitions-v1.ml)

```ocaml
(* G1 candidate common entry domain. No target-correctness, continuation,
   returned-result or helpful ISA nondeterminism is assumed. *)
let gq2_image_domain = new_definition
 `gq2_image_domain bias a <=> bias<=a /\ a<bias+0x1e2000`;;
let gq2_image_execute = new_definition
 `gq2_image_execute bias a <=> bias+0x26000<=a /\ a<bias+0x17c000`;;
let gq2_image_write = new_definition
 `gq2_image_write bias a <=> bias+0x1d3000<=a /\ a<bias+0x1e2000`;;
let gq2_immutable = new_definition
 `gq2_immutable bias a <=>
    (bias<=a /\ a<bias+0x25388) \/
    (bias+0x26000<=a /\ a<bias+0x17b0fc) \/
    (bias+0x17c000<=a /\ a<bias+0x1cec31)`;;
let gq2_initialized_rw = new_definition
 `gq2_initialized_rw bias a <=> bias+0x1cf8d0<=a /\ a<bias+0x1e1f50`;;
let gq2_reference_image = new_definition
 `gq2_reference_image bias (file_bytes:num->byte) (initial_rw:int64->byte)
                      (padding:int64->byte) (a:int64) =
    if gq2_immutable bias (val a) then file_bytes(val a-bias)
    else if gq2_initialized_rw bias (val a) then initial_rw a
    else padding a`;;
let gq2_full_image = new_definition
 `gq2_full_image bias file_bytes initial_rw padding (m:int64->byte) <=>
    !a. gq2_image_domain bias (val a)
        ==> m a=gq2_reference_image bias file_bytes initial_rw padding a`;;
let gq2_pagecover = new_definition
 `gq2_pagecover (p:int64) n a <=>
    ~(n=0) /\ 4096*(val p DIV 4096)<=a /\
    a<4096*((val p+n-1) DIV 4096+1)`;;
let gq2_profile = new_definition
 `gq2_profile (cpuid:num->num->num#num#num#num) osxsave xcr0 cet granule <=>
    osxsave /\ xcr0=7 /\ ~cet /\ granule=4096 /\
    cpuid 0 0=(13,1970169159,1818588270,1231384169) /\
    cpuid 1 0=(132800,67584,2128097807,260803349) /\
    cpuid 7 0=(0,296,0,0) /\ cpuid 7 1=(0,296,0,0) /\
    cpuid 13 0=(7,832,832,0) /\ cpuid 13 1=(0,0,0,0) /\
    cpuid 2147483648 0=(2147483656,0,0,0) /\
    cpuid 2147483649 0=(0,0,33,672139264)`;;
let gq2_file_code = new_definition
 `gq2_file_code (file_bytes:num->byte) <=>
    !i. i<1023 ==> file_bytes(0x152320+i)=EL i gq2_memcmp_mc`;;
let gq2_g1_pre = new_definition
 `gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
    stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) <=>
    gq2_profile cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule /\
    bias MOD 4096=0 /\ bias+0x1e2000<=2 EXP 64 /\
    gq2_file_code file_bytes /\ gq2_full_image bias file_bytes initial_rw padding m /\
    n<2 EXP 64 /\ val(p:int64)+n<=2 EXP 64 /\ val(q:int64)+n<=2 EXP 64 /\
    val(stackpointer:int64) MOD 16=8 /\ val stackpointer+8<2 EXP 64 /\
    assurance_canonical48(val(ra:int64)) /\ ~(ra=word(bias+0x152320)) /\
    read RIP s=word(bias+0x152320) /\ read RSP s=stackpointer /\
    C_ARGUMENTS [p;q;word n] s /\ read memory s=m /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) s=ra /\
    bytes_loaded s (word(bias+0x152320)) gq2_memcmp_mc /\
    read DF s=F /\ ~bit 18(read rflags s) /\
    assurance_canonical48(val(read ABI_FS_BASE a)) /\
    assurance_span_ok (\x. rp x /\ ordinary x) (val stackpointer) 8 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\x. xp x /\ ordinary x) (bias+0x152320) 1023 /\
    (!x. ~(assurance_region stackpointer 8 x /\
            assurance_region (word(bias+0x152320)) 1023 x)) /\
    (!x. gq2_pagecover p n x \/ gq2_pagecover q n x
         ==> assurance_canonical48 x /\ rp x /\ ordinary x) /\
    (!x. gq2_image_domain bias x ==>
       assurance_canonical48 x /\ rp x /\ ordinary x /\
       (xp x <=> gq2_image_execute bias x) /\
       (wp x <=> gq2_image_write bias x))`;;
let gq2_strict_read = new_definition
 `gq2_strict_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n x <=>
    rp x /\ ordinary x /\
    (assurance_region p n x \/ assurance_region q n x \/
     assurance_region stackpointer 8 x)`;;
let gq2_mapped_read = new_definition
 `gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n x <=>
    rp x /\ ordinary x /\
    (gq2_pagecover p n x \/ gq2_pagecover q n x \/
     assurance_region stackpointer 8 x)`;;
let gq2_allowed_fetch = new_definition
 `gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary x <=>
    xp x /\ ordinary x /\ assurance_region (word(bias+0x152320)) 1023 x`;;
(* Only pre-RET transitions: this property does not require a continuation or
   depend on existence of an execution completing past unsupported opcodes. *)
let gq2_before_ret_prefix = new_recursive_definition num_RECURSION
 `(gq2_before_ret_prefix 0 (s:x86state) t <=> t=s) /\
  (gq2_before_ret_prefix (SUC k) s t <=>
    ?u. x86 s u /\ ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len s u) /\
        gq2_before_ret_prefix k u t)`;;
let gq2_strict_prefix_property = new_definition
 `gq2_strict_prefix_property bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave
    xcr0 cet granule stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary a <=>
    !s k t.
    gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
      stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a) /\
    gq2_before_ret_prefix k s t
    ==> ALL (assurance_event_policy
          (gq2_strict_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n) (\x.F))
          (assurance_delta s t)`;;
let gq2_small_events = new_definition
 `gq2_small_events pc e = APPEND
   [EventLoad(word 0x210100,32);
    EventJump(word(pc+0x2f5),word(pc+0x2f5));
    EventJump(word(pc+0x2e5),word(pc+0x2e5));
    EventJump(word(pc+10),word(pc+0x2e0))] e`;;

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/glibc-qualification-v2/prefix-step-definitions-v2.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/glibc-qualification-v2/prefix-step-definitions-v2.ml)

```ocaml
let gq2_prefix_step_v2 = new_definition
 `gq2_prefix_step_v2 bias rp xp ordinary (remaining:num) source target <=>
    assurance_step_policy
      (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary (word 0x300008)
         (word 0x200100) (word 0x210100) 2) (\x.F)
      (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
    (?off len inst. gq2_prefix_site off len inst /\
       read RIP source=word(bias+0x152320+off) /\
       x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
    ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target)`;;

```



Literal source: [development/approved-plan/glibc-qualification-v2/prefix-statements-v2.ml](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/glibc-qualification-v2/prefix-statements-v2.ml)

```ocaml
(* Prefix effects/footprint terms frozen before proof search. *)
let gq2_small_prefix_statement =
 `!pc m e.
    ensures_n x86
      (\s. bytes_loaded s (word pc) gq2_entry_mc /\
           bytes_loaded s (word(pc+0x2e0)) gq2_small_mc /\
           read RIP s=word pc /\ read RDI s=word 0x200100 /\
           read RSI s=word 0x210100 /\ read RDX s=word 2 /\
           read memory s=m /\ read events s=e)
      (\t. read RIP t=word(pc+0x2f9) /\ read memory t=m /\
           read events t=gq2_small_events pc e)
      (MAYCHANGE[RIP;RAX] ,, MAYCHANGE[ZMM2] ,, MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,,
       MAYCHANGE[events]) (\s.10)`;;
let gq2_vpmovmskb_128_component_statement =
 `!r s. OPERAND256 (Simdregister(Simdreg r Lower_128)) s = ARB`;;
let gq2_vpmovmskb_128_executor_statement =
 `!r q s.
    x86_execute (VPMOVMSKB (Register(Gpr r Lower_32))
                  (Simdregister(Simdreg q Lower_128))) s =
    x86_VPMOVMSKB (OPERAND32 (Register(Gpr r Lower_32)) s) ARB s`;;
let gq2_strict_load_failure_statement =
 `!rp ordinary.
    ~assurance_event_policy
      (gq2_strict_read rp ordinary (word 0x300008)
         (word 0x200100) (word 0x210100) 2) (\x.F)
      (EventLoad(word 0x210100,32))`;;
let gq2_same_pre_witness_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding.
    gq2_file_code file_bytes ==>
    gq2_g1_pre 0x40000000 file_bytes initial_rw padding
      gq2_observed_cpuid T 7 F 4096
      (word 0x300008) (word 0x400000) (word 0x200100) (word 0x210100) 2
      (gq2_witness_memory file_bytes initial_rw padding)
      gq2_witness_read gq2_witness_write gq2_witness_execute gq2_witness_read
      (gq2_witness_state file_bytes initial_rw padding,gq2_witness_abi) /\
    gq2_witness_memory file_bytes initial_rw padding (word 0x200100)=word 255 /\
    gq2_witness_memory file_bytes initial_rw padding (word 0x200101)=word 128 /\
    gq2_witness_memory file_bytes initial_rw padding (word 0x210100)=word 255 /\
    gq2_witness_memory file_bytes initial_rw padding (word 0x210101)=word 129 /\
    gq2_witness_memory file_bytes initial_rw padding (word 0x210102)=word 165`;;
let gq2_same_pre_strict_counterexample_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding.
    gq2_file_code file_bytes ==>
    ~gq2_strict_prefix_property 0x40000000 file_bytes initial_rw padding
      gq2_observed_cpuid T 7 F 4096
      (word 0x300008) (word 0x400000) (word 0x200100) (word 0x210100) 2
      (gq2_witness_memory file_bytes initial_rw padding)
      gq2_witness_read gq2_witness_write gq2_witness_execute gq2_witness_read
      gq2_witness_abi`;;
```

```ocaml
(* E3-only goals, frozen before proof search. Both E2 public domains and
   endpoint predicates remain unchanged. Candidate5 supplies the only x86
   constants in this checking namespace. No caller composition is assumed. *)
let e3_scalar_qualifies_caller_statement =
 `!s:x86state. abi_all_decodes_scalar s ==> abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar s`;;
let e3_cleanse_qualifies_caller_statement =
 `!s:x86state. abi_all_decodes_cleanse_scalar s ==> abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar s`;;
let e3_run_mono_step_statement =
 `!n P C D (s:x86state).
   (!k source target. C k source target ==> D k source target) /\
   assurance_checked_run n P C s
   ==> assurance_checked_run n P D s`;;
let bp_e3_crypto_public_caller_policy_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp xp ordinary s a.
   bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n
     m rp xp ordinary (s,a)
   ==> assurance_checked_run (bp_assurance_call_steps n)
     (bp_assurance_call_post 0x10241840 stackpointer ra p q n
       (read events s) m s)
     (\k source target.
       assurance_zero_step
         (bp_e2_crypto_allowed_read rp ordinary p q n stackpointer)
         bp_e2_crypto_allowed_write
         (bp_e2_crypto_allowed_fetch xp ordinary 0x10241840)
         k source target /\ abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar source) s`;;
let bp_e3_crypto_public_caller_interface_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp xp ordinary s a.
   bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p q n
     m rp xp ordinary (s,a)
   ==> eventually_n abi_caller_scalar_extended_step (bp_assurance_call_steps n)
     (bp_e2_crypto_public_post file_bytes initial_rw padding
       stackpointer ra p q n (read events s) m s a) (s,a)`;;
let bp_e3_cleanse_public_caller_policy_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
   bp_e2_cleanse_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p n
     m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a)
   ==> assurance_checked_run (cleanse_functional_steps p n)
     (bp_e2_cleanse_call_post 0x102417d0 stackpointer ra p n m s)
     (\k source target.
       cleanse_access_covered_step 0x102417d0
         (bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_read rp ordinary stackpointer)
         (bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_write wp ordinary p n)
         (bp_e2_cleanse_allowed_fetch xp ordinary 0x102417d0)
         k source target /\ abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar source) s`;;
let bp_e3_cleanse_public_caller_interface_statement =
 `!file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
   bp_e2_cleanse_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw padding stackpointer ra p n
     m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a)
   ==> eventually_n abi_caller_scalar_extended_step (cleanse_functional_steps p n)
     (bp_e2_cleanse_public_post stackpointer ra p n m s a) (s,a)`;;

```
