## Completed E3 compiled secret caller

Ten original literal goals and all 45 expanded definitions are frozen independently of live checker outputs. The full 125-object helper closure is in the digest-pinned expected registry. Human specification review remains PENDING. The caller uses the same fixed post-initialization library/client images; loader execution is assumed. Its public Pre permits all admissible machine lengths, overlap, misalignment and zero length; it excludes input/scratch overlap and protects the incoming return slot. Scratch is exactly 32 bytes below the incoming stack pointer. Post compares initial bytes and permits writes only in the secret region or that scratch footprint; no caller trace result is asserted.

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_CHECKED

```text
!lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw client_padding
    stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
    r1_secret_client_pre lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw
      client_padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a)
    ==> assurance_checked_run (r1_secret_client_steps p n)
      (r1_secret_client_scalar_post stackpointer ra p q n m s)
      (r1_secret_client_step_policy stackpointer p q n rp wp xp ordinary) s
```

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_INTERFACE

```text
!lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw client_padding
    stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
    r1_secret_client_pre lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw
      client_padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a)
    ==> eventually_n abi_caller_scalar_extended_step (r1_secret_client_steps p n)
      (r1_secret_client_public_post stackpointer ra p q n m s a) (s,a)
```

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_BOUNDED

```text
!lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw client_padding
    stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
    r1_secret_client_pre lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw
      client_padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a)
    ==> ensures_n x86 (\u. u=s)
      (r1_secret_client_scalar_post stackpointer ra p q n m s)
      (r1_secret_client_frame stackpointer p n) (\u. r1_secret_client_steps p n)
```

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_COUNT

```text
!p n. r1_secret_client_steps p n =
    19 + (if n=0 then 6 else if n=16 then 15 else 7*n+10) +
    (if n<15 then 4*n+7 else
       let a=(8-val(p:int64) MOD 8) MOD 8 in
       9+6*a+5*((n-a) DIV 8)+4*((n-a) MOD 8))
```

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_STACK_ALIGNMENT

```text
!stackpointer:int64.
    32<=val stackpointer /\ val stackpointer MOD 16=8
    ==> val(word_sub stackpointer (word 32):int64)=val stackpointer-32 /\
        val(word_sub stackpointer (word 32):int64) MOD 16=8
```

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_CRYPTO_HANDOFF

```text
!lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw client_padding
    stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
    r1_secret_client_pre lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw
      client_padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a)
    ==> assurance_checked_run 7
      (r1_secret_client_crypto_ready lib_file stackpointer p q n m
        rp wp xp ordinary s a)
      (r1_secret_client_segment_policy stackpointer p q n rp wp xp ordinary) s
```

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_CLEANSE_HANDOFF

```text
!lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw client_padding
    stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
    r1_secret_client_pre lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw
      client_padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a)
    ==> assurance_checked_run (12 + bp_assurance_call_steps n)
      (r1_secret_client_cleanse_ready lib_file stackpointer p q n m
        rp wp xp ordinary s a)
      (r1_secret_client_segment_policy stackpointer p q n rp wp xp ordinary) s
```

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_LIBRARY_REBIND

```text
!file_bytes initial_rw padding m m'.
    fb_full_image file_bytes initial_rw padding m /\
    (!addr. fb_immutable (val addr) ==> m' addr=m addr)
    ==> fb_full_image file_bytes m' m' m'
```

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_CLIENT_REBIND

```text
!file_bytes initial_rw padding m m'.
    r1_secret_client_full_image file_bytes initial_rw padding m /\
    (!addr. r1_secret_client_immutable (val addr) ==> m' addr=m addr)
    ==> r1_secret_client_full_image file_bytes m' m' m'
```

### R1_SECRET_CLIENT_WITNESSES

```text
!lib_file client_file.
    fb_file_code lib_file /\ clw_file_code lib_file /\
    r1_secret_client_file_code client_file
    ==> r1_secret_client_witness lib_file client_file (word 0) (word 0) 0 F /\
        r1_secret_client_witness lib_file client_file
          (word 0x50000000) (word 0x60000000) 1 F /\
        r1_secret_client_witness lib_file client_file
          (word 0x50000003) (word 0x60000005) 17 T /\
        r1_secret_client_witness lib_file client_file
          (word 0x50000003) (word 0x50000003) 16 F /\
        r1_secret_client_witness lib_file client_file
          (word 0x50000003) (word 0x50000004) 17 F /\
        r1_secret_client_witness lib_file client_file
          (word 0x50000fff) (word 0x60000fff) 15 T /\
        r1_secret_client_witness lib_file client_file
          (word 0x7fffffffffff) (word 0xffff800000000000) 1 T
```

### All expanded caller definitions

**assurance_zero_step**

```text
assurance_zero_step rp wp xp remaining s s' <=>
   assurance_step_policy rp wp xp s s' /\
   (remaining = 1 ==> assurance_actual_ret 2 s s')
```

**cleanse_access_covered_step**

```text
cleanse_access_covered_step pc rp wp xp remaining (s:x86state) s' <=>
    assurance_zero_step rp wp xp remaining s s' /\
    (?off len inst.
       read RIP s=word(pc+off) /\ abi_cleanse_site off len inst /\
       x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst))
```

**clw_file_code**

```text
clw_file_code (file_bytes:num->byte) <=>
    !i. i<97 ==> file_bytes(0x2417d0+i)=EL i openssl_cleanse_mc
```

**fb_initial_abi**

```text
fb_initial_abi = abi_omitted_state_RECORD
    (word 0x37f) (word 0) (word 0xffff) (word 0)
    (word 0) (word 0) (word 0) (word 0)
    (\r:(3)word. word 0) (word 0x1f80)
    (word 0) (word 0x500000) (word 0) (word 0)
```

**r1_secret_client_accumulated_frame**

```text
r1_secret_client_accumulated_frame stackpointer p n =
 MAYCHANGE [RIP;RSP;RAX;RDI;RSI;RDX;R10;R11;RBX;RBP;R12] ,,
 MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,,
 MAYCHANGE [memory :> bytes(p,n);memory :> bytes(word_sub stackpointer (word 32),32)] ,,
 MAYCHANGE [events]
```

**r1_secret_client_allowed_fetch**

```text
r1_secret_client_allowed_fetch xp ordinary a <=>
    xp a /\ ordinary a /\
    (assurance_region (word 0x20001150) 42 a \/
     assurance_region (word 0x20001030) 6 a \/
     assurance_region (word 0x20001040) 6 a \/
     assurance_region (word 0x10241840) 95 a \/
     assurance_region (word 0x102417d0) 97 a)
```

**r1_secret_client_allowed_read**

```text
r1_secret_client_allowed_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n a <=>
    rp a /\ ordinary a /\
    (assurance_region p n a \/ assurance_region q n a \/
     r1_secret_client_scratch stackpointer a \/
     assurance_region stackpointer 8 a \/
     assurance_region (word 0x20003fc8) 8 a \/
     assurance_region (word 0x20003fd0) 8 a)
```

**r1_secret_client_allowed_write**

```text
r1_secret_client_allowed_write wp ordinary stackpointer p n a <=>
    wp a /\ ordinary a /\
    (assurance_region p n a \/ r1_secret_client_scratch stackpointer a)
```

**r1_secret_client_cleanse_entered**

```text
r1_secret_client_cleanse_entered lib_file stackpointer p q n m rp wp xp ordinary
 (initial:x86state) a (entered:x86state) <=>
 ?returned:x86state.
   r1_secret_client_crypto_returned lib_file stackpointer p q n m rp wp xp ordinary initial a returned /\
   r1_secret_client_cleanse_ready lib_file stackpointer p q n m rp wp xp ordinary initial a entered /\
   r1_secret_client_middle_frame stackpointer returned entered
```

**r1_secret_client_cleanse_ready**

```text
r1_secret_client_cleanse_ready lib_file stackpointer p q n m rp wp xp
    ordinary (s:x86state) a (t:x86state) <=>
    bp_e2_cleanse_public_pre lib_file (read memory t) (read memory t)
      (word_sub stackpointer (word 32)) (word 0x2000116d) p n
      (read memory t) rp wp xp ordinary (t,a) /\
    r1_secret_client_saved_prefix stackpointer p q n m s t /\
    read R12 t=(if (!i. i<n ==> m(word_add p (word i))=
                                  m(word_add q (word i)))
                then word 0 else word 1)
```

**r1_secret_client_cleanse_returned**

```text
r1_secret_client_cleanse_returned lib_file stackpointer p q n m rp wp xp ordinary
 (initial:x86state) a (returned:x86state) <=>
 ?entry:x86state.
   r1_secret_client_cleanse_entered lib_file stackpointer p q n m rp wp xp ordinary initial a entry /\
   bp_e2_cleanse_call_post 0x102417d0 (word_sub stackpointer (word 32))
     (word 0x2000116d) p n (read memory entry) entry returned
```

**r1_secret_client_crypto_ready**

```text
r1_secret_client_crypto_ready lib_file stackpointer p q n m rp wp xp
    ordinary (s:x86state) a (t:x86state) <=>
    bp_e2_crypto_public_pre lib_file (read memory t) (read memory t)
      (word_sub stackpointer (word 32)) (word 0x2000115f) p q n
      (read memory t) rp xp ordinary (t,a) /\
    r1_secret_client_saved_prefix stackpointer p q n m s t /\
    read R12 t=read R12 s
```

**r1_secret_client_crypto_returned**

```text
r1_secret_client_crypto_returned lib_file stackpointer p q n m rp wp xp ordinary
    (initial:x86state) a (returned:x86state) <=>
    ?entry:x86state.
      r1_secret_client_crypto_ready lib_file stackpointer p q n m rp wp xp ordinary initial a entry /\

        (word 0x2000115f) p q n (read events entry) (read memory entry) entry returned
```

```text
r1_secret_client_file_code (file_bytes:num->byte) <=>
    (!i. i<42 ==> file_bytes(0x1150+i)=EL i r1_secret_caller_mc) /\

    (!i. i<6 ==> file_bytes(0x1040+i)=EL i r1_cleanse_plt_prefix)
```

**r1_secret_client_frame**

```text

    MAYCHANGE [RIP;RSP;RAX;RDI;RSI;RDX;R10;R11] ,,
    MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,,
    MAYCHANGE [memory :> bytes(p,n);
               memory :> bytes(word_sub stackpointer (word 32),32)] ,,
```


**r1_secret_client_got**

```text
r1_secret_client_got (s:x86state) <=>
    read (memory :> bytes64 (word 0x20003fc8)) s=word 0x10241840 /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 (word 0x20003fd0)) s=word 0x102417d0
```

**r1_secret_client_image_domain**

```text
r1_secret_client_image_domain a <=> 0x20000000 <= a /\ a < 0x20005000
```

**r1_secret_client_image_execute**

```text
r1_secret_client_image_execute a <=> 0x20001000 <= a /\ a < 0x20002000
```

**r1_secret_client_image_write**

```text
r1_secret_client_image_write a <=> 0x20004000 <= a /\ a < 0x20005000
```

**r1_secret_client_immutable**

```text
r1_secret_client_immutable a <=>
    (0x20000000 <= a /\ a < 0x200005a8) \/
    (0x20001000 <= a /\ a < 0x20001185) \/
    (0x20002000 <= a /\ a < 0x200020d0)
```

**r1_secret_client_initialized_rw**

```text
r1_secret_client_initialized_rw a <=> 0x20003da0 <= a /\ a < 0x20004010
```

**r1_secret_client_loaded_code**

```text
r1_secret_client_loaded_code (s:x86state) <=>
    bytes_loaded s (word 0x20001150) r1_secret_caller_mc /\
    bytes_loaded s (word 0x20001030) r1_crypto_plt_prefix /\
    bytes_loaded s (word 0x20001040) r1_cleanse_plt_prefix /\
    bytes_loaded s (word 0x10241840) crypto_memcmp_mc /\
    bytes_loaded s (word 0x102417d0) openssl_cleanse_mc
```

**r1_secret_client_middle_frame**

```text
r1_secret_client_middle_frame stackpointer =
 MAYCHANGE [RIP;RSP;RSI;RDI;R12] ,,
 MAYCHANGE [memory :> bytes(word_sub stackpointer (word 32),32)] ,,
 MAYCHANGE [events]
```

**r1_secret_client_pre**

```text
r1_secret_client_pre lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw
    client_padding stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary
    (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) <=>
    n<2 EXP 64 /\ 32<=val(stackpointer:int64) /\
    val stackpointer MOD 16=8 /\ val stackpointer+8<2 EXP 64 /\
    assurance_canonical48(val(ra:int64)) /\ ~(ra=word 0x20001150) /\
    read RIP s=word 0x20001150 /\ read RSP s=stackpointer /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) s=ra /\
    C_ARGUMENTS [p;q;word n] s /\ read memory s=m /\
    read DF s=F /\ ~bit 18(read rflags s) /\
    assurance_canonical48(val(read ABI_FS_BASE a)) /\
    fb_file_code lib_file /\ clw_file_code lib_file /\
    fb_full_image lib_file lib_rw lib_padding m /\
    r1_secret_client_file_code client_file /\
    r1_secret_client_full_image client_file client_rw client_padding m /\
    r1_secret_client_loaded_code s /\ r1_secret_client_got s /\
    (!addr. lw_image_domain addr ==>
       rp addr /\ ordinary addr /\ (xp addr <=> lw_execute addr) /\
       (wp addr <=> lw_image_write addr)) /\
    (!addr. r1_secret_client_image_domain addr ==>
       rp addr /\ ordinary addr /\
       (xp addr <=> r1_secret_client_image_execute addr) /\
       (wp addr <=> r1_secret_client_image_write addr)) /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) (val p) n /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) (val q) n /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. wp a /\ ordinary a) (val p) n /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) (val stackpointer-32) 32 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. wp a /\ ordinary a) (val stackpointer-32) 32 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) (val stackpointer) 8 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) 0x20003fc8 8 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. rp a /\ ordinary a) 0x20003fd0 8 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. xp a /\ ordinary a) 0x20001150 42 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. xp a /\ ordinary a) 0x20001030 6 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. xp a /\ ordinary a) 0x20001040 6 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. xp a /\ ordinary a) 0x10241840 95 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\a. xp a /\ ordinary a) 0x102417d0 97 /\
    (!addr. ~(assurance_region p n addr /\
               r1_secret_client_scratch stackpointer addr)) /\
    (!addr. ~(assurance_region q n addr /\
               r1_secret_client_scratch stackpointer addr)) /\
    (!addr. ~(assurance_region p n addr /\
               assurance_region stackpointer 8 addr))
```

**r1_secret_client_prefix_frame**

```text
r1_secret_client_prefix_frame stackpointer =
    MAYCHANGE [RIP;RSP;RBX;RBP] ,,
    MAYCHANGE [memory :> bytes(word_sub stackpointer (word 32),32)] ,,
    MAYCHANGE [events]
```

**r1_secret_client_public_post**

```text
r1_secret_client_public_post stackpointer ra p q n m s a
    (t:x86state#abi_omitted_state) <=>
    r1_secret_client_scalar_post stackpointer ra p q n m s (FST t) /\
    SND t=a /\ abi_required_controls_preserved a (SND t)
```

**r1_secret_client_reference_image**

```text
r1_secret_client_reference_image (file_bytes:num->byte)
     (initial_rw:int64->byte) (padding:int64->byte) (a:int64) =
    if r1_secret_client_immutable (val a)
    then file_bytes (val a - 0x20000000)
    else if r1_secret_client_initialized_rw (val a) then initial_rw a
    else padding a
```

**r1_secret_client_saved_prefix**

```text
r1_secret_client_saved_prefix stackpointer p q n m (s:x86state) t <=>
    read RBX t=p /\ read RBP t=word n /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 (word_sub stackpointer (word 8))) t=read R12 s /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 (word_sub stackpointer (word 16))) t=read RBP s /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 (word_sub stackpointer (word 24))) t=read RBX s /\
    (!addr. ~r1_secret_client_scratch stackpointer (val addr)
            ==> read memory t addr=m addr) /\
    (!i. i<n ==> read memory t (word_add p (word i))=m(word_add p (word i))) /\
    (!i. i<n ==> read memory t (word_add q (word i))=m(word_add q (word i))) /\
    r1_secret_client_loaded_code t /\ r1_secret_client_got t
```

**r1_secret_client_scalar_post**

```text
r1_secret_client_scalar_post stackpointer ra p q n m (s:x86state) t <=>
    read RIP t=ra /\ val(read RSP t)=val(stackpointer:int64)+8 /\
    read RAX t=(if (!i. i<n ==> m(word_add p (word i))=
                                  m(word_add q (word i)))
                then word 1 else word 0) /\
    (!i. i<n ==> read memory t (word_add p (word i))=word 0) /\
    (!addr. ~(assurance_region p n (val addr) \/
               r1_secret_client_scratch stackpointer (val addr))
            ==> read memory t addr=m addr) /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) t=ra /\
    read RBX t=read RBX s /\ read RBP t=read RBP s /\
    read R12 t=read R12 s /\ read R13 t=read R13 s /\
    read R14 t=read R14 s /\ read R15 t=read R15 s /\
    read DF t=F /\ r1_secret_client_loaded_code t /\ r1_secret_client_got t /\
    r1_secret_client_frame stackpointer p n s t
```

**r1_secret_client_scratch**

```text
r1_secret_client_scratch (stackpointer:int64) a <=>
    assurance_region (word_sub stackpointer (word 32)) 32 a
```

**r1_secret_client_segment_policy**

```text
r1_secret_client_segment_policy stackpointer p q n rp wp xp ordinary
    remaining source target <=>
    assurance_step_policy
      (r1_secret_client_allowed_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
      (r1_secret_client_allowed_write wp ordinary stackpointer p n)
      (r1_secret_client_allowed_fetch xp ordinary) source target /\
    abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar source
```

**r1_secret_client_step_policy**

```text
r1_secret_client_step_policy stackpointer p q n rp wp xp ordinary
    remaining source target <=>
    assurance_step_policy
      (r1_secret_client_allowed_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
      (r1_secret_client_allowed_write wp ordinary stackpointer p n)
      (r1_secret_client_allowed_fetch xp ordinary) source target /\
    (remaining=1 ==> assurance_actual_ret 1 source target) /\
    abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar source
```

**r1_secret_client_steps**

```text
r1_secret_client_steps p n =
    19 + bp_assurance_call_steps n + cleanse_functional_steps p n
```

**r1_secret_client_suffix_ready**

```text
r1_secret_client_suffix_ready stackpointer ra p q n m (initial:x86state) (returned:x86state) <=>
 read RIP returned=word 0x2000116d /\ read RSP returned=word_sub stackpointer (word 24) /\
 read R12 returned=(if (!i. i<n ==> m(word_add p (word i))=m(word_add q (word i))) then word 0 else word 1) /\
 read RBX returned=p /\ read RBP returned=word n /\
 read (memory :> bytes64 (word_sub stackpointer (word 8))) returned=read R12 initial /\
 read (memory :> bytes64 (word_sub stackpointer (word 16))) returned=read RBP initial /\
 read (memory :> bytes64 (word_sub stackpointer (word 24))) returned=read RBX initial /\
 read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) returned=ra /\
 (!i. i<n ==> read memory returned (word_add p (word i))=word 0) /\
 (!addr. ~(assurance_region p n (val addr) \/ r1_secret_client_scratch stackpointer (val addr))
   ==> read memory returned addr=m addr) /\
 read R13 returned=read R13 initial /\ read R14 returned=read R14 initial /\ read R15 returned=read R15 initial /\
 read DF returned=F /\ ~bit 18(read rflags returned) /\
 r1_secret_client_loaded_code returned /\ r1_secret_client_got returned /\
 r1_secret_client_accumulated_frame stackpointer p n initial returned
```

**r1_secret_client_witness**

```text
r1_secret_client_witness lib_file client_file p q n different <=>
    ?lib_rw lib_padding client_rw client_padding m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
      r1_secret_client_pre lib_file lib_rw lib_padding client_file client_rw
        client_padding (word 0x300008) (word 0x400000) p q n m
        rp wp xp ordinary (s,a) /\
      (!i. i<n ==> m(word_add p (word i))=
          (if different /\ i=0 then word 255 else word 128)) /\
      (!i. i<n ==> m(word_add q (word i))=word 128)
```

**rsw_execute**

```text
rsw_execute a <=> lw_execute a \/ r1_secret_client_image_execute a
```

**rsw_external**

```text
rsw_external a <=> 0x50000000 <= a /\ assurance_canonical48 a
```

**rsw_layout**

```text
rsw_layout (p:int64) (q:int64) n different <=>
    n < 2 EXP 64 /\
    assurance_span_ok rsw_external (val p) n /\
    assurance_span_ok rsw_external (val q) n /\
    (different ==> !i. i<n ==> ~(word_add q (word i)=p))
```

**rsw_memory**

```text
rsw_memory lib_file client_file p different =
    write (bytes64 (word 0x300008)) (word 0x400000)
      (write (bytes64 (word 0x20003fc8)) (word 0x10241840)
        (write (bytes64 (word 0x20003fd0)) (word 0x102417d0)
          (rsw_seed lib_file client_file p different)))
```

**rsw_ordinary**

```text
rsw_ordinary a <=> assurance_canonical48 a
```

**rsw_read**

```text
rsw_read a <=> assurance_canonical48 a
```

**rsw_seed**

```text
rsw_seed (lib_file:num->byte) (client_file:num->byte)
    (p:int64) different (addr:int64) =
    if fb_immutable (val addr) then lib_file(val addr-0x10000000)
    else if r1_secret_client_immutable (val addr)
         then client_file(val addr-0x20000000)
    else if different /\ addr=p then word 255 else word 128
```

**rsw_state**

```text
rsw_state lib_file client_file p q n different =
    x86state_RECORD (word 0x20001150)
      (\j:(4)word. if j=word 7 then p else if j=word 6 then q
                    else if j=word 2 then word n
                    else if j=word 4 then word 0x300008 else word 0)
      (\j:(5)word. word 0) (\j:(3)word. word 0) (word 0)
      (rsw_memory lib_file client_file p different) []
```

**rsw_write**

```text
rsw_write a <=>
    if lw_image_domain a then lw_image_write a
    else if r1_secret_client_image_domain a
         then r1_secret_client_image_write a
    else assurance_canonical48 a
```

## E4 vector-byte comparison qualification lemma

The unchanged candidate model now has one additional original qualification goal checked. This generic mathematical lemma covers 128- and 256-bit lane results; it adds no verified production instruction bytes and establishes no whole-function G1, access policy, frame package or hardware correspondence. The earlier 247-check/46-binding checkpoint remains unchanged. [Independent fresh component acceptance](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/results/development/glibc-e4-byte-spec-root-acceptance-v1.json) [Retained failed and successful attempts](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/glibc-e4-byte-spec-v1/attempt-ledger-v2.json)

### E4_VPCMPEQB_BYTE_SPEC

```text
!dest src1 src2 (s:x86state).
  dimindex(:N) = 128 \/ dimindex(:N) = 256
  ==> x86_VPCMPEQB dest src1 src2 s =
      (dest := (word(nsum (0..dimindex(:N) DIV 8 - 1)
        (\i. (if (word_subword (read src1 s:N word) (8*i,8):byte) =
                    word_subword (read src2 s:N word) (8*i,8)
              then 255 else 0) * 2 EXP (8*i))):N word)) s
```

## E4 restricted actual memcmp prefix through VPCMPEQB

The final artifact is unchanged libc SHA-256 `6b4a45352fd0c540a9c7c718f35ce8c8e46a4e482f9d3885a910c32d1a0e1421`. The 1,023-byte region starts at ELF VA/file offset 0x152320. Eleven transitions cover 39 unique instruction bytes; the last four bytes are c5ed7417 at 0x152619. The domain is the frozen original length-two, fixed-pointer gq2_g1_pre, not the all-length G1 contract. All successors, local mapped access and control policy are checked through PC=bias+0x152320+765. The prefix does not reach RET. Its original RF/TF-unrestricted E4 model interpretation is retained; the new vector normal-entry refinement remains unproved. [Fresh root acceptance and independent extraction](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/results/development/glibc-e4-prefix11-root-acceptance-v1.json)

### GQ2_SMALL_PREFIX

```text
!pc m e.
    ensures_n x86
      (\s. bytes_loaded s (word pc) gq2_entry_mc /\
           bytes_loaded s (word(pc+0x2e0)) gq2_small_mc /\
           read RIP s=word pc /\ read RDI s=word 0x200100 /\
           read RSI s=word 0x210100 /\ read RDX s=word 2 /\
           read memory s=m /\ read events s=e)
      (\t. read RIP t=word(pc+0x2f9) /\ read memory t=m /\
           read events t=gq2_small_events pc e)
      (MAYCHANGE[RIP;RAX] ,, MAYCHANGE[ZMM2] ,, MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,,
       MAYCHANGE[events]) (\s.10)
```

(if (word_subword (read YMM2 s) (8*i,8):byte) = word_subword (read (memory :> bytes256(read RDI s)) s) (8*i,8) then 255 else 0) * 2 EXP (8*i))):int256) /\


**gq2_entry_mc**

```text
gq2_entry_mc = [word 0x48;word 0x83;word 0xfa;word 0x20;word 0x0f;word 0x82;word 0xd6;word 0x02;word 0x00;word 0x00]:byte list
```

**gq2_small_mc**

```text
gq2_small_mc = [word 0x83;word 0xfa;word 0x01;word 0x76;word 0x7b;word 0x89;word 0xf8;word 0x09;word 0xf0;word 0x25;word 0xff;word 0x0f;word 0x00;word 0x00;word 0x3d;word 0xe0;word 0x0f;word 0x00;word 0x00;word 0x7f;word 0x2b;word 0xc5;word 0xfe;word 0x6f;word 0x16]:byte list
```

**gq2_image_domain**

```text
gq2_image_domain bias a <=> bias<=a /\ a<bias+0x1e2000
```

**gq2_image_execute**

```text
gq2_image_execute bias a <=> bias+0x26000<=a /\ a<bias+0x17c000
```

**gq2_image_write**

```text
gq2_image_write bias a <=> bias+0x1d3000<=a /\ a<bias+0x1e2000
```

**gq2_immutable**

```text
gq2_immutable bias a <=>
    (bias<=a /\ a<bias+0x25388) \/
    (bias+0x26000<=a /\ a<bias+0x17b0fc) \/
    (bias+0x17c000<=a /\ a<bias+0x1cec31)
```

**gq2_initialized_rw**

```text
gq2_initialized_rw bias a <=> bias+0x1cf8d0<=a /\ a<bias+0x1e1f50
```

**gq2_reference_image**

```text
gq2_reference_image bias (file_bytes:num->byte) (initial_rw:int64->byte)
                      (padding:int64->byte) (a:int64) =
    if gq2_immutable bias (val a) then file_bytes(val a-bias)
    else if gq2_initialized_rw bias (val a) then initial_rw a
    else padding a
```

**gq2_full_image**

```text
gq2_full_image bias file_bytes initial_rw padding (m:int64->byte) <=>
    !a. gq2_image_domain bias (val a)
        ==> m a=gq2_reference_image bias file_bytes initial_rw padding a
```

**gq2_pagecover**

```text
gq2_pagecover (p:int64) n a <=>
    ~(n=0) /\ 4096*(val p DIV 4096)<=a /\
    a<4096*((val p+n-1) DIV 4096+1)
```

**gq2_profile**

```text
gq2_profile (cpuid:num->num->num#num#num#num) osxsave xcr0 cet granule <=>
    osxsave /\ xcr0=7 /\ ~cet /\ granule=4096 /\
    cpuid 0 0=(13,1970169159,1818588270,1231384169) /\
    cpuid 1 0=(132800,67584,2128097807,260803349) /\
    cpuid 7 0=(0,296,0,0) /\ cpuid 7 1=(0,296,0,0) /\
    cpuid 13 0=(7,832,832,0) /\ cpuid 13 1=(0,0,0,0) /\
    cpuid 2147483648 0=(2147483656,0,0,0) /\
    cpuid 2147483649 0=(0,0,33,672139264)
```

**gq2_file_code**

```text
gq2_file_code (file_bytes:num->byte) <=>
    !i. i<1023 ==> file_bytes(0x152320+i)=EL i gq2_memcmp_mc
```

**gq2_g1_pre**

```text
gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
    stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) <=>
    gq2_profile cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule /\
    bias MOD 4096=0 /\ bias+0x1e2000<=2 EXP 64 /\
    gq2_file_code file_bytes /\ gq2_full_image bias file_bytes initial_rw padding m /\
    n<2 EXP 64 /\ val(p:int64)+n<=2 EXP 64 /\ val(q:int64)+n<=2 EXP 64 /\
    val(stackpointer:int64) MOD 16=8 /\ val stackpointer+8<2 EXP 64 /\
    assurance_canonical48(val(ra:int64)) /\ ~(ra=word(bias+0x152320)) /\
    read RIP s=word(bias+0x152320) /\ read RSP s=stackpointer /\
    C_ARGUMENTS [p;q;word n] s /\ read memory s=m /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) s=ra /\
    bytes_loaded s (word(bias+0x152320)) gq2_memcmp_mc /\
    read DF s=F /\ ~bit 18(read rflags s) /\
    assurance_canonical48(val(read ABI_FS_BASE a)) /\
    assurance_span_ok (\x. rp x /\ ordinary x) (val stackpointer) 8 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\x. xp x /\ ordinary x) (bias+0x152320) 1023 /\
    (!x. ~(assurance_region stackpointer 8 x /\
            assurance_region (word(bias+0x152320)) 1023 x)) /\
    (!x. gq2_pagecover p n x \/ gq2_pagecover q n x
         ==> assurance_canonical48 x /\ rp x /\ ordinary x) /\
    (!x. gq2_image_domain bias x ==>
       assurance_canonical48 x /\ rp x /\ ordinary x /\
       (xp x <=> gq2_image_execute bias x) /\
       (wp x <=> gq2_image_write bias x))
```

**gq2_strict_read**

```text
gq2_strict_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n x <=>
    rp x /\ ordinary x /\
    (assurance_region p n x \/ assurance_region q n x \/
     assurance_region stackpointer 8 x)
```

**gq2_mapped_read**

```text
gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n x <=>
    rp x /\ ordinary x /\
    (gq2_pagecover p n x \/ gq2_pagecover q n x \/
     assurance_region stackpointer 8 x)
```

**gq2_allowed_fetch**

```text
gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary x <=>
    xp x /\ ordinary x /\ assurance_region (word(bias+0x152320)) 1023 x
```

**gq2_before_ret_prefix**

```text
(gq2_before_ret_prefix 0 (s:x86state) t <=> t=s) /\
  (gq2_before_ret_prefix (SUC k) s t <=>
    ?u. x86 s u /\ ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len s u) /\
        gq2_before_ret_prefix k u t)
```

**gq2_strict_prefix_property**

```text
gq2_strict_prefix_property bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave
    xcr0 cet granule stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary a <=>
    !s k t.
    gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
      stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a) /\
    gq2_before_ret_prefix k s t
    ==> ALL (assurance_event_policy
          (gq2_strict_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n) (\x.F))
          (assurance_delta s t)
```

**gq2_small_events**

```text
gq2_small_events pc e = APPEND
   [EventLoad(word 0x210100,32);
    EventJump(word(pc+0x2f5),word(pc+0x2f5));
    EventJump(word(pc+0x2e5),word(pc+0x2e5));
    EventJump(word(pc+10),word(pc+0x2e0))] e
```

**gq2_observed_cpuid**

```text
gq2_observed_cpuid leaf subleaf =
    if leaf=0 /\ subleaf=0 then (13,1970169159,1818588270,1231384169)
    else if leaf=1 /\ subleaf=0 then (132800,67584,2128097807,260803349)
    else if leaf=7 /\ (subleaf=0 \/ subleaf=1) then (0,296,0,0)
    else if leaf=13 /\ subleaf=0 then (7,832,832,0)
    else if leaf=2147483648 /\ subleaf=0 then (2147483656,0,0,0)
    else if leaf=2147483649 /\ subleaf=0 then (0,0,33,672139264)
    else (0,0,0,0)
```

**gq2_witness_overlay**

```text
gq2_witness_overlay a <=>
    (0x200000<=a /\ a<0x201000) \/
    (0x210000<=a /\ a<0x211000) \/
    (0x300000<=a /\ a<0x301000) \/
    (0x500000<=a /\ a<0x501000)
```

**gq2_witness_memory**

```text
gq2_witness_memory file_bytes initial_rw padding (a:int64) =
    if gq2_image_domain 0x40000000 (val a)
    then gq2_reference_image 0x40000000 file_bytes initial_rw padding a
    else if 0x300008<=val a /\ val a<0x300010
    then word(0x400000 DIV (256 EXP (val a-0x300008)))
    else if a=word 0x200100 \/ a=word 0x210100 then word 255
    else if a=word 0x200101 then word 128
    else if a=word 0x210101 then word 129
    else word 165
```

**gq2_witness_read**

```text
gq2_witness_read a <=> gq2_image_domain 0x40000000 a \/ gq2_witness_overlay a
```

**gq2_witness_write**

```text
gq2_witness_write a <=> gq2_image_write 0x40000000 a \/
    (0x300000<=a /\ a<0x301000)
```

**gq2_witness_execute**

```text
gq2_witness_execute a <=> gq2_image_execute 0x40000000 a
```

**gq2_witness_state**

```text
gq2_witness_state file_bytes initial_rw padding =
    x86state_RECORD (word 0x40152320)
      (\r:(4)word. if r=word 7 then word 0x200100
                  else if r=word 6 then word 0x210100
                  else if r=word 2 then word 2
                  else if r=word 4 then word 0x300008 else word 0)
      (\z:(5)word. word 0) (\k:(3)word. word 0) (word 2)
      (gq2_witness_memory file_bytes initial_rw padding) []
```

**gq2_witness_abi**

```text
gq2_witness_abi = abi_omitted_state_RECORD
    (word 0x37f) (word 0) (word 0xffff) (word 0) (word 0) (word 0)
    (word 0) (word 0) (\r:(3)word. word 0) (word 0x1f80)
    (word 0) (word 0x500000) (word 0) (word 0)
```

**gq2_prefix_site**

```text
gq2_prefix_site off len inst <=>
   (off=0 /\ len=4 /\ inst=CMP (Register(Gpr (word 2) Full_64)) (Imm8(word 32))) \/
   (off=4 /\ len=6 /\ inst=JUMP Condition_B (Imm32 (word 726))) \/
   (off=736 /\ len=3 /\ inst=CMP (Register(Gpr (word 2) Lower_32)) (Imm8(word 1))) \/
   (off=739 /\ len=2 /\ inst=JUMP Condition_BE (Imm8 (word 123))) \/
   (off=741 /\ len=2 /\ inst=MOV (Register(Gpr (word 0) Lower_32)) (Register(Gpr (word 7) Lower_32))) \/
   (off=743 /\ len=2 /\ inst=OR (Register(Gpr (word 0) Lower_32)) (Register(Gpr (word 6) Lower_32))) \/
   (off=745 /\ len=5 /\ inst=AND (Register(Gpr (word 0) Lower_32)) (Imm32(word 4095))) \/
   (off=750 /\ len=5 /\ inst=CMP (Register(Gpr (word 0) Lower_32)) (Imm32(word 4064))) \/
   (off=755 /\ len=2 /\ inst=JUMP Condition_NLE (Imm8 (word 43))) \/
   (off=757 /\ len=4 /\ inst=VMOVDQU (Simdregister(Simdreg (word 2) Lower_256)) (Memop Word256 (Bsid (SOME(Gpr (word 6) Full_64)) NONE (word 0) (word 0))))
```

**gq2_prefix_step_v2**

```text
gq2_prefix_step_v2 bias rp xp ordinary (remaining:num) source target <=>
    assurance_step_policy
      (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary (word 0x300008)
         (word 0x200100) (word 0x210100) 2) (\x.F)
      (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
    (?off len inst. gq2_prefix_site off len inst /\
       read RIP source=word(bias+0x152320+off) /\
       x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
    ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target)
```

**assurance_delta**

```text
assurance_delta (s:x86state) (s':x86state) =
    @d:uarch_event list. read events s' = APPEND d (read events s)
```

**assurance_observation**

```text
assurance_observation (s:x86state) (len:num) (inst:instruction) (s':x86state) =
    (read RIP s,len,inst,REVERSE(assurance_delta s s'))
```

**assurance_observed_step**

```text
assurance_observed_step (s:x86state)
      (obs:int64#num#instruction#uarch_event list) (s':x86state) <=>
    ?len inst.
      x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) /\
      (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,,
       x86_execute inst) s s' /\
      obs = assurance_observation s len inst s'
```

**assurance_actual_ret**

```text
assurance_actual_ret len (s:x86state) (s':x86state) <=>
    x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,RET) /\
    (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,, x86_RET) s s'
```

**assurance_canonical48**

```text
assurance_canonical48 a <=>
    a < 2 EXP 64 /\ (a < 2 EXP 47 \/ 2 EXP 64 - 2 EXP 47 <= a)
```

**assurance_region**

```text
assurance_region (p:int64) n a <=> val p <= a /\ a < val p+n
```

**assurance_span_ok**

```text
assurance_span_ok perm a width <=>
    a+width <= 2 EXP 64 /\
    !j. j < width ==> assurance_canonical48 (a+j) /\ perm(a+j)
```

**assurance_event_policy**

```text
assurance_event_policy rp wp e <=>
    match e with
    | EventLoad(adr,width) -> assurance_span_ok rp (val adr) width
    | EventStore(adr,width) -> assurance_span_ok wp (val adr) width
    | _ -> T
```

**assurance_fetch_policy**

```text
assurance_fetch_policy xp
      (obs:int64#num#instruction#uarch_event list) <=>
    assurance_span_ok xp (val(FST obs)) (FST(SND obs))
```

**assurance_checked_run**

```text
(assurance_checked_run 0 (P:x86state->bool)
      (C:num->x86state->x86state->bool) s <=> P s) /\
  (assurance_checked_run (SUC n) P C s <=>
     (?s'. x86 s s') /\
     !s'. x86 s s' ==> C (SUC n) s s' /\
                        assurance_checked_run n P C s')
```

**assurance_step_policy**

```text
assurance_step_policy rp wp xp (s:x86state) (s':x86state) <=>
   ?len inst d.
     x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) /\
     assurance_span_ok xp (val(read RIP s)) len /\
     read events s' = APPEND d (read events s) /\
     ALL (assurance_event_policy rp wp) d
```

**assurance_zero_step**

```text
assurance_zero_step rp wp xp remaining s s' <=>
   assurance_step_policy rp wp xp s s' /\
   (remaining = 1 ==> assurance_actual_ret 2 s s')
```

**condition_semantics**

```text
(condition_semantics Unconditional s <=>
        T) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_B s <=>
        read CF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_BE s <=>
        read CF s \/ read ZF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_L s <=>
        ~(read SF s <=> read OF s)) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_LE s <=>
        ~(read SF s <=> read OF s) \/ read ZF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_NB s <=>
        ~read CF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_NBE s <=>
        ~(read CF s \/ read ZF s)) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_NL s <=>
        (read SF s <=> read OF s)) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_NLE s <=>
        (read SF s <=> read OF s) /\ ~read ZF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_NO s <=>
        ~read OF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_NP s <=>
        ~read PF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_NS s <=>
        ~read SF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_NZ s <=>
        ~read ZF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_O s <=>
        read OF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_P s <=>
        read PF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_S s <=>
        read SF s) /\
  (condition_semantics Condition_Z s <=>
        read ZF s)
```

**add_store_event**

```text
(add_store_event: operand->x86state->x86state->x86state->bool) =
    \(op:operand) (s:x86state) (s0:x86state).
      match op with
      Memop w (bs:bsid) ->
        (events := CONS (EventStore (bsid_semantics bs s, (bytesize w) DIV 8))
                        (read events s0)) s0
      | _ -> (=) s0
```

**add_load_event**

```text
(add_load_event: operand->x86state->x86state->x86state->bool) =
    \(op:operand) (s:x86state) (s0:x86state).
      match op with
      Memop w (bs:bsid) ->
        (events := CONS (EventLoad (bsid_semantics bs s, (bytesize w) DIV 8))
                        (read events s0)) s0
      | _ -> (=) s0
```

**decode_hi**

```text
decode_hi x v sz (opc:3 word) rm l =
  match x,v,val opc with
  | F,_,0 -> read_imm sz l >>= \(imm,l). SOME (TEST rm imm,l)
  | F,_,2 -> SOME (NOT rm,l)
  | F,_,3 -> SOME (NEG rm,l)
  | F,_,4 -> SOME (MUL2 (adx sz) rm,l)
  | F,_,5 -> SOME (IMUL2 (adx sz) rm,l)
  // | F,_,6 -> let hi,lo = adx sz in SOME (DIV2 (lo,hi) (hi,lo) rm,l)
  // | F,_,7 -> SOME (IDIV rm,l)
  | T,_,0 -> SOME (INC rm,l)
  | T,_,1 -> SOME (DEC rm,l)
  | T,T,2 -> SOME (CALL (r1_near_operand64 rm),l)
  | T,T,4 -> SOME (JMP (r1_near_operand64 rm),l)
  | T,T,6 -> SOME (PUSH rm,l)
  | _ -> NONE
```

**decode_aux**

```text
!pfxs rex l. decode_aux pfxs rex l =
  read_byte l >>= \(b,l).
  bitmatch b with
  | [0b00:2; opc:3; 0b0:1; d; v] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size_W rex v pfxs in
    read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
    let dest,src = if d then reg,rm else rm,reg in
    SOME (decode_binop (word_zx opc) dest src,l)
  | [0b00:2; opc:3; 0b10:2; v] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size_W rex v pfxs in
    read_imm (to_wordsize sz) l >>= \(imm,l).
    SOME (decode_binop (word_zx opc) (%(gpr_adjust (word 0) sz)) imm,l)
  | [0x0f:8] -> read_byte l >>= \(b,l).
    (bitmatch b with
    | [0b0001000:7; d] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      let reg = mmreg reg sz in
      let rm = simd_of_RM sz rm in
      let dest,src = if d then rm,reg else reg,rm in
      SOME (MOVUPS dest src, l)
    | [0x1e:8] ->
        read_byte l >>= \(b,l).
        (bitmatch b with
         | [0xfa:8] ->
           (match pfxs with
            | (F, RepZ, SG0) -> SOME (ENDBR64,l)
            | _ -> NONE)
         | _ -> NONE)
    | [0x1f:8] ->
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((_,rm),l).
      (match pfxs with
        | (F, Rep0, SG0) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | (F, Rep0, CS) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | (F, Rep0, DS) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | (F, Rep0, ES) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | (F, Rep0, SS) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | (T, Rep0, SG0) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | (T, Rep0, CS) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | (T, Rep0, DS) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | (T, Rep0, ES) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | (T, Rep0, SS) -> SOME (NOP_N rm,l)
        | _ -> NONE)
    | [0b0010100:7; d] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      let reg = mmreg reg sz in
      let rm = simd_of_RM sz rm in
      let dest,src = if d then rm,reg else reg,rm in
      SOME (MOVAPS dest src, l)
    | [0x38:8] -> read_byte l >>= \(b,l).
      (bitmatch b with
      | [0x00:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
        let sz = Lower_128 in
        read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        SOME (PSHUFB (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
      | [0xdc:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
        let sz = Lower_128 in
        read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        SOME (AESENC (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
      | [0xdd:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
        let sz = Lower_128 in
        read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        SOME (AESENCLAST (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
      | [0xde:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
        let sz = Lower_128 in
        read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        SOME (AESDEC (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
      | [0xdf:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
        let sz = Lower_128 in
        read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        SOME (AESDECLAST (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
      | [0b1111000:7; d] ->
        if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
        let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
        read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        if is_memop rm then
          let reg = %(Gpr reg sz) in
          let rm = operand_of_RM sz rm in
          let dest,src = if d then rm,reg else reg,rm in
          SOME (MOVBE dest src,l)
        else NONE
      | [0xf6:8] ->
        let sz = op_size T (rex_W rex) T pfxs in
        read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        (match pfxs with
        | (T, Rep0, SG0) -> SOME (ADCX reg rm,l)
        | (F, RepZ, SG0) -> SOME (ADOX reg rm,l)
        | _ -> NONE)
      | _ -> NONE)
    | [0x3a:8] -> read_byte l >>= \(b,l).
      (bitmatch b with
      | [0x0e:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
        let sz = Lower_128 in
        read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        read_imm Byte l >>= \(imm8,l).
        SOME (PBLENDW (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm) imm8, l)
      | [0x22:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
        read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        read_imm Byte l >>= \(imm8,l).
        let dest = mmreg reg Lower_128 in
        let sz = if rex_W rex then Full_64 else Lower_32 in
        let src = operand_of_RM sz rm in
        (match pfxs with
         | (T, Rep0, SG0) ->
           if rex_W rex then SOME (PINSRQ dest src imm8, l)
           else SOME (PINSRD dest src imm8, l)
         | _ -> NONE)
      | [0xdf:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
        let sz = Lower_128 in
        read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        read_imm Byte l >>= \(imm8,l).
        SOME (AESKEYGENASSIST (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm) imm8, l)
      | _ -> NONE)
    | [0x4:4; c:4] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = op_size T (rex_W rex) T pfxs in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME (CMOV (decode_condition c) reg rm,l)
    | [0x65:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME (PCMPGTW (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
    | [0x66:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME (PCMPGTD (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
    | [0x6e:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      let dest = mmreg reg Lower_128 in
      let sz = if rex_W rex then Full_64 else Lower_32 in
      let src = operand_of_RM sz rm in
      (match pfxs with
      | (T, Rep0, SG0) ->
        if rex_W rex then SOME (MOVQ dest src, l) else SOME (MOVD dest src, l)
      | _ -> NONE)
    | [0b011:3; d; 0b1111:4] ->
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      let reg = mmreg reg sz in
      let rm = simd_of_RM sz rm in
      let dest,src = if d then rm,reg else reg,rm in
      (match pfxs with
      | (T, Rep0, SG0) -> SOME (MOVDQA dest src, l)
      | (F, RepZ, SG0) -> SOME (MOVDQU dest src, l)
      | _ -> NONE)
    | [0x70:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      read_imm Byte l >>= \(imm8,l).
      SOME (PSHUFD (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm) imm8, l)
    | [0x71:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      (read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
       match rm with
       | RM_reg _ -> if (word_zx reg):(3 word) = word 0b010 then
         (read_imm Byte l >>= \(imm8,l).
          SOME (PSRLW (simd_of_RM sz rm) imm8, l))
         else NONE
       | _ -> NONE)
    | [0x72:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      (read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
       match rm with
       | RM_reg _ -> if (word_zx reg):(3 word) = word 0b100 then
         (read_imm Byte l >>= \(imm8,l).
          SOME (PSRAD (simd_of_RM sz rm) imm8, l))
         else NONE
       | _ -> NONE)
    | [0x7e:8] ->
      (read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      match pfxs with
      | (T, Rep0, SG0) ->
        if rex_W rex then
          let src = mmreg reg Lower_128 in
          let dst = operand_of_RM Full_64 rm in
          SOME (MOVQ dst src, l)
        else
          let src = mmreg reg Lower_128 in
          let dst = operand_of_RM Lower_32 rm in
          SOME (MOVD dst src, l)
    | (F, RepZ, SG0) ->
          let src = if is_memop rm then operand_of_RM Full_64 rm
                  else simd_of_RM Lower_128 rm in
          let dst = mmreg reg Lower_128 in
          SOME (MOVQ dst src, l)
        | _ -> NONE)
    | [0x8:4; c:4] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      read_int32 l >>= \(imm,l).
      SOME (JUMP (decode_condition c) (Imm32 imm),l)
    | [0x9:4; c:4] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = regsize8 (is_some rex) in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((_,rm),l).
      SOME (SET (decode_condition c) rm,l)
    | [0b101:3; op:2; 0b011:3] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME (decode_BT op rm reg,l)
    | [0xa:4; x; 0b100:3] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      read_byte l >>= \(b,l).
      SOME ((if x then SHRD else SHLD) rm reg (Imm8 b),l)
    | [0xa:4; x; 0b101:3] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME ((if x then SHRD else SHLD) rm reg (%cl),l)
    | [0xaf:8] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME (IMUL reg rm,l)
    | [0xb8:8] ->
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      (match pfxs with
      | (F, RepZ, SG0) -> SOME (POPCNT reg rm,l)
      | _ -> NONE)
    | [0xba:8] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      read_opcode_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((opc,rm),l).
      (bitmatch opc with
      | [1:1; op:2] ->
        read_byte l >>= \(b,l).
        SOME (decode_BT op rm (Imm8 b),l)
      | _ -> NONE)
    | [0xbc:8] ->
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      (match pfxs with
      | (F, Rep0, SG0) -> SOME (BSF reg rm,l)
      | (F, RepZ, SG0) -> SOME (TZCNT reg rm,l)
      | _ -> NONE)
    | [0xbd:8] ->
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      (match pfxs with
      | (F, Rep0, SG0) -> SOME (BSR reg rm,l)
      | (F, RepZ, SG0) -> SOME (LZCNT reg rm,l)
      | _ -> NONE)
    | [0xb:4; s; 0b11:2; v] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz2 = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      let sz = if v then Lower_16 else regsize8 (is_some rex) in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      let op = if s then MOVSX else MOVZX in
      SOME (op (%(gpr_adjust reg sz2)) (operand_of_RM sz rm),l)
    | [0b11001:5; r:3] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
      let reg = rex_reg (rex_B rex) r in
      SOME (BSWAP (%(gpr_adjust reg sz)),l)
    | [0xd4:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME (PADDQ (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
    | [0xd6:8] ->
      (read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
        match pfxs with
        | (T, Rep0, SG0) ->
            let src = mmreg reg Lower_128 in
            let dst = if is_memop rm then operand_of_RM Full_64 rm
                    else simd_of_RM Lower_128 rm in
            SOME (MOVQ dst src, l)
        | _ -> NONE)
    | [0xd7:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      let dest = %(gpr_adjust reg Lower_32) in
      let src = simd_of_RM Lower_128 rm in
      SOME (PMOVMSKB dest src, l)
    | [0xdb:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME (PAND (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
    | [0xef:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME (PXOR (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
    | [0xfe:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
      let sz = Lower_128 in
      read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
      SOME (PADDD (mmreg reg sz) (simd_of_RM sz rm), l)
    | _ -> NONE)
  | [0b01010:5; r:3] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    SOME (PUSH (%(Gpr (rex_reg (rex_B rex) r) Full_64)),l)
  | [0b01011:5; r:3] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    SOME (POP (%(Gpr (rex_reg (rex_B rex) r) Full_64)),l)
  | [0x63:8] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz2 = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
    read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
    SOME (MOVSX (%(gpr_adjust reg sz2)) (operand_of_RM Lower_32 rm),l)
  | [0b011010:6; x; 0b0:1] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    read_imm (if x then Byte else Quadword) l >>= \(imm,l).
    SOME (PUSH imm,l)
  | [0b011010:6; x; 0b1:1] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
    read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
    read_imm (if x then Byte else Quadword) l >>= \(imm,l).
    SOME (IMUL3 reg (rm,imm),l)
  | [0x7:4; c:4] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    read_byte l >>= \(b,l).
    SOME (JUMP (decode_condition c) (Imm8 b),l)
  | [0b1000000:7; v] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size_W rex v pfxs in
    read_opcode_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((opc,rm),l).
    read_imm (to_wordsize sz) l >>= \(imm,l).
    SOME (decode_binop (word_zx opc) rm imm,l)
  | [0x83:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
    read_opcode_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((opc,rm),l).
    read_byte l >>= \(b,l).
    SOME (decode_binop (word_zx opc) rm (Imm8 b),l)
  | [0b1000010:7; v] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size_W rex v pfxs in
    read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
    SOME (TEST rm reg,l)
  | [0b1000011:7; v] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size_W rex v pfxs in
    read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
    SOME (XCHG reg rm,l)
  | [0b100010:6; d; v] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size_W rex v pfxs in
    read_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
    let dest,src = if d then reg,rm else rm,reg in
    SOME (MOV dest src,l)
  | [0x8d:8] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size T (rex_W rex) T pfxs in
    read_ModRM rex l >>= \((reg,rm),l).
    (match rm with
    | RM_mem ea -> SOME (LEA (%(gpr_adjust reg sz)) ea,l)
    | _ -> NONE)
  | [0x8f:8] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size_W rex T pfxs in
    read_opcode_ModRM_operand rex sz l >>= \((opc,rm),l).
    if opc = word 0 then
      SOME (POP rm,l)
    else NONE
  | [0x90:8] -> if has_unhandled_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    SOME (NOP,l)
  | [0xa4:8] ->
    (match pfxs with
     | (F, Rep0, SG0) -> SOME (MOVSB F (%rdi) (%rsi) (Imm8(word 1)),l)
     | (F, RepZ, SG0) -> SOME (MOVSB T (%rdi) (%rsi) (%rcx),l)
     | (F, RepNZ, SG0) -> SOME (MOVSB T (%rdi) (%rsi) (%rcx),l)
     | _ -> NONE)
  | [0b1010100:7; v] -> if has_pfxs pfxs then NONE else
    let sz = op_size T (rex_W rex) v pfxs in
    read_imm (to_wordsize sz) l >>= \(imm,l).
    SOME (TEST (%(gpr_adjust (word 0) sz)) imm,l)
```
