### GMQ_PROJECTION

```text
!s a s' a'. gmq_extended_step (s,a) (s',a') ==> x86 s s'
```

### GMQ_BACKLIFT_ALL

```text
!s s' a. x86 s s' ==> ?a'. gmq_extended_step (s,a) (s',a')
```

### GMQ_REFINES_E4

```text
!s a s' a'. gmq_extended_step (s,a) (s',a') ==> abi_e4_extended_step (s,a) (s',a')
```

### GMQ_OMITTED_STATE_PRESERVED

```text
!s a s' a'. gmq_all_decodes_qualified s /\
    gmq_extended_step (s,a) (s',a') ==> a'=a
```

### GMQ_14_FIELDS_PRESERVED

```text
!s a s' a'. gmq_all_decodes_qualified s /\
    gmq_extended_step (s,a) (s',a') ==>
    read ABI_X87_CW a' = read ABI_X87_CW a /\
    read ABI_X87_SW a' = read ABI_X87_SW a /\
    read ABI_X87_TAG a' = read ABI_X87_TAG a /\
    read ABI_X87_FOP a' = read ABI_X87_FOP a /\
    read ABI_X87_IP a' = read ABI_X87_IP a /\
    read ABI_X87_CS a' = read ABI_X87_CS a /\
    read ABI_X87_DP a' = read ABI_X87_DP a /\
    read ABI_X87_DS a' = read ABI_X87_DS a /\
    read ABI_X87_REGS a' = read ABI_X87_REGS a /\
    read ABI_MXCSR a' = read ABI_MXCSR a /\
    read ABI_FS_SELECTOR a' = read ABI_FS_SELECTOR a /\
    read ABI_FS_BASE a' = read ABI_FS_BASE a /\
    read ABI_GS_SELECTOR a' = read ABI_GS_SELECTOR a /\
    read ABI_GS_BASE a' = read ABI_GS_BASE a
```

### GMQ_MEMORY_BYTE_CLASS_QUALIFIED

```text
!(r:4 word) addr.
    gmq_instruction (MOVZX (Register(Gpr r Lower_32)) (Memop Byte addr))
```

### GMQ_RETURN_ECX_LOAD_QUALIFIED

```text
gmq_instruction (MOVZX (Register(Gpr (word 1) Lower_32)) (Memop Byte (Bsid (SOME(Gpr (word 6) Full_64)) (SOME(Gpr (word 0) Full_64)) (word 0) (word 0))))
```

### GMQ_RETURN_EAX_LOAD_QUALIFIED

```text
gmq_instruction (MOVZX (Register(Gpr (word 0) Lower_32)) (Memop Byte (Bsid (SOME(Gpr (word 7) Full_64)) (SOME(Gpr (word 0) Full_64)) (word 0) (word 0))))
```

### GMQ_OLD_CLASS_INCLUDED

```text
!inst. abi_e4_integer_vector_instruction inst ==> gmq_instruction inst
```

### GMQ_CHECKED_RUN_LIFT

```text
!N P C s a.
    assurance_checked_run N P
      (\k s s'. C k s s' /\ gmq_all_decodes_qualified s) s
    ==> eventually_n gmq_extended_step N (\t. P(FST t) /\ SND t = a) (s,a)
```

### gmq_memory_byte_operand

```text
gmq_memory_byte_operand op <=> ?addr. op = Memop Byte addr
```

### gmq_instruction

```text
gmq_instruction inst <=> abi_e4_integer_vector_instruction inst \/
    (?r src. inst = MOVZX (Register(Gpr r Lower_32)) src /\
             gmq_memory_byte_operand src)
```

### gmq_omitted14_preserved

```text
gmq_omitted14_preserved (a:abi_omitted_state) a' <=>
    a'=a /\
    read ABI_X87_CW a' = read ABI_X87_CW a /\
    read ABI_X87_SW a' = read ABI_X87_SW a /\
    read ABI_X87_TAG a' = read ABI_X87_TAG a /\
    read ABI_X87_FOP a' = read ABI_X87_FOP a /\
    read ABI_X87_IP a' = read ABI_X87_IP a /\
    read ABI_X87_CS a' = read ABI_X87_CS a /\
    read ABI_X87_DP a' = read ABI_X87_DP a /\
    read ABI_X87_DS a' = read ABI_X87_DS a /\
    read ABI_X87_REGS a' = read ABI_X87_REGS a /\
    read ABI_MXCSR a' = read ABI_MXCSR a /\
    read ABI_FS_SELECTOR a' = read ABI_FS_SELECTOR a /\
    read ABI_FS_BASE a' = read ABI_FS_BASE a /\
    read ABI_GS_SELECTOR a' = read ABI_GS_SELECTOR a /\
    read ABI_GS_BASE a' = read ABI_GS_BASE a
```

### gmq_extended_step

```text
gmq_extended_step (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) (s',a') <=>
    ?len inst. x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) /\
      (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,, x86_execute inst) s s' /\
      (gmq_instruction inst ==> gmq_omitted14_preserved a a')
```

### gmq_all_decodes_qualified

```text
gmq_all_decodes_qualified (s:x86state) <=>
    !len inst. x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) ==> gmq_instruction inst
```

### x86

```text
x86 s s' <=>
    ?len instr. x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,instr) /\
                (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,,
                x86_execute instr) s s'
```

### x86_execute

```text
x86_execute instr s =
    match instr with
      ADC dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_ADC (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_ADC (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_ADC (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_ADC (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | ADCX dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_ADCX (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_ADCX (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)) s)) s
    | ADD dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_ADD (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_ADD (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_ADD (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_ADD (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | ADOX dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_ADOX (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_ADOX (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)) s)) s
    | AESDEC dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (x86_AESDEC (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)) s)) s
    | AESDECLAST dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (x86_AESDECLAST (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)) s))
        s
    | AESENC dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (x86_AESENC (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)) s)) s
    | AESENCLAST dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (x86_AESENCLAST (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)) s))
        s
    | AESKEYGENASSIST dest src imm8 ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (x86_AESKEYGENASSIST (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)
                                  (OPERAND8 imm8 s)) s)) s
    | AND dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_AND (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_AND (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_AND (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_AND (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | BSF dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_BSF (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_BSF (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_BSF (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)) s)) s
    | BSR dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_BSR (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_BSR (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_BSR (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)) s)) s
    | BSWAP dest ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_BSWAP (OPERAND64 dest s)
         | 32 -> x86_BSWAP (OPERAND32 dest s)) s)) s
    | BZHI dest src1 src2 ->
        (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_BZHI (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src1 s)
                          (OPERAND64 src2 s)
         | 32 -> x86_BZHI (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src1 s)
                          (OPERAND32 src2 s)
         | _ -> (\s s'. F)) s)) s
    | BT dest src ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_BT (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_BT (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_BT (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_BT (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | BTC dest src ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_BTC (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_BTC (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_BTC (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_BTC (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | BTR dest src ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_BTR (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_BTR (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_BTR (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_BTR (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | BTS dest src ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_BTS (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_BTS (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_BTS (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_BTS (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | CALL off ->
        (if r1_indirect_operand off then
          (add_load_event off s ,,
           x86_CALL (read (OPERAND64 off s) s)) s
         else
          (let ip' = word_add (read RIP s)
                              (read (OPERAND64 off s) s) in
           x86_CALL ip' s))
    | CALL_ABSOLUTE target ->
        x86_CALL target s
    | CLC ->
        x86_CLC s
    | CLD ->
        x86_CLD s
    | CMC ->
        x86_CMC s
    | CMOV cc dest src ->
        // "If the source operand is a memory operand, then it is always read,
        // regardless of whether or not the condition is met."
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_CMOV cc (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_CMOV cc (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_CMOV cc (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_CMOV cc (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | CMP dest src ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_CMP (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_CMP (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_CMP (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_CMP (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | DEC dest ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_DEC (OPERAND64 dest s)
         | 32 -> x86_DEC (OPERAND32 dest s)
         | 16 -> x86_DEC (OPERAND16 dest s)
         | 8 -> x86_DEC (OPERAND8 dest s)) s)) s
    | ENDBR64 ->
        x86_ENDBR64 s
    | IMUL3 dest (src1,src2) ->
        (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_IMUL3 (OPERAND64 dest s)
                           (OPERAND64 src1 s,OPERAND64 src2 s)
         | 32 -> x86_IMUL3 (OPERAND32 dest s)
                           (OPERAND32 src1 s, OPERAND32 src2 s)
         | 16 -> x86_IMUL3 (OPERAND16 dest s)
                           (OPERAND16 src1 s,OPERAND16 src2 s)
         | 8 -> x86_IMUL3 (OPERAND8 dest s)
                          (OPERAND8 src1 s,OPERAND8 src2 s)) s)) s
    | IMUL2 (dest1,dest2) src ->
        (add_load_event dest1 s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest1 s ,, add_store_event dest2 s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest2 with
           64 -> x86_IMUL2 (OPERAND64 dest1 s,OPERAND64 dest2 s)
                          (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_IMUL2 (OPERAND32 dest1 s,OPERAND32 dest2 s)
                          (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_IMUL2 (OPERAND16 dest1 s,OPERAND16 dest2 s)
                          (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_IMUL2 (OPERAND8 dest1 s,OPERAND8 dest2 s)
                          (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | INC dest ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_INC (OPERAND64 dest s)
         | 32 -> x86_INC (OPERAND32 dest s)
         | 16 -> x86_INC (OPERAND16 dest s)
         | 8 -> x86_INC (OPERAND8 dest s)) s)) s
    | JUMP cc off ->
        (if cc = Unconditional /\ r1_indirect_operand off then
          (add_load_event off s ,,
           x86_JMP_ABSOLUTE (read (OPERAND64 off s) s)) s
         else
        (events := CONS
          (EventJump
            (read RIP s,
              (if condition_semantics cc s
               then word_add (read RIP s)
                             (read (OPERAND64 off s) s)
               else read RIP s)))
          (read events s) ,,
         RIP :=
           if condition_semantics cc s
           then word_add (read RIP s)
                         (read (OPERAND64 off s) s)
           else read RIP s) s)
    | LEA dest bsid ->
        (add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          64 -> (OPERAND64 dest s) := (bsid_semantics bsid s)
        | 32 -> (OPERAND32 dest s) := word_sx(bsid_semantics bsid s)
        | 16 -> (OPERAND16 dest s) := word_sx(bsid_semantics bsid s)
        | 8 -> (OPERAND8 dest s) := word_sx(bsid_semantics bsid s)) s)) s
    | LZCNT dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_LZCNT (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_LZCNT (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_LZCNT (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)) s)) s
    | MOV dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_MOV (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_MOV (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_MOV (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_MOV (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | MOVAPS dest src ->
        if aligned_OPERAND128 src s /\ aligned_OPERAND128 dest s
        then
          (add_load_event src s ,,
           add_store_event dest s ,,
          (\s. x86_MOVAPS (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
        else (\s'. F)
    | MOVDQA dest src ->
        if aligned_OPERAND128 src s /\ aligned_OPERAND128 dest s
        then
          (add_load_event src s ,,
           add_store_event dest s ,,
          (\s. x86_MOVDQA (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
        else (\s'. F)
    | MOVDQU dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. x86_MOVDQU (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
    | MOVBE dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_MOVBE (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_MOVBE (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_MOVBE (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | _ -> (\s s'. F)) s)) s
    | MOVD dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match (operand_size dest, operand_size src) with
          (32,128) -> x86_MOVD (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)
        | (128,32) -> x86_MOVD (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)) s)) s
    | MOVQ dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match (operand_size dest, operand_size src) with
          (64,128) -> x86_MOVQ (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)
        | (128,64) -> x86_MOVQ (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
        | (128,128) -> x86_MOVQ (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)) s)) s
    | VMOVD dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match (operand_size dest, operand_size src) with
          (32,128) -> x86_VMOVD (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND128 src s)
        | (128,32) -> x86_VMOVD (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)) s)) s
    | VMOVQ dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match (operand_size dest, operand_size src) with
          (64,128) -> x86_VMOVQ (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND128 src s)
        | (128,64) -> x86_VMOVQ (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
        | (128,128) -> x86_VMOVQ (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src s)) s)) s
    | VMOVHPD dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match (operand_size dest, operand_size src) with
          (64,128) -> x86_VMOVHPD (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND128 src s)) s)) s
    | MOVSB rept dest src size ->
        (\s. x86_MOVSB rept (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s) (OPERAND64 size s) s) s
    | MOVSX dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match (operand_size dest,operand_size src) with
           (64,32) -> x86_MOVSX (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | (64,16) -> x86_MOVSX (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | (64,8) -> x86_MOVSX (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)
         | (32,32) -> x86_MOVSX (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | (32,16) -> x86_MOVSX (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | (32,8) -> x86_MOVSX (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)
         | (16,8) -> x86_MOVSX (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | MOVUPS dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. x86_MOVUPS (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
    | MOVZX dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match (operand_size dest,operand_size src) with
           (64,16) -> x86_MOVZX (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | (64,8) -> x86_MOVZX (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)
         | (32,16) -> x86_MOVZX (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | (32,8) -> x86_MOVZX (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)
         | (16,8) -> x86_MOVZX (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | MUL2 (dest1,dest2) src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest1 s ,, add_store_event dest2 s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest2 with
           64 -> x86_MUL2 (OPERAND64 dest1 s,OPERAND64 dest2 s)
                         (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_MUL2 (OPERAND32 dest1 s,OPERAND32 dest2 s)
                         (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_MUL2 (OPERAND16 dest1 s,OPERAND16 dest2 s)
                         (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_MUL2 (OPERAND8 dest1 s,OPERAND8 dest2 s)
                         (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | MULX4 (dest1,dest2) (src1,src2) ->
        (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest1 s ,, add_store_event dest2 s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest2 with
           64 -> x86_MULX4 (OPERAND64 dest1 s,OPERAND64 dest2 s)
                           (OPERAND64 src1 s,OPERAND64 src2 s)
         | 32 -> x86_MULX4 (OPERAND32 dest1 s,OPERAND32 dest2 s)
                           (OPERAND32 src1 s,OPERAND32 src2 s)) s)) s
    | NEG dest ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_NEG (OPERAND64 dest s)
         | 32 -> x86_NEG (OPERAND32 dest s)
         | 16 -> x86_NEG (OPERAND16 dest s)
         | 8 -> x86_NEG (OPERAND8 dest s)) s)) s
    | NOP ->
        x86_NOP s
    | NOP_N dest ->
        (match operand_size dest with
           32 -> x86_NOP_N (OPERAND32 dest s)
         | 16 -> x86_NOP_N (OPERAND16 dest s)) s
    | NOT dest ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_NOT (OPERAND64 dest s)
         | 32 -> x86_NOT (OPERAND32 dest s)
         | 16 -> x86_NOT (OPERAND16 dest s)
         | 8 -> x86_NOT (OPERAND8 dest s)) s)) s
    | OR dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_OR (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_OR (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_OR (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_OR (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | PADDD dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PADDD (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
    | PADDQ dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PADDQ (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
    | PAND dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PAND (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
    | PBLENDW dest src imm8 ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PBLENDW (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)
                        (OPERAND8 imm8 s) s)) s
    | PCMPGTD dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PCMPGTD (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
    | PCMPGTW dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PCMPGTW (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
    | PEXT dest src1 src2 ->
       (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          64 -> x86_PEXT (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src1 s) (OPERAND64 src2 s)
        | 32 -> x86_PEXT (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src1 s) (OPERAND32 src2 s))
          s)) s
    | PINSRD dest src imm8 ->
       (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PINSRD (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
                       (OPERAND8 imm8 s) s)) s
    | PINSRQ dest src imm8 ->
       (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PINSRQ (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
                       (OPERAND8 imm8 s) s)) s
    | PMOVMSKB dest src ->
       (add_load_event src s ,, add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_PMOVMSKB (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)
         | 32  -> x86_PMOVMSKB (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)) s)) s
    | POP dest ->
       (add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_POP (OPERAND64 dest s)
         | 16 -> x86_POP (OPERAND16 dest s)) s)) s
    | POPCNT dest src ->
       (add_load_event src s ,, add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_POPCNT (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_POPCNT (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_POPCNT (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)) s)) s
    | PSHUFB dest src ->
       (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PSHUFB (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
    | PSHUFD dest src imm8 ->
       (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PSHUFD (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s)
                       (OPERAND8 imm8 s) s)) s
    | PSRAD dest imm8 ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PSRAD (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND8 imm8 s) s)) s
    | PSRLW dest imm8 ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PSRLW (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND8 imm8 s) s)) s
    | PUSH src ->
       (add_load_event src s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size src with
           64 -> x86_PUSH (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_PUSH (OPERAND16 src s)) s)) s
    | PXOR dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. x86_PXOR (OPERAND128_SSE dest s) (OPERAND128_SSE src s) s)) s
    | RCL dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_RCL (OPERAND64 dest s)
                         (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 64)
         | 32 -> x86_RCL (OPERAND32 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 16 -> x86_RCL (OPERAND16 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 8 -> x86_RCL (OPERAND8 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)) s)) s
    | RCR dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_RCR (OPERAND64 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 64)
         | 32 -> x86_RCR (OPERAND32 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 16 -> x86_RCR (OPERAND16 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 8 -> x86_RCR (OPERAND8 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)) s)) s
    | RET ->
        x86_RET s
    | ROL dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_ROL (OPERAND64 dest s)
                         (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 64)
         | 32 -> x86_ROL (OPERAND32 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 16 -> x86_ROL (OPERAND16 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 8 -> x86_ROL (OPERAND8 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)) s)) s
    | ROR dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_ROR (OPERAND64 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 64)
         | 32 -> x86_ROR (OPERAND32 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 16 -> x86_ROR (OPERAND16 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 8 -> x86_ROR (OPERAND8 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)) s)) s
    | SAR dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_SAR (OPERAND64 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 64)
         | 32 -> x86_SAR (OPERAND32 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 16 -> x86_SAR (OPERAND16 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 8 -> x86_SAR (OPERAND8 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)) s)) s
    | SBB dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_SBB (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_SBB (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_SBB (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_SBB (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | SET cc dest ->
       (add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           8 -> x86_SET cc (OPERAND8 dest s)
         | _ -> ARB) s)) s
    | SHL dest src ->
       (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        add_store_event dest s ,,
       (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_SHL (OPERAND64 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 64)
         | 32 -> x86_SHL (OPERAND32 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 16 -> x86_SHL (OPERAND16 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 8 -> x86_SHL (OPERAND8 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)) s)) s
    | SHLD dest src c ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_SHLD (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
                          (val(read (OPERAND8 c s) s) MOD 64)
         | 32 -> x86_SHLD (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
                          (val(read (OPERAND8 c s) s) MOD 32)
         | 16 -> x86_SHLD (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
                          (val(read (OPERAND8 c s) s) MOD 32)
         | 8 -> x86_SHLD (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)
                         (val(read (OPERAND8 c s) s) MOD 32)) s)) s
    | SHR dest src ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_SHR (OPERAND64 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 64)
         | 32 -> x86_SHR (OPERAND32 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 16 -> x86_SHR (OPERAND16 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)
         | 8 -> x86_SHR (OPERAND8 dest s)
                        (val(read (OPERAND8 src s) s) MOD 32)) s)) s
    | SHRD dest src c ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_SHRD (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
                          (val(read (OPERAND8 c s) s) MOD 64)
         | 32 -> x86_SHRD (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
                          (val(read (OPERAND8 c s) s) MOD 32)
         | 16 -> x86_SHRD (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
                          (val(read (OPERAND8 c s) s) MOD 32)
         | 8 -> x86_SHRD (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)
                         (val(read (OPERAND8 c s) s) MOD 32)) s)) s
    | STCF ->
        x86_STC s
    | STD ->
        x86_STD s
    | SUB dest src ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_SUB (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_SUB (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_SUB (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_SUB (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | TEST dest src ->
        (add_load_event dest s ,, add_load_event src s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_TEST (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_TEST (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_TEST (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_TEST (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | TZCNT dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_TZCNT (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_TZCNT (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_TZCNT (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)) s)) s
    | VMOVDQA dest src ->

        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPSRLDQ (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src s)
                             (OPERAND8 imm8 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPSRLDQ (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src s)
                             (OPERAND8 imm8 s)) s)) s
    | VPSRLQ dest src imm8 ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPSRLQ (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src s)
                            (OPERAND8 imm8 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPSRLQ (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src s)
                            (OPERAND8 imm8 s)) s)) s
    | VPSRLW dest src imm8 ->
        (add_load_event src s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPSRLW (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src s)
                            (OPERAND8 imm8 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPSRLW (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src s)
                            (OPERAND8 imm8 s)) s)) s
    | VPXOR dest src1 src2 ->
        (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPXOR (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src1 s)
                           (OPERAND256 src2 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPXOR (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src1 s)
                           (OPERAND128 src2 s)) s)) s
    | VPAND dest src1 src2 ->
        (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPAND (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src1 s)
                           (OPERAND256 src2 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPAND (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src1 s)
                           (OPERAND128 src2 s)) s)) s
    | VPANDN dest src1 src2 ->
        (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPANDN (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src1 s)
                           (OPERAND256 src2 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPANDN (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src1 s)
                           (OPERAND128 src2 s)) s)) s
    | VPOR dest src1 src2 ->
        (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPOR (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src1 s) (OPERAND256 src2 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPOR (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src1 s) (OPERAND128 src2 s)) s)) s
    | VPSRAD dest src imm8 ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPSRAD (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src s) (OPERAND8 imm8 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPSRAD (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src s) (OPERAND8 imm8 s)) s)) s
    | VPUNPCKHQDQ dest src1 src2 ->
        (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPUNPCKHQDQ (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src1 s) (OPERAND256 src2 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPUNPCKHQDQ (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src1 s) (OPERAND128 src2 s)) s)) s
    | VPUNPCKLQDQ dest src1 src2 ->
        (add_load_event src1 s ,, add_load_event src2 s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          256 -> x86_VPUNPCKLQDQ (OPERAND256 dest s) (OPERAND256 src1 s) (OPERAND256 src2 s)
        | 128 -> x86_VPUNPCKLQDQ (OPERAND128 dest s) (OPERAND128 src1 s) (OPERAND128 src2 s)) s)) s
    | VZEROUPPER ->
        x86_VZEROUPPER s
    | XCHG dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,, add_store_event src s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
          64 -> x86_XCHG (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
        | 32 -> x86_XCHG (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
        | 16 -> x86_XCHG (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
        | 8 -> x86_XCHG (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | XOR dest src ->
        (add_load_event src s ,, add_load_event dest s ,,
         add_store_event dest s ,,
        (\s. (match operand_size dest with
           64 -> x86_XOR (OPERAND64 dest s) (OPERAND64 src s)
         | 32 -> x86_XOR (OPERAND32 dest s) (OPERAND32 src s)
         | 16 -> x86_XOR (OPERAND16 dest s) (OPERAND16 src s)
         | 8 -> x86_XOR (OPERAND8 dest s) (OPERAND8 src s)) s)) s
    | _ -> (\s'. F)
```

### x86_decode

```text
x86_decode s pc (len,inst) <=>
  ?l. bytes_loaded s pc l /\ LENGTH l = len /\ decode l = SOME (inst, [])
```

### abi_e4_integer_vector_instruction

```text
abi_e4_integer_vector_instruction inst <=>
    abi_caller_scalar_instruction inst \/ inst = NOP \/
    (?op. inst = NOP_N op) \/ inst = VZEROUPPER \/
    (?d x. inst = ADD d x /\ abi_integer_writable d /\ abi_integer_operand x) \/
    (?d x. inst = SUB d x /\ abi_integer_writable d /\ abi_integer_operand x) \/
    (?d x. inst = AND d x /\ abi_integer_writable d /\ abi_integer_operand x) \/
    (?d x. inst = SBB d x /\ abi_integer_writable d /\ abi_integer_operand x) \/
    (?d. inst = INC d /\ abi_integer_writable d) \/
    (?d v. inst = SHL d (Imm8 v) /\ abi_integer_writable d) \/
    (?d x n. inst = TZCNT d x /\ e4_gpr_width d n /\ e4_integer_source_width x n) \/
    (?d n. inst = BSWAP d /\ e4_gpr_width d n /\ (n = 32 \/ n = 64)) \/
    (?d x n. inst = MOVBE d x /\ e4_gpr_width d n /\
       e4_integer_source_width x n /\ ~e4_gpr_width x n) \/
    (?d x n. inst = MOVBE d x /\ e4_gpr_width x n /\
       e4_integer_source_width d n /\ ~e4_gpr_width d n) \/
    (?d x y n. inst = BZHI d x y /\ (n = 32 \/ n = 64) /\
       e4_gpr_width d n /\ e4_integer_source_width x n /\ e4_gpr_width y n) \/
    (?d x n. inst = VMOVDQU d x /\
       ((e4_vector_register d n /\ e4_vector_operand x n) \/
        (e4_vector_operand d n /\ e4_vector_register x n))) \/
    (?d x y n. inst = VPAND d x y /\ e4_vector_register d n /\
       e4_vector_register x n /\ e4_vector_operand y n) \/
    (?d x y n. inst = VPCMPEQB d x y /\ e4_vector_register d n /\
       e4_vector_register x n /\ e4_vector_operand y n) \/
    (?d x n. inst = VPMOVMSKB d x /\ e4_gpr_width d 32 /\ e4_vector_register x n)
```

### abi_e4_extended_step

```text
abi_e4_extended_step (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) (s',a') <=>
    ?len inst.
      x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) /\
      (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,, x86_execute inst) s s' /\
      (abi_e4_integer_vector_instruction inst ==> a' = a)
```

### abi_caller_scalar_instruction

```text
abi_caller_scalar_instruction inst <=>
    abi_cleanse_scalar_instruction inst \/
    (?r imm. inst = ADD (Register(Gpr r Full_64)) (Imm8 imm)) \/
    (?r q. inst = TEST (Register(Gpr r Lower_32)) (Register(Gpr q Lower_32))) \/
    (?r q. inst = MOVZX (Register(Gpr r Lower_32)) (Register(Gpr q Lower_8))) \/
    (?cc r. inst = SET cc (Register(Gpr r Lower_8))) \/
    (?r. inst = PUSH (Register(Gpr r Full_64))) \/
    (?r. inst = POP (Register(Gpr r Full_64))) \/
    (?disp. inst = CALL (Imm32 disp)) \/
    (?r. inst = CALL (Register(Gpr r Full_64))) \/
    (?addr. inst = CALL (Memop Quadword addr)) \/
    (?r. inst = JUMP Unconditional (Register(Gpr r Full_64))) \/
    (?addr. inst = JUMP Unconditional (Memop Quadword addr))
```

### abi_scalar_instruction

```text
abi_scalar_instruction inst <=>
    inst = ENDBR64 \/ inst = RET \/
    (?dst src. inst = MOV dst src /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\ abi_integer_operand src) \/
    (?dst src. inst = XOR dst src /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\ abi_integer_operand src) \/
    (?dst src. inst = OR dst src /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\ abi_integer_operand src) \/
    (?dst src. inst = CMP dst src /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\ abi_integer_operand src) \/
    (?dst. inst = DEC dst /\ abi_integer_writable dst) \/
    (?dst. inst = NEG dst /\ abi_integer_writable dst) \/
    (?r addr. inst = LEA (Register r) addr) \/
    (?cond r size src. inst = CMOV cond (Register(Gpr r size)) src /\
       (size = Full_64 \/ size = Lower_32 \/ size = Lower_16) /\ abi_cmov_operand src) \/
    (?cond disp. inst = JUMP cond (Imm8 disp)) \/
    (?cond disp. inst = JUMP cond (Imm32 disp)) \/
    (?dst count. inst = SHR dst count /\ abi_integer_writable dst /\
       ((?v. count = Imm8 v) \/ count = Register(Gpr (word 1) Lower_8)))
```

```text
e4eq3_site remaining off len inst <=>

  (remaining=3 /\ off=782 /\ len=2 /\ inst=XOR (%eax) (%eax)) \/
```

### e4eq3_step

```text
e4eq3_step pc rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) remaining source target <=>
  assurance_step_policy (e4eq3_read rp ordinary stackpointer) (\x.F)
   (e4eq3_fetch pc xp ordinary) source target /\
  (?off len inst. e4eq3_site remaining off len inst /\
   read RIP source=word(pc+off) /\ x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
  (if remaining=1 then assurance_actual_ret 1 source target
   else ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target))
```

### e4eq3_pre

```text
e4eq3_pre pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (m:int64->byte)
  rp xp ordinary (s:x86state) <=>
  pc+1023<=2 EXP 64 /\ val stackpointer+8<2 EXP 64 /\
  val stackpointer MOD 16=8 /\ assurance_canonical48(val ra) /\
  read RIP s=word(pc+776) /\ read RSP s=stackpointer /\
  read memory s=m /\ read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) s=ra /\
  read ZF s /\ bytes_loaded s (word pc) gq2_memcmp_mc /\
  assurance_span_ok (\x.rp x /\ ordinary x) (val stackpointer) 8 /\
  assurance_span_ok (\x.xp x /\ ordinary x) pc 1023
```

### e4eq3_events

```text
e4eq3_events pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) e = APPEND
  [EventJump(word(pc+788),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
   EventJump(word(pc+782),word(pc+782))] e
```

### e4eq3_post

```text
e4eq3_post pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (m:int64->byte) e (t:x86state) <=>
  read RIP t=ra /\ read RSP t=word_add stackpointer (word 8) /\
  read EAX t=word 0 /\ ival(read EAX t)= &0 /\ read memory t=m /\
  read events t=e4eq3_events pc stackpointer ra e
```

## Current glibc initial-equal guarded short entry-to-return package

Fresh ColdV18 and independent source/runtime/ELF acceptance check five exact frozen theorems and two expanded aliases. The unchanged original gq2_g1_pre is restricted here to initial logical equality, lengths2–31 and the actual OR-page guard; this is a partial case, never a substitute for the all-length G1 contract. All18 original-model transitions reach first actual C3 RET with mapped reads, no writes, signed EAXzero, unchanged memory, exact events and entry-relative base frame. Omitted14 and physical normal-vector lift remain open. All62 covered bytes are requalified reuse. [Independent accepted entry-to-return package](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/glibc-e4-successors-v1/runs/20261002T010142Z-1ddb4094/root-equal-entry-return-acceptance-v1.json)

### E4E18_PREFIX_TO_EQUAL_PRE

```text
!bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
   stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary s a u.
  gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
   stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a) /\
  2<=n /\ n<32 /\ e4sg_page_guard p q /\
  gsb_logical_equal (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n /\
  e4sg_prefix14_post (bias+0x152320) stackpointer p q n m (read events s) u
  ==> e4eq3_pre (bias+0x152320) stackpointer ra m rp xp ordinary u
```

```text
       (4+k) source target
```

### E4E18_EQUAL_POLICY


## Current glibc initial-unequal guarded short entry-to-return package

Every original-model successor reaches first actual C3RET21 under original gq2_g1_pre plus2<=n<32, actual guard and initial nonzero lowmask. Signed EAX is the initial first unsigned-byte difference; memory is unchanged, accesses are mapped-page bounded, and the base entry frame is preserved. No omitted14, physical normal or fullG1 theorem is added. New18 bytes plus prior62 give80 unique bytes. ### E4NEQ7_PREFIX_ENDPOINT_RESOURCES

```text
!bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
   stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary s a u.
  gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
   stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a) /\
  2<=n /\ n<32 /\ e4sg_page_guard p q /\
  ~(gsb_lowmask (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n=word 0) /\
  e4sg_prefix14_post (bias+0x152320) stackpointer p q n m (read events s) u
  ==> ~(read ZF u) /\
   gsb_first_mismatch (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n
    (e4neq7_index m p q) /\
   e4neq7_index m p q<n /\ e4neq7_index m p q<32 /\
   bias+0x152320+1023<=2 EXP 64 /\
   bytes_loaded u (word(bias+0x152320)) gq2_memcmp_mc /\
   read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) u=ra /\
   assurance_span_ok (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) (bias+0x152320) 1023 /\
   assurance_span_ok (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (val(word_add p (word(e4neq7_index m p q)))) 1 /\
   assurance_span_ok (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (val(word_add q (word(e4neq7_index m p q)))) 1 /\
   assurance_span_ok (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (val stackpointer) 8
```

### e4neq7_site

```text
e4neq7_site remaining off len inst <=>
  (remaining=7 /\ off=776 /\ len=6 /\
   inst=JUMP Condition_NZ (Imm32(word 4294966722))) \/
  (remaining=6 /\ off=208 /\ len=4 /\ inst=TZCNT (%eax) (%eax)) \/
  (remaining=5 /\ off=212 /\ len=4 /\
   inst=MOVZX (%ecx)
    (Memop Byte (Bsid (SOME(Gpr (word 6) Full_64))
                     (SOME(Gpr (word 0) Full_64)) (word 0) (word 0)))) \/
  (remaining=4 /\ off=216 /\ len=4 /\
   inst=MOVZX (%eax)
    (Memop Byte (Bsid (SOME(Gpr (word 7) Full_64))
                     (SOME(Gpr (word 0) Full_64)) (word 0) (word 0)))) \/
  (remaining=3 /\ off=220 /\ len=2 /\ inst=SUB (%eax) (%ecx)) \/
  (remaining=2 /\ off=222 /\ len=3 /\ inst=VZEROUPPER) \/
  (remaining=1 /\ off=225 /\ len=1 /\ inst=RET)
```

### e4neq7_step

```text
e4neq7_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
   remaining (source:x86state) target <=>
  assurance_step_policy (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n) (\x.F)
   (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
  (?off len inst. e4neq7_site remaining off len inst /\
   read RIP source=word((bias+0x152320)+off) /\
   x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
  (if remaining=1 then assurance_actual_ret 1 source target
   else ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target))
```

```text
e4neq7_events pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) j e = APPEND
  [EventJump(word(pc+226),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
   EventLoad(word_add p (word j),1);EventLoad(word_add q (word j),1);
   EventJump(word(pc+782),word(pc+208))] e
```

### e4neq7_post

```text
e4neq7_post pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
   (m:int64->byte) e (t:x86state) <=>
  read RIP t=ra /\ read RSP t=word_add stackpointer (word 8) /\
  read memory t=m /\
  gsb_first_mismatch (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n
   (e4neq7_index m p q) /\
  e4neq7_index m p q<n /\ e4neq7_index m p q<32 /\
  read EAX t=word_sub
   (word(val(e4sg_buffer_byte m p (e4neq7_index m p q))):int32)
   (word(val(e4sg_buffer_byte m q (e4neq7_index m p q)))) /\
  ival(read EAX t)=
   &(val(e4sg_buffer_byte m p (e4neq7_index m p q))) -
   &(val(e4sg_buffer_byte m q (e4neq7_index m p q))) /\
  ival(read EAX t)=
   gsb_result (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n /\
  read events t=e4neq7_events pc stackpointer ra p q (e4neq7_index m p q) e
```

## Current unified all-content guarded short package

ColdV20 and independent source/runtime/ELF acceptance establish four frozen theorems and four expanded definitions. Original gq2_g1_pre plus2<=n<32 and the actual OR-page guard permits all logical contents; initial equality or mismatch is not a premise. Every raw-model successor reaches the first actual C3 RET with the initial-byte result, mapped reads, no writes, stack restoration and base-state frame. The relational extra-page lemma assumes both public endpoints and equality of initial logical bytes; the universal main supplies the endpoints for actual executions. It does not assume equal results or grant timing/trace noninterference. All80 code bytes are reused; omitted14, physical normal-vector lift, refined non-vacuity and full G1 remain open. [Independent current acceptance](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/glibc-e4-successors-v1/runs/20261002T044658Z-fa88bbad/root-guardpass-general-acceptance-v1.json)

### E4GP_EQUAL_POST

```text
!pc stackpointer ra p q n m e s t.
  n<=32 /\ gsb_logical_equal (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n /\
  e4e18_post pc stackpointer ra p q m e s t
  ==> e4gp_post pc stackpointer ra p q n m e s t
```

### E4GP_UNEQUAL_POST

```text
!pc stackpointer ra p q n m e s t.
  ~(gsb_logical_equal (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n) /\
  e4u21_post pc stackpointer ra p q n m e s t
  ==> e4gp_post pc stackpointer ra p q n m e s t
```

### E4GP_GUARD_PASS_RETURN_CHECKED

```text
!bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
   stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary s a.
  gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
   stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a) /\
  2<=n /\ n<32 /\ e4sg_page_guard p q
  ==> assurance_checked_run (e4gp_steps m p q n)
   (e4gp_post (bias+0x152320) stackpointer ra p q n m (read events s) s)
   (e4gp_step bias rp xp ordinary stackpointer p q n) s
```

### E4GP_EXTRA_PAGE_BYTES_RESULT_INDEPENDENT

```text
!bias file_bytes initial_rw padding initial_rw2 padding2 cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
   stackpointer ra p q n m m2 rp wp xp ordinary s a s2 a2 t t2.
  gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
   stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a) /\
  gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw2 padding2 cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
   stackpointer ra p q n m2 rp wp xp ordinary (s2,a2) /\
  2<=n /\ n<32 /\ e4sg_page_guard p q /\
  (!i. i<n ==> e4sg_buffer_byte m2 p i=e4sg_buffer_byte m p i /\
               e4sg_buffer_byte m2 q i=e4sg_buffer_byte m q i) /\
  e4gp_post (bias+0x152320) stackpointer ra p q n m (read events s) s t /\
  e4gp_post (bias+0x152320) stackpointer ra p q n m2 (read events s2) s2 t2
  ==> gsb_result (e4sg_buffer_byte m2 p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m2 q) n =
       gsb_result (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n /\
      e4gp_steps m2 p q n=e4gp_steps m p q n /\
      ival(read EAX t2)=ival(read EAX t) /\ read EAX t2=read EAX t
```

### e4gp_steps

```text
e4gp_steps (m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) n =
  if gsb_logical_equal (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n
  then 18 else 21
```

### e4gp_step

```text
e4gp_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
   remaining (source:x86state) target <=>
  assurance_step_policy
   (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n) (\x.F)
   (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
  (if remaining=1 then assurance_actual_ret 1 source target
   else ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target))
```

### e4gp_events

```text
e4gp_events pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
   (m:int64->byte) e =
  if gsb_logical_equal (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n
  then e4eq3_events pc stackpointer ra (e4sg_events11 pc p q e)
  else e4neq7_events pc stackpointer ra p q (e4neq7_index m p q)
        (e4sg_events11 pc p q e)
```

### e4gp_post

```text
e4gp_post pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
   (m:int64->byte) e (s:x86state) t <=>
  read RIP t=ra /\ read RSP t=word_add stackpointer (word 8) /\
  read memory t=m /\
  read EAX t=(iword(gsb_result (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n):int32) /\
  ival(read EAX t)=gsb_result (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n /\
  read events t=e4gp_events pc stackpointer ra p q n m e /\
  e4sr_call_frame s t
```

## Accepted glibc normal-entry components

V21 and independent root acceptance reconstruct the following16 exact component theorems and nine transparent definitions. These are local qualifiers, frame/policy lemmas and RF-clear identity, rather than an entry-to-return normal theorem. Qualified sites cover62 of the inherited80 bytes; the18-byte unequal suffix is raw-only. All original G1 assumptions remain as displayed above. Each new literal retains its explicit antecedent; no normal18, normal21, full omitted14 return ABI or wholeG1 conclusion is supplied. [Independent acceptance](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/glibc-e4-successors-v1/runs/20261002T061737Z-ac89f1ec/root-normal-components-acceptance-v2.json)

### E4EN_PREFIX_SITES_QUALIFIED

```text
!off len inst. e4_gq2_prefix14_site_v1 off len inst ==> abi_e4_integer_vector_instruction inst
```

### E4EN_SUFFIX_SITES_QUALIFIED

```text
!k off len inst. e4eq3_site k off len inst ==> abi_e4_integer_vector_instruction inst
```

### E4EN_DECODE_QUALIFIED

```text
!s len inst. x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst) /\ abi_e4_integer_vector_instruction inst ==> abi_all_decodes_e4_qualified s
```

### E4EN_PREFIX_FRAME_RF_TF

```text
!s t:x86state. e4sr_prefix_frame s t ==> bit 16 (read rflags t)=bit 16 (read rflags s) /\ bit 8 (read rflags t)=bit 8 (read rflags s)
```

### E4EN_SUFFIX_FRAME_RF_TF

```text
!s t:x86state. e4sr_suffix_frame s t ==> bit 16 (read rflags t)=bit 16 (read rflags s) /\ bit 8 (read rflags t)=bit 8 (read rflags s)
```

### E4EN_SUFFIX_SITE_FRAME

```text
!k off len inst s t. e4eq3_site k off len inst /\ (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,, x86_execute inst) s t ==> e4sr_suffix_frame s t
```

### E4EN_PREFIX_POLICY_QUALIFIED

```text
!bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t. e4sg_step bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t ==> abi_all_decodes_e4_qualified s
```

### E4EN_SUFFIX_POLICY_QUALIFIED

```text
!pc rp xp ordinary sp k s t. e4eq3_step pc rp xp ordinary sp k s t ==> abi_all_decodes_e4_qualified s
```

### E4EN_PREFIX_POLICY_SHIFT

```text
!bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t. e4sg_step bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t ==> e4en_step bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n (4+k) s t
```

### E4EN_SUFFIX_POLICY

```text
!bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t. e4eq3_step (bias+0x152320) rp xp ordinary sp k s t ==> e4en_step bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t
```

### E4EN_POLICY_QUALIFIED

```text
!bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t. e4en_step bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t ==> abi_all_decodes_e4_qualified s
```

### E4EN_POLICY_NORMAL_SUCCESSORS

```text
!bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t. e4en_step bias rp xp ordinary sp p q n k s t /\ x86 s t /\ e4vn_normal_flags s ==> e4vn_normal_flags t
```

### E4EN_RF_CLEAR_IDENTITY

```text
!s:x86state. ~bit 16 (read rflags s) ==> e4vn_rf_clear s=s
```

### E4EN_QUALIFIED_OMITTED_RECORD

```text
!s t:x86state a b:abi_omitted_state. abi_all_decodes_e4_qualified s /\ abi_e4_extended_step (s,a) (t,b) ==> b=a
```

### E4EN_SUFFIX_FRAME_SEQ_IDEMPOT

```text
e4sr_suffix_frame ,, e4sr_suffix_frame = e4sr_suffix_frame
```

### E4EN_SUFFIX_FRAME_TRANS

```text
!s u t:x86state. e4sr_suffix_frame s u /\ e4sr_suffix_frame u t ==> e4sr_suffix_frame s t
```

### e4en_step

```text
e4en_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) p q n
 remaining (source:x86state) target <=>
 e4e18_step bias rp xp ordinary stackpointer p q n remaining source target /\
 (e4sg_step bias rp xp ordinary stackpointer p q n remaining source target \/
  e4eq3_step (bias+0x152320) rp xp ordinary stackpointer remaining source target)
```

### e4vn_normal_flags

```text
e4vn_normal_flags (s:x86state) <=>
    ~bit 16 (read rflags s) /\ ~bit 8 (read rflags s)
```

### e4vn_rf_clear

```text
e4vn_rf_clear (s:x86state) = write (rflags :> bitelement 16) F s
```

### e4vn_normal_instruction

```text
e4vn_normal_instruction inst <=>
    abi_caller_scalar_instruction inst \/
    inst = AND (%eax) (Imm32(word 4095)) \/
    inst = VMOVDQU (%_% ymm2) (Memop Word256 (Bsid (SOME(Gpr (word 6) Full_64)) NONE (word 0) (word 0))) \/
    inst = VPCMPEQB (%_% ymm2) (%_% ymm2) (Memop Word256 (Bsid (SOME(Gpr (word 7) Full_64)) NONE (word 0) (word 0))) \/
    inst = VPMOVMSKB (%eax) (%_% ymm2) \/
    inst = INC (%eax) \/
    inst = BZHI (%edx) (%eax) (%edx)
```

### e4vn_all_decodes_normal

```text
e4vn_all_decodes_normal (s:x86state) <=>
    !len inst. x86_decode s (read RIP s) (len,inst)
       ==> e4vn_normal_instruction inst
```

### e4vn_rf_retired_step

```text
e4vn_rf_retired_step (s:x86state) t <=>
    ?u. x86 s u /\ t = e4vn_rf_clear u
```

### e4vn_rf_retired_extended_step

```text
e4vn_rf_retired_extended_step (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) t <=>
    ?u b. abi_e4_extended_step (s,a) (u,b) /\ t = (e4vn_rf_clear u,b)
```

### e4vn_normal_step_policy

```text
e4vn_normal_step_policy (C:num->x86state->x86state->bool) k s t <=>
    C k s t /\ e4vn_normal_flags s /\ e4vn_normal_flags t
```

### e4vn_rf_checked_run

```text
(e4vn_rf_checked_run 0 (P:x86state->bool)
      (C:num->x86state->x86state->bool) s <=> P s) /\
  (e4vn_rf_checked_run (SUC n) P C s <=>
     (?t. e4vn_rf_retired_step s t) /\
     !t. e4vn_rf_retired_step s t ==> C (SUC n) s t /\
                                      e4vn_rf_checked_run n P C t)
```

### e4n01_site

```text
e4n01_site n remaining off len inst <=>
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=7 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=9) /\ off=0 /\ len=4 /\ inst=CMP (%rdx) (Imm8(word 32))) \/
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=6 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=8) /\ off=4 /\ len=6 /\ inst=JUMP Condition_B (Imm32(word 726))) \/
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=5 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=7) /\ off=736 /\ len=3 /\ inst=CMP (%edx) (Imm8(word 1))) \/
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=4 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=6) /\ off=739 /\ len=2 /\ inst=JUMP Condition_BE (Imm8(word 123))) \/
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=3 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=5) /\ off=864 /\ len=2 /\ inst=JUMP Condition_B (Imm8(word 20))) \/
  (n=0 /\ remaining=2 /\ off=886 /\ len=2 /\ inst=XOR (%eax) (%eax)) \/
  (n=0 /\ remaining=1 /\ off=888 /\ len=1 /\ inst=RET) \/
  (n=1 /\ remaining=4 /\ off=866 /\ len=3 /\ inst=MOVZX (%ecx) (Memop Byte (Bsid (SOME(Gpr(word 6) Full_64)) NONE (word 0) (word 0)))) \/
  (n=1 /\ remaining=3 /\ off=869 /\ len=3 /\ inst=MOVZX (%eax) (Memop Byte (Bsid (SOME(Gpr(word 7) Full_64)) NONE (word 0) (word 0)))) \/
  (n=1 /\ remaining=2 /\ off=872 /\ len=2 /\ inst=SUB (%eax) (%ecx)) \/
  (n=1 /\ remaining=1 /\ off=874 /\ len=1 /\ inst=RET)
```

### e4n01_step

```text
e4n01_step bias rp xp ordinary stackpointer p q n remaining source target <=>
  assurance_step_policy (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (\x.F) (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
  (?off len inst. e4n01_site n remaining off len inst /\
    read RIP source=word(bias+0x152320+off) /\
    x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
  (if remaining=1 then assurance_actual_ret 1 source target
   else ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target))
```

### e4n01_events

```text
e4n01_events pc stackpointer ra (p:int64) (q:int64) n e = APPEND
  (if n=0 then
   [EventJump(word(pc+889),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
    EventJump(word(pc+866),word(pc+886));
    EventJump(word(pc+741),word(pc+864));
    EventJump(word(pc+10),word(pc+736))]
   else
   [EventJump(word(pc+875),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
    EventLoad(p:int64,1);EventLoad(q:int64,1);
    EventJump(word(pc+866),word(pc+866));
    EventJump(word(pc+741),word(pc+864));
    EventJump(word(pc+10),word(pc+736))]) e
```

### e4n01_post

```text
e4n01_post pc stackpointer ra (p:int64) (q:int64) n
   (m:int64->byte) e (t:x86state) <=>
  read RIP t=ra /\ read RSP t=word_add stackpointer (word 8) /\
  read memory t=m /\ read events t=e4n01_events pc stackpointer ra p q n e /\
  (if n=0 then read EAX t=word 0 /\ ival(read EAX t)= &0
   else
```


### e4n01_site

```text
e4n01_site n remaining off len inst <=>
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=7 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=9) /\ off=0 /\ len=4 /\ inst=CMP (%rdx) (Imm8(word 32))) \/
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=6 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=8) /\ off=4 /\ len=6 /\ inst=JUMP Condition_B (Imm32(word 726))) \/
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=5 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=7) /\ off=736 /\ len=3 /\ inst=CMP (%edx) (Imm8(word 1))) \/
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=4 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=6) /\ off=739 /\ len=2 /\ inst=JUMP Condition_BE (Imm8(word 123))) \/
  ((n=0 /\ remaining=3 \/ n=1 /\ remaining=5) /\ off=864 /\ len=2 /\ inst=JUMP Condition_B (Imm8(word 20))) \/
  (n=0 /\ remaining=2 /\ off=886 /\ len=2 /\ inst=XOR (%eax) (%eax)) \/
  (n=0 /\ remaining=1 /\ off=888 /\ len=1 /\ inst=RET) \/
  (n=1 /\ remaining=4 /\ off=866 /\ len=3 /\ inst=MOVZX (%ecx) (Memop Byte (Bsid (SOME(Gpr(word 6) Full_64)) NONE (word 0) (word 0)))) \/
  (n=1 /\ remaining=3 /\ off=869 /\ len=3 /\ inst=MOVZX (%eax) (Memop Byte (Bsid (SOME(Gpr(word 7) Full_64)) NONE (word 0) (word 0)))) \/
  (n=1 /\ remaining=2 /\ off=872 /\ len=2 /\ inst=SUB (%eax) (%ecx)) \/
  (n=1 /\ remaining=1 /\ off=874 /\ len=1 /\ inst=RET)
```

### e4n01_step

```text
e4n01_step bias rp xp ordinary stackpointer p q n remaining source target <=>
  assurance_step_policy (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (\x.F) (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
  (?off len inst. e4n01_site n remaining off len inst /\
    read RIP source=word(bias+0x152320+off) /\
    x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
  (if remaining=1 then assurance_actual_ret 1 source target
   else ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target))
```

```text
   else

    EventLoad(p:int64,1);EventLoad(q:int64,1);
    EventJump(word(pc+866),word(pc+866));
    EventJump(word(pc+741),word(pc+864));
    EventJump(word(pc+10),word(pc+736))]) e
```


## Accepted guardfail8–15 finite raw prefix

V28 checks14 unique theorem objects and13 expanded equations across six audits. Only thirteen transitions to offset896 are covered; no MOVBE/input read/RET or entry-relative frame theorem is added. ### E4GF8_WINDOW_COVERAGE (theorem)

```text
!(m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) (n:num).
 8<=n /\ n<16
 ==> (e4gf8_window_equal m p q 0 /\ e4gf8_window_equal m p q (n-8)
      <=> gsb_logical_equal (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n)
```

### e4gf8_window_equal (definition)

```text
e4gf8_window_equal (m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) lo <=>
  !i. i<8 ==> e4sg_buffer_byte m p (lo+i)=e4sg_buffer_byte m q (lo+i)
```

### e4gf8_steps (definition)

```text
e4gf8_steps (m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) n =
  if ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q 0) then 20
  else if ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q (n-8)) then 24 else 22
```

### e4gf8_result (definition)

```text
e4gf8_result (m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) n (r:int) <=>
  (r= -- &1 \/ r= &0 \/ r= &1) /\
  (r= &0 <=> gsb_logical_equal (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n) /\
  (!j. gsb_first_mismatch (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n j
       ==> (r< &0 <=> val(e4sg_buffer_byte m p j)<val(e4sg_buffer_byte m q j)) /\
           (&0<r <=> val(e4sg_buffer_byte m q j)<val(e4sg_buffer_byte m p j)))
```

```text
e4gf8_site off len <=>
  (off=0 /\ len=4) \/ (off=4 /\ len=6) \/
  (off=736 /\ len=3) \/ (off=739 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=741 /\ len=2) \/ (off=743 /\ len=2) \/

  (off=808 /\ len=2) \/ (off=880 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=882 /\ len=3) \/ (off=885 /\ len=1) \/
  (off=896 /\ len=5) \/ (off=901 /\ len=5) \/
  (off=906 /\ len=3) \/ (off=909 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=911 /\ len=7) \/ (off=918 /\ len=7) \/
  (off=925 /\ len=3) \/ (off=928 /\ len=2) \/ (off=930 /\ len=1)
```

```text
e4gf8_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n

  assurance_step_policy (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
```

```text
e4gf8_prefix_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
```

```text
e4gf8_prefix_post pc (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
```

### e4gf8_events (definition)

```text
e4gf8_events pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  (m:int64->byte) e = APPEND
  (if ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q 0) then
    [EventJump(word(pc+886),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
     EventJump(word(pc+911),word(pc+880));EventLoad(q,8);EventLoad(p,8)]
   else if ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q (n-8)) then
    [EventJump(word(pc+886),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
     EventJump(word(pc+930),word(pc+880));
     EventLoad(word_add q (word(n-8)),8);EventLoad(word_add p (word(n-8)),8);
     EventJump(word(pc+911),word(pc+911));EventLoad(q,8);EventLoad(p,8)]
   else
    [EventJump(word(pc+931),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
     EventJump(word(pc+930),word(pc+930));
     EventLoad(word_add q (word(n-8)),8);EventLoad(word_add p (word(n-8)),8);
     EventJump(word(pc+911),word(pc+911));EventLoad(q,8);EventLoad(p,8)])
  (e4gf8_prefix_events pc e)
```

### e4gf8_post (definition)

```text
e4gf8_post pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  (m:int64->byte) e (t:x86state) <=>
  read RIP t=ra /\ read RSP t=word_add stackpointer (word 8) /\
  read memory t=m /\ e4gf8_result m p q n (ival(read EAX t)) /\
  read events t=e4gf8_events pc stackpointer ra p q n m e
```

```text

```

## Accepted guardfail8–15 complete raw-model return

V29 freshly reconstructs the unchanged original RAW-V3 seven targets and12 contract equations. The complete audit has41 unique closed theorem objects and15 expanded equations. For8<=n<16 and the actual OR-page guard failing,20/24/22 all-successor transitions reach the first actual RET for head mismatch, tail mismatch or equality. Initial-byte comparison result, mapped and strict access, unchanged memory and the entry-relative base-state frame are checked. This scope does not establish normal/RF/omitted14 refinement or same-domain non-vacuity witnesses; all-length G1 and human architectural review remain open. ### E4GF8_WINDOW_COVERAGE (theorem)

```text
!(m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) (n:num).
 8<=n /\ n<16
 ==> (e4gf8_window_equal m p q 0 /\ e4gf8_window_equal m p q (n-8)
      <=> gsb_logical_equal (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n)
```

### e4gf8_window_equal (definition)

```text
e4gf8_window_equal (m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) lo <=>
  !i. i<8 ==> e4sg_buffer_byte m p (lo+i)=e4sg_buffer_byte m q (lo+i)
```

### e4gf8_steps (definition)

```text
e4gf8_steps (m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) n =
  if ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q 0) then 20
  else if ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q (n-8)) then 24 else 22
```

### e4gf8_result (definition)

```text
e4gf8_result (m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) n (r:int) <=>
  (r= -- &1 \/ r= &0 \/ r= &1) /\
  (r= &0 <=> gsb_logical_equal (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n) /\
  (!j. gsb_first_mismatch (e4sg_buffer_byte m p) (e4sg_buffer_byte m q) n j
       ==> (r< &0 <=> val(e4sg_buffer_byte m p j)<val(e4sg_buffer_byte m q j)) /\
           (&0<r <=> val(e4sg_buffer_byte m q j)<val(e4sg_buffer_byte m p j)))
```

### e4gf8_site (definition)

```text
e4gf8_site off len <=>
  (off=0 /\ len=4) \/ (off=4 /\ len=6) \/
  (off=736 /\ len=3) \/ (off=739 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=741 /\ len=2) \/ (off=743 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=745 /\ len=5) \/ (off=750 /\ len=5) \/
  (off=755 /\ len=2) \/ (off=800 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=803 /\ len=2) \/ (off=805 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=808 /\ len=2) \/ (off=880 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=882 /\ len=3) \/ (off=885 /\ len=1) \/
  (off=896 /\ len=5) \/ (off=901 /\ len=5) \/
  (off=906 /\ len=3) \/ (off=909 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=911 /\ len=7) \/ (off=918 /\ len=7) \/
  (off=925 /\ len=3) \/ (off=928 /\ len=2) \/ (off=930 /\ len=1)
```

### e4gf8_step (definition)

```text
e4gf8_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  remaining (source:x86state) target <=>
  assurance_step_policy (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (\x.F) (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
  (?off len inst. e4gf8_site off len /\
   read RIP source=word(bias+0x152320+off) /\
   x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
  (if remaining=1 then assurance_actual_ret 1 source target
   else ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target))
```

### e4gf8_prefix_step (definition)

```text
e4gf8_prefix_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  (remaining:num) (source:x86state) target <=>
  assurance_step_policy (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (\x.F) (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
  (?off len inst. e4gf8_site off len /\
   read RIP source=word(bias+0x152320+off) /\
   x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
  ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target)
```

### e4gf8_strict_step (definition)

```text
e4gf8_strict_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  remaining (source:x86state) target <=>
  e4gf8_step bias rp xp ordinary stackpointer p q n remaining source target /\
  assurance_step_policy (gq2_strict_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (\x.F) (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target
```

```text
e4gf8_prefix_events pc e = APPEND

   EventJump(word(pc+805),word(pc+805));
   EventJump(word(pc+757),word(pc+800));
   EventJump(word(pc+741),word(pc+741));
   EventJump(word(pc+10),word(pc+736))] e
```

### e4gf8_prefix_post (definition)

```text
e4gf8_prefix_post pc (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  (m:int64->byte) e (t:x86state) <=>
  read RIP t=word(pc+896) /\ read memory t=m /\
  read events t=e4gf8_prefix_events pc e /\
  read RDI t=p /\ read RSI t=q /\ read RDX t=word n /\
  read RSP t=stackpointer /\ read CF t=F /\ (read ZF t <=> n=8)
```

### e4gf8_events (definition)

```text
e4gf8_events pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  (m:int64->byte) e = APPEND
  (if ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q 0) then
    [EventJump(word(pc+886),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
     EventJump(word(pc+911),word(pc+880));EventLoad(q,8);EventLoad(p,8)]
   else if ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q (n-8)) then
    [EventJump(word(pc+886),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
     EventJump(word(pc+930),word(pc+880));
     EventLoad(word_add q (word(n-8)),8);EventLoad(word_add p (word(n-8)),8);
     EventJump(word(pc+911),word(pc+911));EventLoad(q,8);EventLoad(p,8)]
   else
    [EventJump(word(pc+931),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
     EventJump(word(pc+930),word(pc+930));
     EventLoad(word_add q (word(n-8)),8);EventLoad(word_add p (word(n-8)),8);
     EventJump(word(pc+911),word(pc+911));EventLoad(q,8);EventLoad(p,8)])
  (e4gf8_prefix_events pc e)
```

### E4GF8_LOGICAL_SUBSPAN_PAGECOVER (theorem)

```text
!(span_p:int64) (span_n:num) (span_off:num) (span_width:num) (span_i:num).
 0<span_n /\ span_off+span_width<=span_n /\ span_i<span_width
 ==> gq2_pagecover span_p span_n (val span_p+span_off+span_i)
```

### E4GF8_LOGICAL_SUBSPAN_READABLE (theorem)

```text
!(span_p:int64) (span_n:num) (span_off:num) (span_width:num)
 (span_rp:num->bool) (span_ordinary:num->bool).
 0<span_n /\ span_off+span_width<=span_n /\ val span_p+span_n<=2 EXP 64 /\
 (!x. gq2_pagecover span_p span_n x ==> assurance_canonical48 x /\ span_rp x /\ span_ordinary x)
 ==> assurance_span_ok (\x. span_rp x /\ span_ordinary x /\ gq2_pagecover span_p span_n x)
     (val span_p+span_off) span_width
```

### E4GF8_PREFIX_COMMON_RESOURCES (theorem)

```text
!bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
 (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) (n:num) (m:int64->byte)
 (rp:num->bool) (wp:num->bool) (xp:num->bool) (ordinary:num->bool)
 (s:x86state) (a:abi_omitted_state).
 gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
  stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a) /\
 8<=n /\ n<16 /\ ~(e4sg_page_guard p q)
 ==> bias+0x152320+1023<=2 EXP 64 /\
     read RDI s=p /\ read RSI s=q /\ read RDX s=word n /\
     read RSP s=stackpointer /\ read memory s=m /\
     read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) s=ra /\
     bytes_loaded s (word(bias+0x152320)) gq2_memcmp_mc /\
     assurance_span_ok (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) (bias+0x152320) 1023
```

### E4GF8_FRAME_FUNCTION (theorem)

```text
e4gf8_frame =
  (MAYCHANGE [RIP;RSP;RAX;RCX] ,, MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,, MAYCHANGE [events])
```

### E4GF8_FRAME_REFL (theorem)

```text
!s:x86state. e4gf8_frame s s
```

### E4GF8_MOVBE64_READ_WEIGHTED (theorem)

```text
!(m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (lo:num).
 val(word_bytereverse(read(bytes64(word_add p (word lo))) m):int64) =
 nsum {i | i<8} (\i. 2 EXP (8*(7-i))*val(e4sg_buffer_byte m p (lo+i)))
```

### E4GF8_MOVBE64_WINDOW_EQUAL (theorem)

```text
!(m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) (lo:num).
 (word_bytereverse(read(bytes64(word_add p (word lo))) m):int64) =
 word_bytereverse(read(bytes64(word_add q (word lo))) m)
 <=> e4gf8_window_equal m p q lo
```

### E4GF8_MOVBE64_FIRST_DIFF_ORDER (theorem)

```text
!(m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) (lo:num) (j:num).
 j<8 /\ ~(e4sg_buffer_byte m p (lo+j)=e4sg_buffer_byte m q (lo+j)) /\
 (!i. i<j ==> e4sg_buffer_byte m p (lo+i)=e4sg_buffer_byte m q (lo+i))
 ==> (val(word_bytereverse(read(bytes64(word_add p (word lo))) m):int64) <
      val(word_bytereverse(read(bytes64(word_add q (word lo))) m):int64)
      <=> val(e4sg_buffer_byte m p (lo+j))<val(e4sg_buffer_byte m q (lo+j)))
```

### E4GF8_HEAD_RESULT (theorem)

```text
!(m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) (n:num).
 8<=n /\ n<16 /\ ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q 0)
 ==> e4gf8_result m p q n
     (if val(word_bytereverse(read(bytes64 p) m):int64) <
         val(word_bytereverse(read(bytes64 q) m):int64)
      then -- &1 else &1)
```

### E4GF8_TAIL_RESULT (theorem)

```text
!(m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) (n:num).
 8<=n /\ n<16 /\ e4gf8_window_equal m p q 0 /\
 ~(e4gf8_window_equal m p q (n-8))
 ==> e4gf8_result m p q n
     (if val(word_bytereverse(read(bytes64(word_add p (word(n-8)))) m):int64) <
         val(word_bytereverse(read(bytes64(word_add q (word(n-8)))) m):int64)
      then -- &1 else &1)
```

### E4GF8_EQUAL_RESULT (theorem)

```text
!(m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) (n:num).
 8<=n /\ n<16 /\ e4gf8_window_equal m p q 0 /\ e4gf8_window_equal m p q (n-8)
 ==> e4gf8_result m p q n (&0)
```

### E4GF8_SBB_OR_RESULT (theorem)

```text
!borrow:bool.
 ival(word_or(word_sub (word 0:int32) (word(bitval borrow))) (word 1)) =
 (if borrow then -- &1 else &1)
```

```text

 (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) (n:num) (m:int64->byte)
 (rp:num->bool) (wp:num->bool) (xp:num->bool) (ordinary:num->bool)
 (s:x86state) (a:abi_omitted_state).
 gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
  stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s,a) /\
 8<=n /\ n<16 /\ ~(e4sg_page_guard p q)
 ==>
     assurance_span_ok (gq2_strict_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n) (val stackpointer) 8 /\
     assurance_span_ok (gq2_strict_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n) (val p) 8 /\
```

## Accepted zero-length normal/RF/GMQ refinement

V30 reconstructs these eight original frozen statements with no new definitions or ISA semantics. Under original G1 plus n=0 and RF=TF=0, all seven successors through actual RET888 satisfy the qualified normal and RF-clearing relations. No buffer access or added buffer permission is required. The GMQ result retains the documented-effect14/control preservation predicate, not unconditional equality of the extended record. Five previously raw-covered bytes qualify: raw145 is reused; normal coverage becomes85. This does not establish the n1/guardfail normal branches, full G1 or physical full-processor correspondence. Human review remains pending. [Independent acceptance](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/glibc-e4-successors-v1/runs/20261003T055813Z-8e339746/root-n0-normal-return-acceptance-v2.json)

### E4N0N_ZERO_SITES_QUALIFIED

```text
!k off len (inst:instruction). e4n01_site 0 k off len inst ==> gmq_instruction inst
```

### E4N0N_ZERO_SITE_FRAME

```text
!k off len (inst:instruction) (s:x86state) (t:x86state).
 e4n01_site 0 k off len inst /\
 (RIP := word_add (read RIP s) (word len) ,, x86_execute inst) s t
 ==> e4n01_frame s t
```

### E4N0N_FRAME_RF_TF

```text
!s t:x86state. e4n01_frame s t
 ==> bit 16 (read rflags t)=bit 16 (read rflags s) /\
     bit 8 (read rflags t)=bit 8 (read rflags s)
```

```text
 ==> gmq_all_decodes_qualified s
```

## Accepted guardfail8–15 normal/RF/GMQ subcase package

V31 freshly reconstructed and independently accepted all eight original literals below, together with six local lemmas, two generic helpers and the original witness package. Their construction audits at feeds83/86,90/93,97/100 and104/107 remain preserved. They retain original gq2_g1_pre plus8<=n<16, failed page guard and RF=TF=0. Code loading is derived and threaded through every original successor, rather than assumed as an additional public premise. Functional result, mapped/strict policy, finite actual return and original base-state frame are retained. The public pair removes only derived code conjuncts with checked weakening lemmas. The RF and GMQ pairs retain the separate raw permission/frame guarantee and documented omitted14/control effects. Fresh V31 acceptance qualifies51 previously raw-covered bytes; coverage at V31 was145 raw/136 normal. Original preconditions and theorem meanings are unchanged. Human specification/architecture review remains pending. ## Accepted failed-guard4–7 raw/strict/normal return package

V32 reconstructs all90 live objects under the unchanged original G1 contract. Its76 theorem rows and14 definition/precondition rows include one exact inherited result-independence theorem reuse. The original raw domain adds4<=n<8 and failure of the original OR-page guard; the normal refinement adds existing RF=TF=0. Every allowed execution returns after26 or28 transitions; raw and normal/GMQ mapped and strict accesses and frame properties remain separate conjuncts. No new ISA or classifier definitions are introduced. Human review and full G1 remain open. [Independent acceptance](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/glibc-e4-successors-v1/runs/20261004T035649Z-9a047c40/root-guardfail4to7-normal-return-acceptance-v2.json) [Exact live associations and sources](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/inventory/coverage-e4-guardfail4to7-normal-return-v1.json)
