### e4gf4_window_equal

```text
e4gf4_window_equal (m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) lo <=>
  !i. i<4 ==> e4sg_buffer_byte m p (lo+i)=e4sg_buffer_byte m q (lo+i)
```

### e4gf4_steps

```text
e4gf4_steps (m:int64->byte) (p:int64) (q:int64) n =
  if e4gf4_window_equal m p q 0 /\ e4gf4_window_equal m p q (n-4)
  then 26 else 28
```

### e4gf4_site

```text
e4gf4_site off len <=>
  (off=0 /\ len=4) \/
  (off=4 /\ len=6) \/
  (off=736 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=739 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=741 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=743 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=745 /\ len=5) \/
  (off=750 /\ len=5) \/
  (off=755 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=800 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=803 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=805 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=808 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=810 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=813 /\ len=6) \/
  (off=819 /\ len=4) \/
  (off=823 /\ len=4) \/
  (off=827 /\ len=4) \/
  (off=831 /\ len=4) \/
  (off=835 /\ len=6) \/
  (off=841 /\ len=6) \/
  (off=847 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=850 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=853 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=856 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=858 /\ len=1) \/
  (off=880 /\ len=2) \/
  (off=882 /\ len=3) \/
  (off=885 /\ len=1)
```

### e4gf4_step

```text
e4gf4_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  remaining (source:x86state) target <=>
  assurance_step_policy (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (\x.F) (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
  (?off len inst. e4gf4_site off len /\
   read RIP source=word(bias+0x152320+off) /\
   x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
  (if remaining=1 then assurance_actual_ret 1 source target
   else ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target))
```

### e4gf4_prefix_step

```text
e4gf4_prefix_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  (remaining:num) (source:x86state) target <=>
  assurance_step_policy (gq2_mapped_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (\x.F) (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target /\
  (?off len inst. e4gf4_site off len /\
   read RIP source=word(bias+0x152320+off) /\
   x86_decode source (read RIP source) (len,inst)) /\
  ~(?len. assurance_actual_ret len source target)
```

### e4gf4_strict_step

```text
e4gf4_strict_step bias rp xp ordinary (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  remaining (source:x86state) target <=>
  e4gf4_step bias rp xp ordinary stackpointer p q n remaining source target /\
  assurance_step_policy (gq2_strict_read rp ordinary stackpointer p q n)
    (\x.F) (gq2_allowed_fetch bias xp ordinary) source target
```

### e4gf4_prefix_events

```text
e4gf4_prefix_events pc e = APPEND
  [EventJump(word(pc+819),word(pc+819));
   EventJump(word(pc+810),word(pc+810));
   EventJump(word(pc+805),word(pc+805));
   EventJump(word(pc+757),word(pc+800));
   EventJump(word(pc+741),word(pc+741));
   EventJump(word(pc+10),word(pc+736))] e
```

### e4gf4_prefix_post

```text
e4gf4_prefix_post pc (stackpointer:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  (m:int64->byte) e (t:x86state) <=>
  read RIP t=word(pc+819) /\ read memory t=m /\
  read events t=e4gf4_prefix_events pc e /\
  read RDI t=p /\ read RSI t=q /\ read RDX t=word n /\
  read RSP t=stackpointer /\ read CF t=F /\ (read ZF t <=> n=4)
```

### e4gf4_events

```text
e4gf4_events pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  (m:int64->byte) e = APPEND
  (if e4gf4_window_equal m p q 0 /\ e4gf4_window_equal m p q (n-4) then
    [EventJump(word(pc+859),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
     EventJump(word(pc+858),word(pc+858));
     EventLoad(word_add q (word(n-4)),4);EventLoad(word_add p (word(n-4)),4);
     EventLoad(q,4);EventLoad(p,4)]
   else
    [EventJump(word(pc+886),ra);EventLoad(stackpointer,8);
     EventJump(word(pc+858),word(pc+880));
     EventLoad(word_add q (word(n-4)),4);EventLoad(word_add p (word(n-4)),4);
     EventLoad(q,4);EventLoad(p,4)])
  (e4gf4_prefix_events pc e)
```

### e4gf4_post

```text
e4gf4_post pc (stackpointer:int64) (ra:int64) (p:int64) (q:int64) n
  (m:int64->byte) e (t:x86state) <=>
  read RIP t=ra /\ read RSP t=word_add stackpointer (word 8) /\
  read memory t=m /\ e4gf8_result m p q n (ival(read EAX t)) /\
  read events t=e4gf4_events pc stackpointer ra p q n m e
```

### e4gf4_frame

```text
e4gf4_frame (s:x86state) (t:x86state) <=>
  (MAYCHANGE [RIP;RSP;RAX;RCX;RDI;RSI] ,, MAYCHANGE SOME_FLAGS ,, MAYCHANGE [events]) s t
```

### gq2_g1_pre

```text
gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
    stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) <=>
    gq2_profile cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule /\
    bias MOD 4096=0 /\ bias+0x1e2000<=2 EXP 64 /\
    gq2_file_code file_bytes /\ gq2_full_image bias file_bytes initial_rw padding m /\
    n<2 EXP 64 /\ val(p:int64)+n<=2 EXP 64 /\ val(q:int64)+n<=2 EXP 64 /\
    val(stackpointer:int64) MOD 16=8 /\ val stackpointer+8<2 EXP 64 /\
    assurance_canonical48(val(ra:int64)) /\ ~(ra=word(bias+0x152320)) /\
    read RIP s=word(bias+0x152320) /\ read RSP s=stackpointer /\
    C_ARGUMENTS [p;q;word n] s /\ read memory s=m /\
    read (memory :> bytes64 stackpointer) s=ra /\
    bytes_loaded s (word(bias+0x152320)) gq2_memcmp_mc /\
    read DF s=F /\ ~bit 18(read rflags s) /\
    assurance_canonical48(val(read ABI_FS_BASE a)) /\
    assurance_span_ok (\x. rp x /\ ordinary x) (val stackpointer) 8 /\
    assurance_span_ok (\x. xp x /\ ordinary x) (bias+0x152320) 1023 /\
    (!x. ~(assurance_region stackpointer 8 x /\
            assurance_region (word(bias+0x152320)) 1023 x)) /\
    (!x. gq2_pagecover p n x \/ gq2_pagecover q n x
         ==> assurance_canonical48 x /\ rp x /\ ordinary x) /\
    (!x. gq2_image_domain bias x ==>
       assurance_canonical48 x /\ rp x /\ ordinary x /\
       (xp x <=> gq2_image_execute bias x) /\
       (wp x <=> gq2_image_write bias x))
```

### gq2_g1_pre__expanded_v1

```text
gq2_g1_pre bias file_bytes initial_rw padding cpuid osxsave xcr0 cet granule
 stackpointer ra p q n m rp wp xp ordinary (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) <=>
 (osxsave /\ xcr0=7 /\ ~cet /\ granule=4096 /\
 cpuid 0 0=(13,1970169159,1818588270,1231384169) /\
 cpuid 1 0=(132800,67584,2128097807,260803349) /\
 cpuid 7 0=(0,296,0,0) /\ cpuid 7 1=(0,296,0,0) /\
 cpuid 13 0=(7,832,832,0) /\ cpuid 13 1=(0,0,0,0) /\
 cpuid 2147483648 0=(2147483656,0,0,0) /\
```

## E5 normal-entry refinement

The concrete RF/TF finding is **CLOSED_WITH_NORMAL_ENTRY_BOUND** under separately versioned E5. All qualified scalar successors preserve RF=0 and TF=0; the RF-clearing relation is identical on this domain. Both OpenSSL public interfaces and the actual composed caller were freshly requalified through their unchanged posts. **RF=1/debug/resume entries and fault-priority correspondence are EXCLUDED_SCOPE.** E5 establishes this specific model correspondence; unqualified full-processor correspondence is NOT_ESTABLISHED. The physical interpretation also requires the explicitly enumerated legal state, disabled debug facilities and absent delivered events; these are architectural assumptions, not hidden theorem premises. The old word-zero caller witness remains logical, not a physical flags image. The independent agent inspected the sealed Intel references and E5 source/gate. E2/E3 statements, models and accepted receipts remain intact; the old HIGH_OPEN review is preserved history. Both actual GOT targets and their linkage-path preservation are checked. Human review remains **PENDING**. [Accepted E5 refinement](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/development/approved-plan/normal-entry-e5-v1/runs/cold-v1/root-e5-acceptance-v1.json) [Changed claims and interpretation](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/spec/e5/CLAIM-CHANGES-v1.md) [Independent primary-reference review](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/results/external-consumer-v1/e5-architecture-reference-review-v1/review-v1.md) [Canonical-GOT resolution](/Users/scasella/Downloads/binary-proofs/results/development/e3-canonical-got-status-v1.json)

The old public preconditions and postconditions are unchanged; E5 adds RF=0/TF=0 and uses its declared RF-clearing transition relation. All24 new theorem targets are strict closed aconv comparisons; seven equations use the frozen definition closure/type-alpha policy. The complete old325callee/125caller chain is replayed. No new instruction bytes or caller trace claim are added.

### E5_RF_CLEAR_IDENTITY

```text
!s:x86state. ~bit 16 (read rflags s) ==> e5_rf_clear s=s
```

### E5_SCALAR_OPERATION_OUTSIDE_FLAGS

```text
!inst s t:x86state k.
 k<64 /\ ~MEM k [0;2;4;6;7;11] /\
 abi_caller_scalar_instruction inst /\ x86_execute inst s t
 ==> bit k (read rflags t)=bit k (read rflags s)
```

### E5_QUALIFIED_STEP_OUTSIDE_FLAGS

```text
!s t:x86state k.
 k<64 /\ ~MEM k [0;2;4;6;7;11] /\
 abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar s /\ x86 s t
 ==> bit k (read rflags t)=bit k (read rflags s)
```

### E5_QUALIFIED_STEP_NORMAL

```text
!s t:x86state. e5_normal_flags s /\
 abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar s /\ x86 s t ==> e5_normal_flags t
```

### E5_RF_RETIRED_STEP_NORMAL_EQ

```text
!s t:x86state. e5_normal_flags s /\ abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar s
 ==> (e5_rf_retired_step s t <=> x86 s t)
```

### E5_RF_RETIRED_EXTENDED_NORMAL_EQ

```text
!s t:x86state a b:abi_omitted_state.
 e5_normal_flags s /\ abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar s
 ==> (e5_rf_retired_extended_step (s,a) (t,b) <=>
      abi_caller_scalar_extended_step (s,a) (t,b))
```

### E5_CHECKED_RUN_NORMAL

```text
!n P C s:x86state.
 (!k source target. C k source target ==> abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar source) /\
 assurance_checked_run n P C s /\ e5_normal_flags s
 ==> assurance_checked_run n (\t. P t /\ e5_normal_flags t)
       (e5_normal_step_policy C) s
```

### E5_RF_CHECKED_RUN_NORMAL

```text
!n P C s:x86state.
 (!k source target. C k source target ==> abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar source) /\
 assurance_checked_run n P C s /\ e5_normal_flags s
 ==> e5_rf_checked_run n (\t. P t /\ e5_normal_flags t)
       (e5_normal_step_policy C) s
```

### E5_NORMAL_CHECKED_RUN_EXTENDED_LIFT

```text
!n P C s:x86state a:abi_omitted_state.
 (!k source target. C k source target ==> abi_all_decodes_caller_scalar source) /\
 assurance_checked_run n P C s /\ e5_normal_flags s
 ==> eventually_n e5_rf_retired_extended_step n
       (\t. P(FST t) /\ e5_normal_flags(FST t) /\ SND t=a /\
            abi_required_controls_preserved a (SND t)) (s,a)
```

### E5_CRYPTO_NORMAL_TRACE_ORIGINAL

```text
!file_bytes initial_rw1 padding1 initial_rw2 padding2 stackpointer ra p q n
       m1 m2 rp xp ordinary s1 a1 s2 a2 trace1 trace2 t1 t2.
    bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw1 padding1 stackpointer ra
      p q n m1 rp xp ordinary (s1,a1) /\
    bp_e2_crypto_public_pre file_bytes initial_rw2 padding2 stackpointer ra
      p q n m2 rp xp ordinary (s2,a2) /\
    e5_normal_flags s1 /\ e5_normal_flags s2 /\
    crypto_first_ret_execution trace1 s1 t1 /\
    crypto_first_ret_execution trace2 s2 t2
    ==> trace1=crypto_public_call 0x10241840 p q n stackpointer ra /\
        trace2=crypto_public_call 0x10241840 p q n stackpointer ra /\
        trace1=trace2 /\
        read RIP t1=ra /\ read RSP t1=word_add stackpointer (word 8) /\
        read RIP t2=ra /\ read RSP t2=word_add stackpointer (word 8)
```

### E5_RF_CLEAR_NONIDENTITY

```text
!s:x86state. bit 16 (read rflags s) ==> ~(e5_rf_clear s=s)
```

### e5_normal_flags

```text
e5_normal_flags (s:x86state) <=>
    ~bit 16 (read rflags s) /\ ~bit 8 (read rflags s)
```

### e5_rf_clear

```text
e5_rf_clear (s:x86state) = write (rflags :> bitelement 16) F s
```

### e5_rf_retired_step

```text
e5_rf_retired_step (s:x86state) t <=>
    ?u. x86 s u /\ t=e5_rf_clear u
```

### e5_rf_retired_extended_step

```text
e5_rf_retired_extended_step (s:x86state,a:abi_omitted_state) t <=>
    ?u b. abi_caller_scalar_extended_step (s,a) (u,b) /\
          t=(e5_rf_clear u,b)
```

### e5_normal_step_policy

```text
e5_normal_step_policy (C:num->x86state->x86state->bool) k s t <=>
    C k s t /\ e5_normal_flags s /\ e5_normal_flags t
```

### e5_normal_environment

```text
e5_normal_environment long_mode cpl (dr7:int64) cet_enabled
      external_event_delivered debug_agent_modified <=>
    long_mode /\ cpl=3 /\
    (!i. i<8 ==> ~bit i dr7) /\ ~cet_enabled /\
    ~external_event_delivered /\ ~debug_agent_modified
```

### e5_rf_checked_run

```text
(e5_rf_checked_run 0 (P:x86state->bool)
      (C:num->x86state->x86state->bool) s <=> P s) /\
  (e5_rf_checked_run (SUC n) P C s <=>
     (?t. e5_rf_retired_step s t) /\
     !t. e5_rf_retired_step s t ==> C (SUC n) s t /\
                                      e5_rf_checked_run n P C t)
```
